# M3 — 셸 + 디자인 체계 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking. 작업마다 아래 "검토 절차"(구현 → 명세 검토 → 품질 검토 → **디자인 비평(opus)** → 수정 반복)를 따른다.

**Goal:** 승인된 디자인 명세(레이아웃 B, 시스템 글꼴, 틸, 합니다체)대로 Mac 네이티브 창·크기 조절 패널·명령 팔레트·설정 창·시트·드롭 오버레이·토스트를 갖춘 셸과 `@layer` CSS 디자인 체계를 만들고, 지금의 라이브러리 목록과 리더를 새 크롬 안으로 옮긴다.

**Architecture:** 렌더러는 react-aria-components(RAC) 위에 목업 CSS(`docs/design/mockups/assets/mockup.css`)의 구조·값을 옮긴 컴포넌트 계층(`ui/*` + `styles/components`)과 화면 계층(`shell/*`, `library/*`, `viewers/*`, `palette/*`, `settings/*`, `sheets/*` + `styles/screens`)으로 나눈다. 데이터는 TanStack Query(IPC 조회 캐시, M2 푸시 이벤트로 무효화), UI 상태는 zustand. 모든 조작은 M2 명령 레지스트리를 거치고(메뉴·팔레트·툴바·단축키·컨텍스트 메뉴가 같은 핸들러), 렌더러가 실행 가능한 명령 목록을 main에 알려 메뉴의 활성 상태를 맞춘다. main은 창 크롬(숨긴 제목 막대·신호등·비브런시·`titleBarOverlay`·창 상태 기억), 설정 창, 모달 시트 창을 맡는다.

**Tech Stack:** Electron 44.9.1, React 19.3.1, TypeScript 7.0.2, electron-vite 6.0.0, Vite 8.3.4, react-aria-components 1.22.1, lucide-react 1.60.0, react-resizable-panels 4.14.8, @tanstack/react-query 5.104.5, zustand 5.0.15, Playwright 1.64.1(+ `@axe-core/playwright` 4.14.0, 개발 전용), vitest 5.0.5, pnpm 10.15.1.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-quality-phase-design.md`(M3 절, "품질 프로세스", "재사용", "위험과 대응", "Verification") + `docs/design/design-spec.md`(모든 픽셀의 기준, 0절 소유자 결정) + `docs/design/review-rubric.md` + ADR-0004/0005. 기준 구현 CSS는 `docs/design/mockups/assets/mockup.css`.

## Global Constraints

모든 작업의 요구 사항에 아래가 암묵적으로 포함된다. 따옴표 안은 명세 원문이다.

- **버전**: 저장소에 고정된 그대로 쓴다 — `"electron": "44.9.1"`, `"react": "19.3.1"`, `"react-dom": "19.3.1"`, `"typescript": "7.0.2"`, `"packageManager": "pnpm@10.15.1"`. 새 패키지도 "모든 패키지는 `package.json`에 정확한 버전으로 고정하고(`^`·`~` 금지)"(ADR-0004).
- **라이선스**: "AGPL인 Zotero 코드(reader, note-editor)는 **UX 참고만** 하고 가져오지 않는다. 런타임 의존성은 MIT/ISC/Apache/OFL만 쓴다." 렌더러에 번들되는 패키지(RAC·lucide·패널·Query·zustand)는 react처럼 `apps/desktop`의 devDependencies에 두지만 배포물에 들어가므로 이 규칙을 그대로 적용한다. `@axe-core/playwright`(MPL-2.0)는 테스트 전용이며 배포물에 들어가지 않는다.
- **IPC**: "`ipc.ts`의 `handle()` 래퍼(발신자 검사 + zod + Result)는 모든 새 채널에 그대로 쓴다." M2 이후 이 래퍼는 `apps/desktop/src/main/ipc/register.ts`의 `createHandle()`이다. "preload는 이름공간 API와 허용 목록 기반 `on`만 노출한다." "**범용 `invoke`/`send` 노출 금지.**" 렌더러에는 fs/shell/DB가 없고, 로그에는 오류 코드만 쓰고 경로·원문을 쓰지 않는다.
- **문체**: "문체: 오류·경고·백업처럼 무게 있는 안내가 많아 합니다체. 버튼은 문체와 무관하게 동사형(열기, 가져오기, 건너뛰기)." "오류 = **원인 + 다음 행동**". "**사용자 문장에 내부 용어를 쓰지 않는다**". "**관형사(이·그·저·새·다른·각·모든·어느) + 명사**는 언제나 묶고"(U+00A0). 문구는 모두 `strings/` 모듈에 두고 `strings.test.ts`가 검사한다.
- **가짜 금지**: "**가짜 금지** — 동작하지 않는 컨트롤은 그리지 않는다. 내용이 정해지지 않은 "…" 메뉴는 두지 않는다." "**가짜 컨트롤 금지**: 메뉴·팔레트·툴바·도움말을 모두 레지스트리에서 렌더링하므로 핸들러 없이는 화면에 나타날 수 없다." 이 계획의 각 작업은 자기 기능의 컨트롤·명령만 추가한다(예: 실행기는 팔레트와 같은 작업에서, ⌘, 메뉴는 설정 창과 같은 작업에서).
- **레이아웃 B만**: "이후 구현(M3–)은 **레이아웃 B만** 만든다." "**B를 고르면 불변 조건**: 사이드바 위 88px 고정, 사이드바 폭 모든 탭 공유, 접힘 시 실행기 위치(탭 줄 오른쪽 끝). M3에서 탭 전환 전후의 창 위 88px 스크린샷이 픽셀 단위로 같은지 E2E로 지킨다." M3–M6에는 탭이 하나뿐이므로 "탭 전환"은 화면 전환(빈 라이브러리 ↔ 라이브러리 ↔ PDF ↔ 동영상)이고, 비교 영역은 사이드바의 위 88px(x 0–사이드바 폭, y 0–88)이다. "탭이 하나뿐이면(라이브러리만 열린 상태, M3–M6 내내) 탭 줄을 그리지 않는다."
- **토큰만**: "화면 코드에서 색·간격·반경·글자 크기를 직접 쓰지 않는다." 토큰이 아닌 값은 목업처럼 같은 줄에 `/* local: 이유 */`. "한글 UI에 700을 쓰지 않는다(600이 최대)". `css-lint.test.ts`(Task 1)가 색·글자 크기 리터럴을 막는다.
- **작업마다 녹색**: 작업을 끝낼 때 `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test`, `pnpm tokens:check`, `pnpm e2e:xvfb`가 모두 통과한다. 증거 하네스를 건드리는 작업은 `xvfb-run -a -s "-screen 0 1440x900x24" pnpm exec playwright test -c playwright.evidence.config.ts`도 통과한다(Linux CI가 매번 돌린다).
- **macOS 실행은 M3 전체에서 2회**: Task 8 끝(`evidence`, 창 크롬 보정)과 Task 14(`all`). 3회째가 필요하면 소유자에게 먼저 묻는다(비공개 저장소 10배 차감).
- **커밋 메시지 끝**(모든 커밋):
  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01LH2FDh4QwsGUFR3V2KewHi
  ```

## 검토 절차 (모든 작업)

1. **구현자**: 작업의 단계를 순서대로(테스트 먼저 → 실패 확인 → 구현 → 통과 → 커밋).
2. **명세 검토**: 작업의 요구 사항과 Global Constraints를 대조한다. 계획과 다르게 구현했다면 이유가 커밋 본문에 있어야 한다.
3. **품질 검토**: 코드 품질, 테스트의 의미(실패할 수 있는 단언인지), 접근성 속성.
4. **디자인 비평(UI 작업, 모델 opus)** — 작업의 "디자인 비평" 단계에 적힌 짝과 범위로:
   1. 목업 렌더가 없거나 목업이 바뀌었으면 `pnpm mockups:render`(→ `docs/design/renders/`, 커밋 안 함).
   2. `LEARN_TOUR_2X=1 pnpm e2e:xvfb tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts` → `test-results/tour/<상태>-<테마>.png`(1440×900 @2x, 목업 렌더와 같은 크기).
   3. 비평 에이전트(opus)에게 다음을 준다: `docs/design/review-rubric.md` 전문, `docs/design/design-spec.md`의 지정 절, `docs/design/mockups/assets/mockup.css`(값의 원천), 짝 표(투어 PNG ↔ 렌더 PNG), **비평 범위**(작업에 적힌 요소만. 나중 작업의 요소는 지적하지 않는다), **플랫폼 차이 목록**(아래). 출력은 루브릭 "보고 형식", 심각도 blocker/major/minor/nit.
   4. blocker·major는 모두, minor는 2px 이하 수정이면 고친다 → 기준 스크린샷 갱신(`pnpm e2e:xvfb tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts --update-snapshots`) → 이전 보고서를 붙여 다시 비평. blocker·major 0건이 될 때까지 반복한다. 남은 minor는 커밋 본문에 한 줄씩 적는다.
   - **플랫폼 차이(지적 대상 아님)**: Linux 투어에는 신호등·비브런시가 없고(네이티브), 창 조절 단추(Window Controls Overlay)는 페이지 스크린샷에 찍히지 않으며, 글꼴은 번들 Pretendard, 키캡은 Linux 표기(`Ctrl`, `Alt`, `Shift`)다. 목업은 레이아웃 B 렌더(`*-B.png`)와만 비교한다(없는 변형은 작업에 적힌 대체 렌더).
5. **수정 반복** 후 커밋(작업마다 하나 이상, 메시지 끝에 위 트레일러).

## 시작 전 (Task 1 앞)

- [ ] **M2 위에 있는지 확인** — M2는 `main`에 병합됐다(b5cefdd, PR #3, 마무리 수정 포함). 코디네이터가 `m3-shell`을 그 위로 rebase한다.

```bash
git log --oneline -3                                  # 계획 커밋 하나가 b5cefdd(Merge M2) 위에 있어야 한다
git merge-base --is-ancestor b5cefdd HEAD && echo "on M2"
```
Expected: `on M2`. 아니면 `git rebase main` 후 다시 확인.

- [ ] **설치와 기준선**

```bash
cd /home/baejs/projects/tutor/.worktrees/m3
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm tokens:check && pnpm e2e:xvfb
```
Expected: 모두 통과(M2 완료 상태).

- [ ] **이 계획이 기대는 M2 계약 확인** — 아래 명령이 모두 한 줄 이상 출력해야 한다. 하나라도 없으면 멈추고 M2 코드를 읽어 이 계획의 해당 Interfaces를 고친 뒤 시작한다.

```bash
grep -n "export async function runMenuCommand" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "export async function importPaths" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "export const openDialogCalls" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "export async function nativeKeystroke" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "export async function bringAppToFront" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "fontSize: z.union" packages/domain/src/settings.ts
grep -n "density: z.enum(\['compact', 'regular', 'comfortable'\])" packages/domain/src/settings.ts
grep -n "export async function stubContextMenu" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "export async function menuSnapshot" tests/e2e/helpers.ts
grep -n "export function createHandle" apps/desktop/src/main/ipc/register.ts
grep -n "export function buildAppMenu" apps/desktop/src/main/menu/app-menu.ts
grep -n "export function popupCommandMenu" apps/desktop/src/main/menu/context-menu.ts
grep -n "export function runCommand" apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/commands/registry.ts
grep -n "export function useLearnEvent" apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/events.ts
grep -n "prefsChanged" packages/domain/src/events.ts
grep -n "windowState: 'ui:windowState'" packages/domain/src/ipc-channels.ts
grep -n "open: 'reader:open'" packages/domain/src/ipc-channels.ts
grep -n "export function registerAllIpc" apps/desktop/src/main/ipc/all.ts
grep -n "'layout.panes'" packages/domain/src/settings.ts
ls apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/viewers/usePosition.ts tests/e2e/platform.spec.ts tests/e2e/position.spec.ts
```
M2 도우미(`tests/e2e/helpers.ts`, b5cefdd 기준): `runMenuCommand(app, id)`는 메뉴 트리에서 `id`로 MenuItem을 찾아 `click()`을 부르고 **항목이 비활성이거나 숨겨져 있으면 예외를 던진다**(Task 2에서 활성 상태가 생기므로 잠깐 기다리도록 고친다). `importPaths(page, paths)`는 `learn.library.import`(사본)로 가져오고 새 항목을 돌려준다. `stubOpenDialog(app, paths)` + `openDialogCalls(app)`는 대화상자 호출 횟수를 센다. macOS 전용 `nativeKeystroke(app, key, modifiers)`·`bringAppToFront(app)`·`osascript(script)`·`NativeInputNotPermitted`는 System Events로 실제 키를 보낸다. Linux에서는 Playwright(CDP)로 누른 ⌘/Ctrl 조합이 네이티브 메뉴 가속기에 닿지 않는다(이 계획 작성 중 스파이크로 확인: Ctrl+K·Ctrl+/·Ctrl+,·Ctrl+=·Ctrl+Shift+S 모두 0회). 그래서 Linux E2E는 ⌘ 조합을 `runMenuCommand`로 실행하고, 실제 키 입력 경로는 macOS 증거(Task 8·13, `osascript` System Events)로 확인한다.

- [ ] **목업 렌더 준비**(디자인 비평 입력, 커밋하지 않음): `pnpm mockups:render` → `docs/design/renders/*.png` 생성 확인.

## File Structure

새로 만들거나 크게 바꾸는 파일과 책임. 경로는 저장소 루트 기준이고 `R = apps/desktop/src/renderer/src`, `M = apps/desktop/src/main`.

**도메인(`packages/domain/src`, zod 없는 파일은 sandbox preload도 가져온다)**
- `accelerator.ts` (새, zod 없음) — Electron 가속기 → 플랫폼별 가속기(`⌃⌘` → Linux `Ctrl+Shift`), ⌃⌥⇧⌘ 순서 키캡, 표기 문자열.
- `commands.ts` (변경) — `CommandSpec` v3(`palette`, `keywords`, `key`, `labelOff`, 메뉴 `app`/`help`, 컨텍스트 메뉴 종류 `item|collection|tag|column`), M3 명령, **단 하나의 가용성 판정** `isCommandAvailable(id, ctx, targets)`와 `CommandContextState`, `availableMenuCommands`, `MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS`.
- `sheets.ts` (새, zod 없음) — 시트 창 payload/결과 타입.
- `ipc-channels.ts`, `ipc.ts` (변경) — `ui.reportContext/confirmDuplicate/showShortcuts/openSettings`, 종류별 `ui.showContextMenu`, `sheet.payload/resolve`, `library.showInFolder/openExternally`, `app.showLibraryFolder/chooseLibraryFolder`. renderer의 `prefs.set`에서 `libraryRoot`를 뺀다(R4).
- `settings.ts` — 필드는 바꾸지 않는다. M2(b5cefdd)가 이미 prefs `density: 'compact'|'regular'|'comfortable'`(기본 `regular`)와 `fontSize: 12|13|14|15`(기본 13)를 명세대로 갖고 있다. M3는 이 값을 화면에 적용만 한다.
- `library.ts` (변경) — `AppInfo.libraryRootDisplay`.

**main(`M`)**
- `window-url.ts` (새) — 창 종류·플랫폼·결정적 렌더·비브런시·데모 플래그를 쿼리로 붙인 URL.
- `deterministic.ts` (새) — `LEARN_DETERMINISTIC=1`일 때의 Chromium 스위치 목록.
- `window-options.ts` (새) — 플랫폼·테마별 창 크롬 옵션(순수 함수)과 테마 변경 시 적용.
- `window.ts` (변경) — 메인 창: 크롬 옵션, `name:'main'` + `windowStatePersistence`, 시트 오프셋, 성능 표시.
- `about.ts` (새) — About 패널 옵션.
- `sheets.ts` (새) — 모달 시트 창 호스트(열기·payload·결과, 내용 높이에 맞춤).
- `measure.ts` (새) — 숨긴 창의 렌더러에서 요소 높이를 재는 도우미.
- `settings-window.ts` (새) — ⌘, 설정 창(하나만, 폭 600, 내용 높이).
- `display-path.ts` (새) — 홈 경로를 `~`로 줄인 표시 문자열.
- `perf.ts` (새) — 냉시작 측정 표시(`globalThis.__learnPerf`).
- `menu/app-menu.ts`, `menu/context-menu.ts` (변경) — 보고된 상황으로 활성 상태, `app`/`help` 메뉴 배치, 플랫폼 가속기·레이블, ⌘+의 ⌘= 숨은 짝, 종류별 컨텍스트 메뉴.
- `ipc/ui.ts`, `ipc/library.ts`, `ipc/app.ts` (변경), `ipc/sheet.ts` (새).
- `app-protocol.ts` (변경) — 글꼴 MIME, `contentTypeFor()`.
- `index.ts` (변경) — 위 모듈 연결, 메뉴 명령을 메인 창으로 보냄, 명령 상태로 메뉴 재구성.

**렌더러(`R`)**
- `styles/` — `layers.css`(층 순서), `reset.css`, `legacy.css`(Task 1–7 동안만), `base.css`, `utilities.css`, `index.ts`(가져오기 순서), `components/*.css`, `screens/*.css`, `css-lint.test.ts`.

### Task 1: UI 스택 의존성, CSS `@layer` 구조, 글꼴 MIME

화면은 바꾸지 않는다(옛 화면은 `legacy` 층으로 격리). 이후 모든 CSS가 기대는 층 순서·기초 규칙·검사를 만든다.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/desktop/package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `README.md`
- Create: `R/styles/layers.css`, `R/styles/reset.css`, `R/styles/base.css`, `R/styles/utilities.css`, `R/styles/index.ts`, `R/styles/css-lint.test.ts`
- Move: `R/styles.css` → `R/styles/legacy.css`(내용을 `@layer legacy { … }`로 감쌈)
- Modify: `R/main.tsx`(`import './styles'`)
- Modify: `M/app-protocol.ts`(MIME 표 + `contentTypeFor`)
- Create: `M/app-protocol.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`(글꼴 로드 확인)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: 층 순서 `reset, legacy, tokens, base, components, screens, utilities`(Task 7에서 `legacy` 제거). `R/styles/index.ts`는 CSS를 이 순서로 가져오는 유일한 곳이며, 이후 작업은 이 파일 끝에 `import './components/x.css'` / `import './screens/y.css'`를 덧붙인다. 공용 키프레임 `fade-in`, `pop-in`(4px), `rise-in`(8px)과 거리 변수 `--pop-distance`/`--toast-distance`(reduced motion에서 0, base), 클래스 `.num .mono .ellipsis .spacer .ui-prose .ui-balance .t-* .c-* .visually-hidden`.
- Produces: `export function contentTypeFor(file: string): string`(`M/app-protocol.ts`).

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 CSS 구조 검사 작성** — `R/styles/css-lint.test.ts`

```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const LAYERS = ['reset', 'legacy', 'tokens', 'base', 'components', 'screens', 'utilities'];

function cssFiles(d: string): string[] {
  return fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((e) =>
    e.isDirectory() ? cssFiles(path.join(d, e.name)) : e.name.endsWith('.css') ? [path.join(d, e.name)] : [],
  );
}
const layered = cssFiles(dir).filter((f) => path.basename(f) !== 'layers.css');
/** legacy.css is the pre-M3 stylesheet kept for the old screens until Task 7 deletes it. */
const linted = layered.filter((f) => path.basename(f) !== 'legacy.css');
const rel = (f: string) => path.relative(dir, f);
const withoutComments = (s: string) => s.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');

describe('renderer CSS architecture', () => {
  it('declares the cascade layer order once, imported before the tokens', () => {
    const order = /@layer ([^;]+);/.exec(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, 'layers.css'), 'utf8'))?.[1]?.split(',').map((s) => s.trim());
    expect(order).toEqual(LAYERS.filter((l) => l !== 'legacy' || fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, 'legacy.css'))));
    const index = fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, 'index.ts'), 'utf8');
    expect(index.indexOf("import './layers.css';")).toBe(index.search(/import /));
    expect(index.indexOf("import './layers.css';")).toBeLessThan(index.indexOf("import '../design/tokens.css';"));
  });

  it.each(layered.map((f) => [rel(f), f]))('%s puts every rule inside one known layer', (_name, file) => {
    const body = withoutComments(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')).trim();
    const layer = /^@layer ([a-z]+) \{/.exec(body)?.[1];
    expect(layer && LAYERS.includes(layer)).toBe(true);
    expect(body.endsWith('}')).toBe(true);
    // Exactly one top-level block: braces balance only at the very end.
    let depth = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
      if (body[i] === '{') depth++;
      if (body[i] === '}') depth--;
      if (depth === 0 && i > body.indexOf('{') && i < body.length - 1) throw new Error(`${rel(file)}: rule outside the layer at offset ${i}`);
    }
  });

  it.each(linted.map((f) => [rel(f), f]))('%s takes colours and type sizes from tokens (literals only on /* local */ lines)', (_name, file) => {
    const offenders = fs
      .readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
      .split('\n')
      .map((line, i) => ({ line, n: i + 1 }))
      .filter(({ line }) => !line.includes('local'))
      .filter(({ line }) => {
        const code = line.replace(/\/\*.*?\*\//g, '');
        return /#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\b/.test(code) || /\b(rgb|rgba|hsl|hsla|oklch|oklab|lab|lch)\(/.test(code) || /\bfont(-size)?\s*:[^;]*\b\d+(\.\d+)?px/.test(code) || /font-weight\s*:\s*(700|800|900|bold)/.test(code);
      })
      .map(({ line, n }) => `${n}: ${line.trim()}`);
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`R/styles/base.css`:
```css
@layer base {
  body {
    font: var(--type-body);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    background: var(--bg-window);
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
    /* Korean UI wrapping (design-spec 4). */
    word-break: keep-all;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
    line-break: strict;
    cursor: default;
    user-select: none;
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  /* macOS: the main window is transparent so the sidebar's vibrancy shows (Task 8). */
  :root[data-vibrancy='1'] body {
    background: transparent;
  }
  :focus {
    outline: none;
  }
  :focus-visible {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: var(--focus-ring-offset);
  }
  input,
  textarea {
    user-select: text;
    cursor: text;
  }
  .num {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .mono {
    font: var(--type-mono);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .ellipsis {
    overflow: hidden;
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .spacer {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
  }
  .icon {
    flex: none;
  }
  /* UI prose we write: no one-word last line. Never on user or document text (design-spec 4). */
  .ui-prose {
    text-wrap: pretty;
  }
  .ui-balance {
    text-wrap: balance;
  }
  .t-caption { font: var(--type-caption); }
  .t-footnote { font: var(--type-footnote); }
  .t-body { font: var(--type-body); }
  .t-headline { font: var(--type-headline); }
  .t-paragraph { font: var(--type-paragraph); }
  .t-callout { font: var(--type-callout); }
  .t-title3 { font: var(--type-title3); }
  .t-title2 { font: var(--type-title2); }
  .t-title1 { font: var(--type-title1); letter-spacing: -0.01em; }
  .c-secondary { color: var(--text-secondary); }
  .c-tertiary { color: var(--text-tertiary); }
  .c-accent { color: var(--accent-text); }
  .c-danger { color: var(--danger-text); }
  .c-warning { color: var(--warning-text); }
  .c-success { color: var(--success-text); }
  /* Entrance motion (design-spec 8.1): popovers 4 px, toasts 8 px; reduced motion keeps only the fade. */
  :root {
    --pop-distance: -4px;
    --toast-distance: 8px;
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    :root {
      --pop-distance: 0px;
      --toast-distance: 0px;
    }
  }
  @keyframes fade-in {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
    }
  }
  @keyframes pop-in {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      translate: 0 var(--pop-distance);
    }
  }
  @keyframes rise-in {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      translate: 0 var(--toast-distance);
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

vi.mock('electron', () => ({ protocol: {} }));
const { contentTypeFor } = await import('./app-protocol');

describe('app protocol static content types', () => {
  it('serves the bundled fonts with font MIME types', () => {
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/assets/PretendardVariable-3f2a.woff2')).toBe('font/woff2');
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/assets/x.WOFF')).toBe('font/woff');
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/assets/x.otf')).toBe('font/otf');
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/pdfjs/standard_fonts/x.ttf')).toBe('font/ttf');
  });
  it('keeps the existing types and falls back to octet-stream', () => {
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/index.html')).toBe('text/html; charset=utf-8');
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/assets/index-1.js')).toBe('text/javascript');
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/assets/index-1.css')).toBe('text/css');
    expect(contentTypeFor('/r/x.unknown')).toBe('application/octet-stream');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 구현** — `M/app-protocol.ts`에서 `STATIC_TYPES`에 세 줄을 더하고 함수로 노출한다.

```ts
const STATIC_TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.mjs': 'text/javascript', '.css': 'text/css',
  '.json': 'application/json', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.png': 'image/png', '.wasm': 'application/wasm',
  '.bcmap': 'application/octet-stream', '.pfb': 'application/octet-stream', '.ttf': 'font/ttf', '.icc': 'application/octet-stream',
  '.woff2': 'font/woff2', '.woff': 'font/woff', '.otf': 'font/otf',
};

/** MIME type for a file served from the renderer bundle. */
export function contentTypeFor(file: string): string {
  return STATIC_TYPES[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] ?? 'application/octet-stream';
}
```
`serveStatic` 안의 `const type = STATIC_TYPES[...] ?? 'application/octet-stream';` 줄을 `const type = contentTypeFor(file);`로 바꾼다.

### Task 2: 명령 레지스트리 v3 — 기반 작업 (UI 작업보다 먼저)

M2 마무리 검토가 M3에 넘긴 R1을 화면 작업 전에 끝낸다. 레지스트리 하나에 **팔레트 노출·검색어·한 개의 가용성 판정·플랫폼별 가속기와 ⌘ 표기·컨텍스트 메뉴 종류**를 모으고, 렌더러가 자기 상황(`CommandContextState`)을 main에 알리면(`ui:reportContext`, ADR-0005 §2의 `ui:context`) main이 **같은 판정 함수**로 메뉴 막대와 컨텍스트 메뉴의 활성 상태를 정한다. 화면은 바뀌지 않는다(파일 메뉴 레이블과 단축키만 명세 7.3·7.4에 맞춘다).

**결정(이 작업이 확정):**
- 가용성은 `packages/domain/src/commands.ts`의 순수 함수 `isCommandAvailable(id, ctx, targets)` 하나뿐이다. main의 `context-menu.ts` RULES와 렌더러 `runCommand`의 맥락 검사를 이 함수로 옮긴다.
- main이 렌더러 상황을 아는 방법: 렌더러가 `CommandContextState`(M3: `view`, `blocked`; Task 10에서 `textSize`)가 바뀔 때마다 `learn.ui.reportContext(ctx)`로 보낸다. main은 마지막 상황으로 `availableMenuCommands(ctx)`를 계산해 메뉴를 다시 만든다. 보고 전(시작 직후)에는 레지스트리 항목이 모두 비활성이다. 메인 창 외의 창(설정·시트)이 보낸 보고는 무시한다.
- 컨텍스트 메뉴의 선택 결과는 지금처럼 **`ui:showContextMenu` invoke의 반환값**(`{ commandId } | null`)으로 돌아온다(`menu:command` 푸시가 아니다). 렌더러는 그 명령을 main이 이미 새 파일 상태로 판정했으므로 `runPicked`로 바로 실행한다(가져오기·시트 중이면 무시).
- `nav.back`(라이브러리로 돌아가기)의 가속기는 ⌘[가 아니라 **⌘1**이다: 명세 7.4에서 ⌘[/⌘]는 PDF 이동 기록(M7), ⌘1은 첫 탭(=라이브러리 탭)이다. M3–M6에는 탭이 없으므로 ⌘1이 "라이브러리로" 역할을 한다.
- "파일 없음 자료는 맨 위에 '다시 연결…'"(명세 7.3): 컨텍스트 메뉴의 `다시 연결…`은 대상이 외부 연결 + 파일 없음일 때만 **보이고**(그 밖에는 비활성이 아니라 빠진다) 맨 위 묶음에 온다.

- Create: `packages/domain/src/accelerator.ts`, `packages/domain/test/accelerator.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/domain/src/commands.ts`, `packages/domain/test/commands.test.ts`, `packages/domain/src/ipc-channels.ts`, `packages/domain/src/ipc.ts`, `packages/domain/test/ipc-types.test.ts`, `packages/domain/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (domain `accelerator.ts`, zod 없음): `platformAccelerator(acc, platform): string`(`⌃⌘X` → Linux/Windows `Ctrl+Shift+X`), `acceleratorKeys(acc, platform): string[]`(macOS ⌃⌥⇧⌘ 순서 기호, 그 밖 `Ctrl`/`Alt`/`Shift`), `acceleratorLabel(acc, platform): string`("⌥⌘O" / "Ctrl+Alt+O"), `interface KeySpec { code: string; shift?: boolean; inText?: boolean }`, `keySpecKeys(key, platform): string[]`.
- Produces (domain `commands.ts`, zod 없음):
  - `type CommandContext = 'app' | 'library' | 'viewer' | 'item'`, `type MenuName = 'app' | 'file' | 'edit' | 'view' | 'go' | 'window' | 'help'`, `type ContextMenuKind = 'item' | 'collection' | 'tag' | 'column'`, `CONTEXT_MENU_KINDS`.
  - `CommandSpec` = `{ id; label; labelOff?; accelerator?; key?: KeySpec; menu?: { menu; group; order }; contextMenu?: { kinds: readonly ContextMenuKind[]; group: number; order: number; onlyWhenAvailable?: boolean }; contexts: CommandContext[]; palette?: boolean; keywords?: readonly string[] }` (M2의 `contextMenu?: boolean`을 대체).
  - `interface CommandContextState { view: 'library' | 'viewer'; blocked: boolean }`(이후 작업이 필드를 더한다), `interface CommandTargetInfo { storageMode: StorageMode; availability: Availability }`.
  - `isCommandAvailable(id, ctx, targets?): boolean`, `availableMenuCommands(ctx): CommandId[]`, `commandLabel(spec, platform): string`, `inPalette(spec): boolean`, `MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS: ReadonlySet<CommandId>`(Task 10에서 `app.settings`), `commandById(id)`.
- Produces (IPC): `Channels.ui.reportContext = 'ui:reportContext'`, `LearnApi.ui.reportContext(input: CommandContextState): Promise<Result<void>>`, `commandContextInput`(zod, `.strict()`), `commandIdSchema`; `showContextMenuInput`은 `kind`로 나뉜 합집합 — `{ kind: 'item'; itemIds; x; y } | { kind: 'collection'; collectionIds; x; y } | { kind: 'tag'; tagIds; x; y } | { kind: 'column'; columnId; x; y }`(M3는 `item`만 항목이 있고, 나머지 종류는 해당 명령이 생기는 M5까지 메뉴 없이 `null`).
- Produces (main): `buildAppMenu({ isDev, platform, commands, enabled: ReadonlySet<CommandId>, onCommand })`(레지스트리 항목은 `enabled.has(id) || MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS.has(id)`일 때만 활성, `menu: 'app'`은 macOS 밖에서 파일 메뉴 끝, 가속기는 `platformAccelerator`, 레이블은 `commandLabel`, `'CmdOrCtrl+Plus'`에 숨은 `'CmdOrCtrl+='` 짝 `<id>#1`, 빈 최상위 메뉴 생략); `contextMenuEntries(kind, commands, ctx, targets, platform): Array<ContextEntry | 'separator'>`, `popupCommandMenu(entries, at)`(M2 그대로, 구분선 지원); `IpcDeps.uiContext: { report(sender: WebContents, ctx: CommandContextState): void; current(): CommandContextState }`. 메뉴 명령은 항상 메인 창으로 보낸다. 테스트용 표시 `globalThis.__learnMenuStateVersion`(보고마다 +1).
- Produces (renderer): `interface CommandEnv extends CommandContextState { targetsOf(itemIds: readonly string[]): CommandTargetInfo[]; importFiles(mode); back(); open(itemId); relink(itemId) }`(이후 작업이 핸들러 필드를 더한다); `COMMAND_HANDLERS: Record<CommandId, (env, itemIds) => void>`; `contextOf(env): CommandContextState`; `isCommandEnabled(id, env, target?)`; `runCommand(id, env, target?)`; `runPicked(id, env, itemIds)`; `useReportContext(env)`.
- Produces (tests): `runMenuCommand`는 항목이 활성화될 때까지 최대 5초 기다린다; `launch()`는 첫 상황 보고(`__learnMenuStateVersion ≥ 1`)까지 기다린다; `menuItemEnabled(app, id): Promise<boolean>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: 가속기 표기 — 실패하는 테스트** `packages/domain/test/accelerator.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { acceleratorKeys, acceleratorLabel, keySpecKeys, platformAccelerator } from '../src/accelerator';

describe('accelerators', () => {
  it('maps ⌃⌘ combinations to Ctrl+Shift off macOS and leaves the rest', () => {
    expect(platformAccelerator('Ctrl+Cmd+S', 'linux')).toBe('Ctrl+Shift+S');
    expect(platformAccelerator('Ctrl+Cmd+S', 'darwin')).toBe('Ctrl+Cmd+S');
    expect(platformAccelerator('Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O', 'win32')).toBe('Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O');
  });
  it('draws macOS keycaps in ⌃⌥⇧⌘ order with Apple key symbols', () => {
    expect(acceleratorKeys('Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌥', '⌘', 'O']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('Ctrl+Cmd+S', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌃', '⌘', 'S']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('Shift+CmdOrCtrl+R', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⇧', '⌘', 'R']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('CmdOrCtrl+Plus', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌘', '+']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('CmdOrCtrl+-', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌘', '−']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('CmdOrCtrl+,', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌘', ',']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('CmdOrCtrl+Backspace', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌘', '⌫']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('Ctrl+Tab', 'darwin')).toEqual(['⌃', '⇥']);
    expect(acceleratorLabel('Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O', 'darwin')).toBe('⌥⌘O');
  });
  it('spells modifiers out on Linux and Windows', () => {
    expect(acceleratorKeys('Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O', 'linux')).toEqual(['Ctrl', 'Alt', 'O']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('Ctrl+Cmd+S', 'linux')).toEqual(['Ctrl', 'Shift', 'S']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('CmdOrCtrl+Plus', 'win32')).toEqual(['Ctrl', '+']);
    expect(acceleratorKeys('CmdOrCtrl+-', 'linux')).toEqual(['Ctrl', '-']);
    expect(acceleratorLabel('Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O', 'linux')).toBe('Ctrl+Alt+O');
  });
  it('draws single-key shortcuts from the physical key code', () => {
    expect(keySpecKeys({ code: 'F6' }, 'darwin')).toEqual(['F6']);
    expect(keySpecKeys({ code: 'F6', shift: true }, 'darwin')).toEqual(['⇧', 'F6']);
    expect(keySpecKeys({ code: 'KeyJ' }, 'linux')).toEqual(['J']);
    expect(keySpecKeys({ code: 'Digit1', shift: true }, 'linux')).toEqual(['Shift', '1']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 구현** `packages/domain/src/accelerator.ts`

```ts
/**
 * Electron accelerator helpers shared by the native menu (main) and keycaps/tooltips/palette (renderer).
 * No zod and no imports: the sandboxed preload may load this file.
 */
export interface KeySpec {
  /** KeyboardEvent.code (physical key), so shortcuts work with the Korean input source too (ADR-0005). */
  code: string;
  shift?: boolean;
  /** Also handled while a text field has focus (function keys such as F6). Letters never are. */
  inText?: boolean;
}

const MAC_SYMBOL: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  Ctrl: '⌃', Control: '⌃', Alt: '⌥', Option: '⌥', Shift: '⇧', Cmd: '⌘', Command: '⌘', CmdOrCtrl: '⌘', CommandOrControl: '⌘',
};
const MAC_ORDER = ['⌃', '⌥', '⇧', '⌘'];
const OTHER_NAME: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  Ctrl: 'Ctrl', Control: 'Ctrl', CmdOrCtrl: 'Ctrl', CommandOrControl: 'Ctrl', Alt: 'Alt', Option: 'Alt', Shift: 'Shift',
  Cmd: 'Super', Command: 'Super', Super: 'Super', Meta: 'Super',
};
const OTHER_ORDER = ['Ctrl', 'Alt', 'Shift', 'Super'];
const MAC_KEY: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  Plus: '+', '-': '−', Enter: '↩', Return: '↩', Escape: 'esc', Esc: 'esc', Backspace: '⌫', Delete: '⌦', Tab: '⇥',
  Up: '↑', Down: '↓', Left: '←', Right: '→',
};
const OTHER_KEY: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = { Plus: '+', Return: 'Enter', Escape: 'Esc', Up: '↑', Down: '↓', Left: '←', Right: '→' };

const split = (acc: string): { mods: string[]; key: string } => {
  const parts = acc.split('+');
  const key = parts.pop() ?? '';
  return { mods: parts, key };
};

/** ⌃⌘ combinations have no Linux/Windows counterpart (Cmd would be the Super key): they become Ctrl+Shift there. */
export function platformAccelerator(accelerator: string, platform: string): string {
  if (platform === 'darwin') return accelerator;
  const { mods, key } = split(accelerator);
  const hasCmd = mods.some((m) => m === 'Cmd' || m === 'Command');
  const hasCtrl = mods.some((m) => m === 'Ctrl' || m === 'Control');
  if (!(hasCmd && hasCtrl)) return accelerator;
  const rest = mods.filter((m) => m !== 'Cmd' && m !== 'Command' && m !== 'Shift');
  return [...rest, 'Shift', key].join('+');
}

/** Keycaps for an accelerator: macOS symbols in ⌃⌥⇧⌘ order, elsewhere spelled-out modifiers. */
export function acceleratorKeys(accelerator: string, platform: string): string[] {
  const { mods, key } = split(platformAccelerator(accelerator, platform));
  if (platform === 'darwin') {
    const symbols = mods.map((m) => MAC_SYMBOL[m] ?? m).sort((a, b) => MAC_ORDER.indexOf(a) - MAC_ORDER.indexOf(b));
    return [...symbols, MAC_KEY[key] ?? (key.length === 1 ? key.toUpperCase() : key)];
  }
  const names = mods.map((m) => OTHER_NAME[m] ?? m).sort((a, b) => OTHER_ORDER.indexOf(a) - OTHER_ORDER.indexOf(b));
  return [...names, OTHER_KEY[key] ?? (key.length === 1 ? key.toUpperCase() : key)];
}

/** One-line form for menus, tooltips and the palette: "⌥⌘O" on macOS, "Ctrl+Alt+O" elsewhere. */
export function acceleratorLabel(accelerator: string, platform: string): string {
  const keys = acceleratorKeys(accelerator, platform);
  return platform === 'darwin' ? keys.join('') : keys.join('+');
}

export function keySpecKeys(key: KeySpec, platform: string): string[] {
  const name = key.code.startsWith('Key') ? key.code.slice(3) : key.code.startsWith('Digit') ? key.code.slice(5) : key.code;
  return key.shift ? [platform === 'darwin' ? '⇧' : 'Shift', name] : [name];
}
```
`packages/domain/src/index.ts`에 `export * from './accelerator';`. Run → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 3: 레지스트리와 가용성 — 실패하는 테스트**

`packages/domain/test/commands.test.ts`의 `describe('command registry', …)` 블록을 다음으로 바꾼다(채널·태그 색·위치 테스트는 그대로).
```ts
const LIB: CommandContextState = { view: 'library', blocked: false };
const VIEWER: CommandContextState = { view: 'viewer', blocked: false };
const linkedMissing: CommandTargetInfo = { storageMode: 'linked', availability: 'missing' };
const managedOk: CommandTargetInfo = { storageMode: 'managed', availability: 'available' };

describe('command registry', () => {
  it('has unique ids and resolves by id', () => {
    const ids = COMMANDS.map((c) => c.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const c of COMMANDS) expect(commandById(c.id)).toBe(c);
  });
  it('gives every accelerator and every single key to one command (one native menu serves every view in M3)', () => {
    const accels = COMMANDS.flatMap((c) => (c.accelerator ? [c.accelerator] : []));
    expect(new Set(accels).size).toBe(accels.length);
    const keys = COMMANDS.flatMap((c) => (c.key ? [`${c.key.shift ? 'Shift+' : ''}${c.key.code}`] : []));
    expect(new Set(keys).size).toBe(keys.length);
    expect(accels).not.toContain('CmdOrCtrl+['); // ⌘[ / ⌘] belong to PDF history (M7, design-spec 7.4)
  });
  it('labels every command in Korean; placements use known menus; context menus use known kinds', () => {
    for (const c of COMMANDS) {
      expect(c.label, c.id).toMatch(/[가-힣]/);
      if (c.menu) expect(['app', 'file', 'edit', 'view', 'go', 'window', 'help'], c.id).toContain(c.menu.menu);
      for (const kind of c.contextMenu?.kinds ?? []) expect(CONTEXT_MENU_KINDS, c.id).toContain(kind);
      if (c.contextMenu?.kinds.includes('item')) expect(c.contexts, c.id).toContain('item');
    }
  });
  it('accepts every accelerator shape the design spec uses (7.4)', () => {
    const re = ACCELERATOR_SYNTAX;
    for (const a of ['CmdOrCtrl+,', 'CmdOrCtrl+/', 'Ctrl+Tab', 'Ctrl+Shift+Tab', 'CmdOrCtrl+Plus', 'CmdOrCtrl+-', 'CmdOrCtrl+0', 'CmdOrCtrl+Backspace', 'Ctrl+Cmd+S', 'Alt+CmdOrCtrl+I', 'Shift+CmdOrCtrl+R', 'CmdOrCtrl+[', 'CmdOrCtrl+1', 'Ctrl+Cmd+F']) {
      expect(a).toMatch(re);
    }
    for (const c of COMMANDS) if (c.accelerator) expect(c.accelerator, c.id).toMatch(re);
  });
  it('gives palette commands Korean search words (초성 search works on them)', () => {
    for (const c of COMMANDS.filter(inPalette)) {
      expect(c.keywords?.length ?? 0, c.id).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(c.keywords!.some((k) => /[가-힣]/.test(k)), c.id).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(inPalette(commandById('item.open'))).toBe(false);
    expect(inPalette(commandById('file.importManaged'))).toBe(true);
  });
  it('defines the registry commands with the design-spec labels and keys', () => {
    expect(COMMANDS.map((c) => c.id).sort()).toEqual(['file.importLinked', 'file.importManaged', 'item.open', 'item.relink', 'nav.back']);
    expect(commandById('file.importManaged')).toMatchObject({ label: '파일 가져오기…', accelerator: 'CmdOrCtrl+O' });
    expect(commandById('file.importLinked')).toMatchObject({ label: '외부 파일 연결…', accelerator: 'Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O' });
    expect(commandById('nav.back')).toMatchObject({ label: '라이브러리로 돌아가기', accelerator: 'CmdOrCtrl+1', contexts: ['viewer'] });
  });
});

describe('availability (one predicate for the menu bar, palette, toolbar and context menus)', () => {
  it('follows the view, and nothing runs while blocked', () => {
    expect(isCommandAvailable('file.importManaged', LIB)).toBe(true);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('file.importManaged', VIEWER)).toBe(false);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('nav.back', LIB)).toBe(false);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('nav.back', VIEWER)).toBe(true);
    for (const c of COMMANDS) expect(isCommandAvailable(c.id, { ...VIEWER, blocked: true }, [linkedMissing]), c.id).toBe(MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS.has(c.id));
  });
  it('item commands need targets: 열기 one item, 다시 연결… one linked item whose file is missing', () => {
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.open', LIB)).toBe(false);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.open', LIB, [managedOk])).toBe(true);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.open', LIB, [managedOk, managedOk])).toBe(false);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.relink', LIB, [linkedMissing])).toBe(true);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.relink', LIB, [managedOk])).toBe(false);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.relink', LIB, [{ storageMode: 'managed', availability: 'missing' }])).toBe(false);
    expect(isCommandAvailable('item.relink', LIB, [linkedMissing, linkedMissing])).toBe(false);
  });
  it('lists the menu-bar commands of a view (never item commands)', () => {
    expect(availableMenuCommands(LIB)).toEqual(['file.importManaged', 'file.importLinked']);
    expect(availableMenuCommands(VIEWER)).toEqual(['nav.back']);
    expect(availableMenuCommands({ ...LIB, blocked: true })).toEqual([...MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS].filter((id) => commandById(id).menu));
  });
  it('names macOS apps only on macOS', () => {
    const c = { ...commandById('file.importManaged'), labelOff: '다른 이름' };
    expect(commandLabel(c, 'darwin')).toBe('파일 가져오기…');
    expect(commandLabel(c, 'linux')).toBe('다른 이름');
    expect(commandLabel(commandById('file.importManaged'), 'linux')).toBe('파일 가져오기…');
  });
});
```
상단 import를 `import { ACCELERATOR_SYNTAX, COMMANDS, CONTEXT_MENU_KINDS, MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS, availableMenuCommands, commandById, commandLabel, inPalette, isCommandAvailable, type CommandContextState, type CommandTargetInfo } from '../src/commands';`로 바꾼다. 이후 작업은 "defines the registry commands"의 id 목록과 `availableMenuCommands` 기대값에 자기 명령을 더한다.

- [ ] **Step 4: 구현** `packages/domain/src/commands.ts` 전체

```ts
import type { KeySpec } from './accelerator';
import type { Availability, StorageMode } from './library';

/**
 * Single command registry (ADR-0005): the menu bar, ⌘K palette, native context menus, toolbar tooltips and the
 * shortcut sheet all render from it, and one predicate (isCommandAvailable) decides what can run.
 * No zod: the sandboxed preload imports this file (type imports only).
 */
export type CommandId = 'file.importManaged' | 'file.importLinked' | 'nav.back' | 'item.open' | 'item.relink';
/** `app`: anywhere in the main window; `library` / `viewer`: the current view; `item`: needs item targets. */
export type CommandContext = 'app' | 'library' | 'viewer' | 'item';
/** `app` is the macOS application menu (Linux/Windows put those items at the end of 파일); `help` is 도움말. */
export type MenuName = 'app' | 'file' | 'edit' | 'view' | 'go' | 'window' | 'help';
export const CONTEXT_MENU_KINDS = ['item', 'collection', 'tag', 'column'] as const;
/** Native context menus (ADR-0005 §4): items now; collections, tags and table column headers from M4–M5. */
export type ContextMenuKind = (typeof CONTEXT_MENU_KINDS)[number];

export interface CommandSpec {
  id: CommandId;
  /** Korean label exactly as menus show it ("…" when more input follows). */
  label: string;
  /** Label off macOS when the macOS one names a macOS app (Finder). */
  labelOff?: string;
  /** Electron accelerator, owned by the native menu. */
  accelerator?: string;
  /** Single-key shortcut, matched by physical key in the renderer keymap. */
  key?: KeySpec;
  menu?: { menu: MenuName; group: number; order: number };
  /** Native context menus listing this command; separators between groups. */
  contextMenu?: { kinds: readonly ContextMenuKind[]; group: number; order: number; onlyWhenAvailable?: boolean };
  contexts: CommandContext[];
  /** In the ⌘K palette while available. Default: yes, unless the command needs item targets. */
  palette?: boolean;
  /** Extra palette search words: Korean first (초성 search applies), English for habit. */
  keywords?: readonly string[];
}

/** Electron accelerator grammar used by the registry (design-spec 7.4 keys included). */
export const ACCELERATOR_SYNTAX = /^((CmdOrCtrl|Cmd|Ctrl|Alt|Shift)\+)*([A-Za-z0-9]|\[|\]|,|\/|-|Plus|F\d{1,2}|Enter|Delete|Backspace|Tab|Space|Escape)$/;

export const COMMANDS: readonly CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    id: 'file.importManaged', label: '파일 가져오기…', accelerator: 'CmdOrCtrl+O', menu: { menu: 'file', group: 1, order: 1 }, contexts: ['library'],
    keywords: ['가져오기', '추가', '사본', 'PDF', '동영상', 'import', 'add'],
  },
  {
    id: 'file.importLinked', label: '외부 파일 연결…', accelerator: 'Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O', menu: { menu: 'file', group: 1, order: 2 }, contexts: ['library'],
    keywords: ['연결', '외부', '링크', 'link'],
  },
  {
    id: 'nav.back', label: '라이브러리로 돌아가기', accelerator: 'CmdOrCtrl+1', menu: { menu: 'go', group: 1, order: 1 }, contexts: ['viewer'],
    keywords: ['라이브러리', '뒤로', '목록', 'library', 'back'],
  },
  { id: 'item.relink', label: '다시 연결…', contexts: ['item'], contextMenu: { kinds: ['item'], group: 0, order: 1, onlyWhenAvailable: true } },
  { id: 'item.open', label: '열기', contexts: ['item'], contextMenu: { kinds: ['item'], group: 1, order: 1 } },
];

export function commandById(id: CommandId): CommandSpec {
  const c = COMMANDS.find((x) => x.id === id);
  if (!c) throw new Error(`unknown command: ${id}`);
  return c;
}

export const inPalette = (c: CommandSpec): boolean => c.palette ?? !c.contexts.includes('item');
export const commandLabel = (c: CommandSpec, platform: string): string => (platform !== 'darwin' && c.labelOff ? c.labelOff : c.label);

/** Commands main runs itself: always available, even before (or without) a renderer. */
export const MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS: ReadonlySet<CommandId> = new Set<CommandId>([]);

/** What availability depends on. The main window's renderer owns it and reports it (ui:reportContext). */
export interface CommandContextState {
  view: 'library' | 'viewer';
  /** An import, a modal sheet or the fatal screen owns the window: nothing runs. */
  blocked: boolean;
}

/** A command target, as far as availability goes (fresh from disk in main's context menus). */
export interface CommandTargetInfo {
  storageMode: StorageMode;
  availability: Availability;
}

type Rule = (ctx: CommandContextState, targets: readonly CommandTargetInfo[]) => boolean;
const RULES: Partial<Record<CommandId, Rule>> = {
  'item.open': (_ctx, t) => t.length === 1,
  'item.relink': (_ctx, t) => t.length === 1 && t[0]!.storageMode === 'linked' && t[0]!.availability === 'missing',
};

/** The single availability predicate: main (menu bar, context menus) and the renderer (palette, toolbar, keymap). */
export function isCommandAvailable(id: CommandId, ctx: CommandContextState, targets: readonly CommandTargetInfo[] = []): boolean {
  const spec = COMMANDS.find((c) => c.id === id);
  if (!spec) return false;
  if (MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS.has(id)) return true;
  if (ctx.blocked) return false;
  const inContext = spec.contexts.includes('item') ? targets.length > 0 : spec.contexts.includes('app') || spec.contexts.includes(ctx.view);
  return inContext && (RULES[id]?.(ctx, targets) ?? true);
}

/** Menu-bar commands available in `ctx` (item commands live in context menus). */
export function availableMenuCommands(ctx: CommandContextState): CommandId[] {
  return COMMANDS.filter((c) => c.menu !== undefined && !c.contexts.includes('item') && isCommandAvailable(c.id, ctx)).map((c) => c.id);
}
```
(`library.ts`는 zod 없이 타입만 내보내므로 type import는 preload 번들에 아무것도 더하지 않는다.)

`M/menu/app-menu.ts` 전체:
```ts
import type { MenuItemConstructorOptions } from 'electron';
import { MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS, commandLabel, platformAccelerator, type CommandId, type CommandSpec, type MenuName } from '@learn/domain';

/** Pure template builder (type-only Electron import) so the menu tree is unit-tested. */
export interface AppMenuOptions {
  /** Shows "개발자 도구". Unpackaged runs only, and never under E2E (`LEARN_E2E=1`). */
  isDev: boolean;
  platform: NodeJS.Platform;
  commands: readonly CommandSpec[];
  /** availableMenuCommands(last reported context): the only registry items that are enabled. */
  enabled: ReadonlySet<CommandId>;
  /** Registry item clicked (mouse or accelerator). */
  onCommand(id: CommandId): void;
}

type Item = MenuItemConstructorOptions;
const SEP: Item = { type: 'separator' };

/** ⌘+ is typed as ⌘= on US keyboards: a hidden item with the same command answers it. */
const TWINS: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>> = { 'CmdOrCtrl+Plus': ['CmdOrCtrl+='] };

/** Where a command lands on this platform: "app" items move to the end of 파일 off macOS. */
function placement(c: CommandSpec, platform: NodeJS.Platform): { menu: MenuName; group: number; order: number } | null {
  if (!c.menu) return null;
  if (c.menu.menu === 'app' && platform !== 'darwin') return { menu: 'file', group: 90 + c.menu.group, order: c.menu.order };
  return c.menu;
}

function commandItems(c: CommandSpec, opts: AppMenuOptions): Item[] {
  const enabled = opts.enabled.has(c.id) || MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS.has(c.id);
  const click = () => opts.onCommand(c.id);
  const label = commandLabel(c, opts.platform);
  const item: Item = { id: c.id, label, enabled, click };
  if (c.accelerator) item.accelerator = platformAccelerator(c.accelerator, opts.platform);
  const twins = (c.accelerator ? TWINS[c.accelerator] : undefined) ?? [];
  return [item, ...twins.map((acc, i): Item => ({ id: `${c.id}#${i + 1}`, label, enabled, visible: false, accelerator: platformAccelerator(acc, opts.platform), click }))];
}

/** Registry commands for one menu, grouped (separator between groups) and ordered. */
function registryItems(name: MenuName, opts: AppMenuOptions): Item[] {
  const placed = opts.commands
    .flatMap((c) => {
      const at = placement(c, opts.platform);
      return at?.menu === name ? [{ c, at }] : [];
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.at.group - b.at.group || a.at.order - b.at.order);
  const out: Item[] = [];
  let group: number | null = null;
  for (const { c, at } of placed) {
    if (group !== null && at.group !== group) out.push(SEP);
    group = at.group;
    out.push(...commandItems(c, opts));
  }
  return out;
}

/** Non-empty groups joined by separators. */
const join = (...groups: Item[][]): Item[] => groups.filter((g) => g.length > 0).flatMap((g, i) => (i === 0 ? g : [SEP, ...g]));

/**
 * Korean application menu built from the command registry (ADR-0005). No Reload / Force Reload anywhere;
 * "개발자 도구" only when `isDev`. Menus with no items are left out.
 */
export function buildAppMenu(opts: AppMenuOptions): Item[] {
  const mac = opts.platform === 'darwin';
  const reg = (name: MenuName) => registryItems(name, opts);
  const appMenu: Item = {
    id: 'menu.app',
    label: 'Learn',
    submenu: join(
      [{ role: 'about', label: 'Learn 정보' }],
      reg('app'),
      [{ role: 'services', label: '서비스' }],
      [{ role: 'hide', label: 'Learn 가리기' }, { role: 'hideOthers', label: '기타 가리기' }, { role: 'unhide', label: '모두 보기' }],
      [{ role: 'quit', label: 'Learn 종료' }],
    ),
  };
  const file: Item = { id: 'menu.file', label: '파일', submenu: join(reg('file'), [{ role: 'close', label: '창 닫기', accelerator: 'CmdOrCtrl+W' }]) };
  const edit: Item = {
    id: 'menu.edit',
    label: '편집',
    submenu: join(
      [{ role: 'undo', label: '실행 취소' }, { role: 'redo', label: '실행 복귀' }],
      [{ role: 'cut', label: '오려두기' }, { role: 'copy', label: '복사하기' }, { role: 'paste', label: '붙여넣기' }, { role: 'selectAll', label: '모두 선택' }],
      reg('edit'),
    ),
  };
  const view: Item = {
    id: 'menu.view',
    label: '보기',
    submenu: join(reg('view'), [{ role: 'togglefullscreen', label: '전체 화면' }], opts.isDev ? [{ role: 'toggleDevTools', label: '개발자 도구' }] : []),
  };
  const go: Item = { id: 'menu.go', label: '이동', submenu: reg('go') };
  const windowMenu: Item = {
    id: 'menu.window',
    label: '윈도우',
    role: 'windowMenu',
    submenu: join(
      [{ role: 'minimize', label: '최소화' }, ...(mac ? [{ role: 'zoom', label: '확대/축소' } satisfies Item] : [])],
      mac ? [{ role: 'front', label: '모두 앞으로 가져오기' }] : [],
      reg('window'),
    ),
  };
  const help: Item = { id: 'menu.help', label: '도움말', role: 'help', submenu: reg('help') };
  return [...(mac ? [appMenu] : []), file, edit, view, go, windowMenu, help].filter((m) => Array.isArray(m.submenu) && m.submenu.length > 0);
}
```
(M2의 편집·윈도우·개발자 도구 모양 테스트는 모양이 같아 그대로 통과한다. 보기 메뉴는 이제 레지스트리 항목이 앞, `전체 화면`이 뒤다.)

`M/menu/context-menu.test.ts`: `itemContextEntries` 테스트 묶음을 다음으로 바꾼다(`popupCommandMenu` 테스트는 `entries` 만드는 줄만 새 함수로).
```ts
const LIB: CommandContextState = { view: 'library', blocked: false };
const ids = (entries: ReturnType<typeof contextMenuEntries>) => entries.map((e) => (e === 'separator' ? '-' : e.id));

describe('context menu entries (registry + isCommandAvailable)', () => {
  it('an available file: 열기 only; a missing linked file: 다시 연결… first, then 열기', () => {
    expect(ids(contextMenuEntries('item', COMMANDS, LIB, [target('managed', 'available')], 'darwin'))).toEqual(['item.open']);
    expect(ids(contextMenuEntries('item', COMMANDS, LIB, [target('linked', 'missing')], 'darwin'))).toEqual(['item.relink', '-', 'item.open']);
  });
  it('disables (not hides) the other commands that cannot run, and everything while blocked', () => {
    const two = contextMenuEntries('item', COMMANDS, LIB, [target('managed', 'available'), target('managed', 'available')], 'darwin');
    expect(two).toEqual([{ id: 'item.open', label: '열기', enabled: false }]);
    expect(contextMenuEntries('item', COMMANDS, { ...LIB, blocked: true }, [target('linked', 'missing')], 'darwin')).toEqual([{ id: 'item.open', label: '열기', enabled: false }]);
  });
  it('kinds without commands yet (collection, tag, column) give no entries', () => {
    for (const kind of ['collection', 'tag', 'column'] as const) expect(contextMenuEntries(kind, COMMANDS, LIB, [], 'darwin')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```
`M/menu/context-menu.ts`: `RULES`와 `itemContextEntries`를 지우고 다음으로 바꾼다(`popupCommandMenu`는 `'separator'`를 `{ type: 'separator' }`로 그리게만 고친다).
```ts
import { Menu, type BaseWindow, type MenuItemConstructorOptions } from 'electron';
import { commandLabel, isCommandAvailable, type CommandContextState, type CommandId, type CommandSpec, type CommandTargetInfo, type ContextMenuKind } from '@learn/domain';

export interface ContextEntry {
  id: CommandId;
  label: string;
  enabled: boolean;
}

/**
 * Registry commands for one kind of native context menu, grouped and ordered, enabled by the shared predicate.
 * `onlyWhenAvailable` commands (다시 연결…) are left out instead of shown disabled (design-spec 7.3).
 */
export function contextMenuEntries(
  kind: ContextMenuKind,
  commands: readonly CommandSpec[],
  ctx: CommandContextState,
  targets: readonly CommandTargetInfo[],
  platform: string,
): Array<ContextEntry | 'separator'> {
  const placed = commands
    .filter((c) => c.contextMenu?.kinds.includes(kind))
    .map((c) => ({ c, at: c.contextMenu!, enabled: isCommandAvailable(c.id, ctx, targets) }))
    .filter((e) => e.enabled || !e.at.onlyWhenAvailable)
    .sort((a, b) => a.at.group - b.at.group || a.at.order - b.at.order);
  const out: Array<ContextEntry | 'separator'> = [];
  let group: number | null = null;
  for (const { c, at, enabled } of placed) {
    if (group !== null && at.group !== group) out.push('separator');
    group = at.group;
    out.push({ id: c.id, label: commandLabel(c, platform), enabled });
  }
  return out;
}
```

IPC 단위 테스트의 `IpcDeps` 글자값이 새 필드 때문에 깨지지 않게, 시험용 도우미 하나로 모은다 — `M/ipc/test-deps.ts`(테스트만 import한다; main 진입점에서 닿지 않으므로 번들에 들어가지 않는다):
```ts
import type { IpcDeps } from './register';

/** IpcDeps for IPC unit tests: inert stubs, a test overrides what it exercises. M3 tasks that add IpcDeps fields add their stub here. */
export function stubDeps(over: Partial<IpcDeps> = {}): IpcDeps {
  return {
    getContext: () => {
      throw new Error('getContext not stubbed');
    },
    appInfo: () => {
      throw new Error('appInfo not stubbed');
    },
    prefs: {} as IpcDeps['prefs'],
    uiContext: { report: () => {}, current: () => ({ view: 'library', blocked: false }) },
    ...over,
  };
}
```
`all.test.ts`·`ui.test.ts`·`reader.test.ts`·`broadcast.test.ts`의 `{ getContext: …, appInfo: …, prefs: {} as never }` 글자값을 `stubDeps({ getContext: …, appInfo: () => ({}) as never })`로 바꾼다(각 파일의 `getContext` 본문은 그대로). Task 6(`sheets`)·Task 10(`windows`, `relaunch`, `uiContext.current`의 `textSize: 13`)은 이 파일에 자기 필드의 기본값을 더한다.

`M/ipc/ui.ts`: `showContextMenu` 핸들러를 종류별로 바꾸고 보고 핸들러를 더한다.
```ts
  // Resolves when the native menu closes: the picked command (the renderer runs it with runPicked) or null.
  handle(Channels.ui.showContextMenu, showContextMenuInput, async (input, event) => {
    const window = BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender);
    if (!window) return null;
    if (input.kind !== 'item') return null; // collection/tag/column menus get their commands in M4–M5
    const ctx = deps.getContext();
    let targets: LibraryItem[] = input.itemIds.flatMap((id) => ctx.repo.getItem(id) ?? []);
    if (targets.length === 0) return null;
    if (targets.length === 1) {
      try {
        targets = [await refreshItemAvailability(ctx, targets[0]!.id)];
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn(`[ui] availability check failed: ${toLearnError(e).code}`);
      }
    }
    const entries = contextMenuEntries('item', COMMANDS, deps.uiContext.current(), targets, process.platform);
    if (entries.length === 0) return null;
    const commandId = await popupCommandMenu(entries, { window, x: input.x, y: input.y });
    return commandId ? { commandId } : null;
  });

  handle(Channels.ui.reportContext, commandContextInput, (ctx, event) => {
    deps.uiContext.report(event.sender, ctx);
  });
```
`M/ipc/library.ts`: 대화상자 제목 `'자료 가져오기'` → `'파일 가져오기'`.
`M/index.ts`:
```ts
/** Last command context of the main window (ADR-0005 §2); blocked until the renderer's first report. */
let uiContext: CommandContextState = { view: 'library', blocked: true };
const menuState = globalThis as { __learnMenuStateVersion?: number };

function installAppMenu(): void {
  Menu.setApplicationMenu(
    Menu.buildFromTemplate(
      buildAppMenu({ isDev: isDevBuild, platform: process.platform, commands: COMMANDS, enabled: new Set(availableMenuCommands(uiContext)), onCommand: dispatchMenuCommand }),
    ),
  );
}

/** Menu clicks and accelerators always act on the main window (also when a settings or sheet window is focused). */
function dispatchMenuCommand(commandId: CommandId): void {
  const target = mainWindow;
  if (!target || target.isDestroyed()) return;
  if (!target.isFocused()) target.focus();
  sendEvent(target.webContents, 'menuCommand', { commandId });
}
```

```ts
          installAppMenu();
          menuState.__learnMenuStateVersion = (menuState.__learnMenuStateVersion ?? 0) + 1;

        current: () => uiContext,
      },
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { COMMANDS, type CommandId, type CommandTargetInfo } from '@learn/domain';
import { COMMAND_HANDLERS, contextOf, isCommandEnabled, runCommand, runPicked, type CommandEnv } from './registry';

const ID = '7d3f3c3e-8a51-4f7e-9d7c-2b1f0f6f8f10';
const missingLinked: CommandTargetInfo = { storageMode: 'linked', availability: 'missing' };
const env = (over: Partial<CommandEnv> = {}): CommandEnv => ({
  view: 'library',
  blocked: false,
  targetsOf: (ids) => ids.map(() => missingLinked),
  importFiles: vi.fn(),
  back: vi.fn(),
  open: vi.fn(),
  relink: vi.fn(),
  ...over,
});

describe('renderer command registry', () => {
  it('has a handler for every registry command and nothing else (no fake controls)', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(COMMAND_HANDLERS).sort()).toEqual(COMMANDS.map((c) => c.id).sort());
  });
  it('reports only the context fields, never the handlers', () => {
    expect(contextOf(env({ view: 'viewer' }))).toEqual({ view: 'viewer', blocked: false });
  });
  it('runs through the shared availability predicate', () => {
    const lib = env();
    expect(runCommand('file.importManaged', lib)).toBe(true);
    expect(runCommand('file.importLinked', lib)).toBe(true);
    expect(lib.importFiles).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'managed');
    expect(lib.importFiles).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, 'linked');
    expect(runCommand('nav.back', lib)).toBe(false);
    const viewer = env({ view: 'viewer' });
    expect(runCommand('nav.back', viewer)).toBe(true);
    expect(viewer.back).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it('item commands use the targets of the given items', () => {
    const e = env();
    expect(isCommandEnabled('item.relink', e, { itemIds: [ID] })).toBe(true);
    expect(isCommandEnabled('item.relink', env({ targetsOf: () => [{ storageMode: 'managed', availability: 'available' }] }), { itemIds: [ID] })).toBe(false);
    expect(runCommand('item.open', e, { itemIds: [ID] })).toBe(true);
    expect(e.open).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID);
  });
  it('runs a context-menu pick (main already judged it) unless the window is blocked', () => {
    const e = env({ targetsOf: () => [] });
    expect(runPicked('item.relink', e, [ID])).toBe(true);
    expect(e.relink).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ID);
    expect(runPicked('item.open', env({ blocked: true }), [ID])).toBe(false);
  });
  it('ignores unknown ids', () => {
    expect(runCommand('file.nope' as CommandId, env())).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`R/commands/registry.ts` 전체:
```ts
import { COMMANDS, isCommandAvailable, type CommandContextState, type CommandId, type CommandTargetInfo } from '@learn/domain/commands';
import type { StorageMode } from '@learn/domain';

/** What the main window can do now: its command context plus the handlers. Rebuilt every render; later tasks add handlers. */
export interface CommandEnv extends CommandContextState {
  /** Availability facts for item targets (from the library list). */
  targetsOf(itemIds: readonly string[]): CommandTargetInfo[];
  importFiles(mode: StorageMode): void;
  back(): void;
  open(itemId: string): void;
  relink(itemId: string): void;
}

export interface CommandTarget {
  itemIds?: readonly string[];
}

type Handler = (env: CommandEnv, itemIds: readonly string[]) => void;

/** Renderer side of the registry: exactly one handler per command id (menus can only show handled commands). */
export const COMMAND_HANDLERS: Record<CommandId, Handler> = {
  'file.importManaged': (env) => env.importFiles('managed'),
  'file.importLinked': (env) => env.importFiles('linked'),
  'nav.back': (env) => env.back(),
  'item.open': (env, [itemId]) => env.open(itemId!),
  'item.relink': (env, [itemId]) => env.relink(itemId!),
};

/** Just the context fields (what main needs for the menu bar). */
export function contextOf(env: CommandEnv): CommandContextState {
  return { view: env.view, blocked: env.blocked };
}

export function isCommandEnabled(id: CommandId, env: CommandEnv, target: CommandTarget = {}): boolean {
  if (!Object.hasOwn(COMMAND_HANDLERS, id)) return false;
  const itemIds = target.itemIds ?? [];
  return isCommandAvailable(id, contextOf(env), env.targetsOf(itemIds));
}

/** Runs a command if it is available now; returns whether it ran. */
export function runCommand(id: CommandId, env: CommandEnv, target: CommandTarget = {}): boolean {
  if (!isCommandEnabled(id, env, target)) return false;
  COMMAND_HANDLERS[id](env, target.itemIds ?? []);
  return true;
}

/**
 * A command picked from a native context menu: main enabled it with fresh file state (the renderer's list may lag
 * one libraryChanged behind), so only the window-level block is checked here.
 */
export function runPicked(id: CommandId, env: CommandEnv, itemIds: readonly string[]): boolean {
  if (!Object.hasOwn(COMMAND_HANDLERS, id) || env.blocked || !COMMANDS.some((c) => c.id === id)) return false;
  COMMAND_HANDLERS[id](env, itemIds);
  return true;
}
```
`R/commands/useReportContext.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { CommandContextState } from '@learn/domain';
import { api } from '../api';
import { contextOf, type CommandEnv } from './registry';

/** Tells main the window's command context whenever it changes (ADR-0005 §2): main enables the menu bar from it. */
export function useReportContext(env: CommandEnv): void {
  const json = JSON.stringify(contextOf(env));
  useEffect(() => {
    void api().ui.reportContext(JSON.parse(json) as CommandContextState);
  }, [json]);
}
```
`R/App.tsx`(M2 판): `commandEnv`에 `targetsOf: (ids) => ids.flatMap((id) => items.find((i) => i.id === id) ?? []),`를 더하고, `useMenuCommands(commandEnv);` 아래에 `useReportContext(commandEnv);`, 컨텍스트 메뉴 결과 실행 `runCommand(picked.commandId, commandEnvRef.current, { itemIds: [itemId] })`을 `runPicked(picked.commandId, commandEnvRef.current, [itemId])`로 바꾼다. M2 App은 훅을 조건부 return보다 먼저 부르고 `blocked`가 `!info || libraryError …`이므로 FatalScreen 상태에서도 `{ blocked: true }`가 보고된다.

2. `runMenuCommand` 본문 앞에 기다림을 넣고 새 도우미를 더한다.
```ts
export async function runMenuCommand(app: ElectronApplication, id: CommandId): Promise<void> {
  await expect.poll(() => menuItemEnabled(app, id), { timeout: 5_000, message: `menu item ${id} never became clickable` }).toBe(true);
  await app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow, Menu, webContents }, id) => {
    const item = Menu.getApplicationMenu()?.getMenuItemById(id);
    if (!item) throw new Error(`no application menu item with id ${id}`);
    if (!item.enabled || !item.visible) throw new Error(`menu item ${id} is not clickable`);
    item.click(undefined, BrowserWindow.getFocusedWindow() ?? undefined, webContents.getFocusedWebContents() ?? undefined);
  }, id);
}

/** Whether the application-menu item exists, is visible and is enabled right now. */
export async function menuItemEnabled(app: ElectronApplication, id: CommandId): Promise<boolean> {
  return app.evaluate(({ Menu }, id) => {
    const item = Menu.getApplicationMenu()?.getMenuItemById(id);
    return Boolean(item && item.enabled && item.visible);
  }, id);
}
```

- "1. 레지스트리"의 `CommandSpec` 코드 블록을 Step 4의 실제 인터페이스(`contexts: CommandContext[]`, `labelOff`, `key: KeySpec`, `menu`, `contextMenu: { kinds, group, order, onlyWhenAvailable }`, `palette`, `keywords`)로 바꾸고, 그 아래 첫 항목을 "가용성은 `isCommandAvailable(id, ctx, targets)` 하나다. main(메뉴 막대·컨텍스트 메뉴)과 렌더러(팔레트·도구 막대·키맵)가 같은 함수를 쓴다. 렌더러는 핸들러 표(`COMMAND_HANDLERS`)만 갖는다."로 바꾼다.
- "2. 메뉴가 소유하는 단축키": 메뉴 이름 목록을 "Learn · 파일 · 편집 · 보기 · 이동 · 윈도우 · 도움말(macOS 한국어 표준 이름 '윈도우')"로, `ui:context` 문장을 "렌더러는 `CommandContextState`(`view`, `blocked`, M3 Task 10부터 `textSize`)를 바뀔 때마다 `ui:reportContext`로 보내고, main은 `availableMenuCommands(ctx)`로 메뉴를 다시 만든다. 첫 보고 전과 메인 창 밖에서 온 보고에는 레지스트리 항목이 모두 비활성이다. `MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS`(설정)는 항상 활성."으로.
- "4. 컨텍스트 메뉴": "선택한 명령은 `ui:showContextMenu` invoke의 **반환값**(`{ commandId } | null`)으로 돌아오고, 렌더러는 `runPicked`로 실행한다(main이 새 파일 상태로 이미 판정). 종류는 `item | collection | tag | column`(M3는 item만 항목이 있다). `onlyWhenAvailable` 명령(다시 연결…)은 쓸 수 없으면 빠진다."로.
- 단축키 표기: "⌘[/⌘]는 PDF 이동 기록(M7)이므로 M3의 라이브러리로 돌아가기는 ⌘1(첫 탭)."을 "2." 끝에 더한다.

- [ ] **Step 2: 구현**

`M/window-url.ts`:
```ts
export type WindowKind = 'main' | 'settings' | 'sheet';

export interface WindowUrlOptions {
  platform: NodeJS.Platform;
  /** LEARN_DETERMINISTIC=1: the renderer disables motion and the caret for screenshots. */
  deterministic: boolean;
  /** macOS sidebar vibrancy is on: the main window's page keeps its sidebar transparent. */
  vibrancy: boolean;
  /** LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY=1 in an unpackaged build: key and pointer overlay for demo recordings. */
  demo: boolean;
}

/** The renderer reads these flags synchronously before its first paint (ui/platform.ts). */
export function windowUrl(base: string, kind: WindowKind, o: WindowUrlOptions): string {
  const url = new URL(base);
  url.searchParams.set('window', kind);
  url.searchParams.set('platform', o.platform);
  if (o.deterministic) url.searchParams.set('det', '1');
  if (kind === 'main' && o.vibrancy) url.searchParams.set('vibrancy', '1');
  if (kind === 'main' && o.demo) url.searchParams.set('demo', '1');
  return url.toString();
}
```
`M/deterministic.ts`:
```ts
/** Chromium switches that make screenshots repeatable (visual tour, evidence page shots). */
export const DETERMINISTIC_SWITCHES: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string?]> = [
  ['force-color-profile', 'srgb'],
  ['disable-lcd-text'],
  ['disable-gpu-compositing'],
  ['font-render-hinting', 'none'],
];

export const isDeterministic = (env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): boolean => env['LEARN_DETERMINISTIC'] === '1';
```
`M/index.ts` — import 둘을 더하고, `registerAppScheme();` 바로 위에:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readWindowParams } from './platform';

describe('readWindowParams', () => {
  it('reads the flags main put on the URL', () => {
    expect(readWindowParams('?window=settings&platform=darwin&det=1')).toEqual({ window: 'settings', platform: 'darwin', deterministic: true, vibrancy: false, demo: false, part: 1 });
    expect(readWindowParams('?window=gallery&part=2&platform=linux')).toMatchObject({ window: 'gallery', part: 2 });
  });
  it('falls back to the main window on Linux', () => {
    expect(readWindowParams('')).toEqual({ window: 'main', platform: 'linux', deterministic: false, vibrancy: false, demo: false, part: 1 });
    expect(readWindowParams('?window=evil')).toMatchObject({ window: 'main' });
  });
});
```

`R/ui/platform.ts`:
```ts
export type RendererWindow = 'main' | 'settings' | 'sheet' | 'gallery';
export interface WindowParams {
  window: RendererWindow;
  platform: string;
  deterministic: boolean;
  vibrancy: boolean;
  demo: boolean;
  /** Gallery page (component board part 1 or 2). */
  part: number;
}
const KINDS: readonly RendererWindow[] = ['main', 'settings', 'sheet', 'gallery'];

export function readWindowParams(search: string): WindowParams {
  const p = new URLSearchParams(search);
  const w = p.get('window') as RendererWindow | null;
  return {
    window: w && KINDS.includes(w) ? w : 'main',
    platform: p.get('platform') ?? 'linux',
    deterministic: p.get('det') === '1',
    vibrancy: p.get('vibrancy') === '1',
    demo: p.get('demo') === '1',
    part: p.get('part') === '2' ? 2 : 1,
  };
}

/** Set on <html data-platform> by main.tsx before the first render. */
export const currentPlatform = (): string => document.documentElement.dataset['platform'] ?? 'linux';
```

`R/ui/Icon.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from './cx';

export type IconSize = 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl';
// design-spec 5: 16 / stroke 1.5 (1 px at 16), field icons 14, disclosure 12 at stroke 2, media 20, empty states 32 at 1.25.
const PX: Record<IconSize, number> = { xs: 12, sm: 14, md: 16, lg: 20, xl: 32 };
const STROKE: Record<IconSize, number> = { xs: 2, sm: 1.5, md: 1.5, lg: 1.5, xl: 1.25 };

export function Icon({ icon: Glyph, size = 'md', className, label }: { icon: LucideIcon; size?: IconSize; className?: string; label?: string }) {
  return (
    <Glyph
      size={PX[size]}
      strokeWidth={STROKE[size]}
      className={cx('icon', className)}
      aria-hidden={label ? undefined : true}
      aria-label={label}
      role={label ? 'img' : undefined}
      focusable="false"
    />
  );
}
```

`R/ui/Tooltip.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { Tooltip as AriaTooltip, TooltipTrigger } from 'react-aria-components';
import { acceleratorLabel } from '@learn/domain';
import { currentPlatform } from './platform';

/** Name + shortcut tooltip for icon-only buttons (design-spec 5: "찾기 ⌘F"). Shown on hover and keyboard focus. */
export function Tooltip({ label, accelerator, children }: { label: string; accelerator?: string; children: ReactElement }) {
  return (
    <TooltipTrigger delay={600} closeDelay={0}>
      {children}
      <AriaTooltip className="tooltip" placement="bottom" offset={6}>
        {label}
        {accelerator && <span className="tooltip-shortcut">{acceleratorLabel(accelerator, currentPlatform())}</span>}
      </AriaTooltip>
    </TooltipTrigger>
  );
}
```

`R/ui/Button.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Button as AriaButton, type ButtonProps as AriaButtonProps } from 'react-aria-components';
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { Tooltip } from './Tooltip';

/** Gallery only: draw a state without interacting (CSS reads [data-force~=…] next to RAC's data attributes). */
export type ForcedState = 'hover' | 'pressed' | 'focus' | 'on' | 'active' | 'selected' | 'focused' | 'cursor';
export type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'plain' | 'plain-accent' | 'plain-danger';

type Base = Omit<AriaButtonProps, 'className' | 'children' | 'style'>;

export interface ButtonProps extends Base {
  children: ReactNode;
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: 'small' | 'regular' | 'large';
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  /** Plain button whose label lines up with the text column (sheet footers, design-spec 6 버튼). */
  leading?: boolean;
  testId?: string;
  force?: ForcedState[];
}

export function Button({ children, variant = 'secondary', size = 'regular', icon, leading, testId, force, ...rest }: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <AriaButton
      {...rest}
      className={cx('btn', `btn-${variant}`, size !== 'regular' && `btn-${size}`, icon && 'has-icon', leading && 'leading')}
      data-testid={testId}
      data-force={force?.join(' ')}
    >
      {icon && <Icon icon={icon} size={size === 'small' ? 'sm' : 'md'} />}
      {children}
    </AriaButton>
  );
}

export interface IconButtonProps extends Omit<Base, 'aria-label'> {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  /** Accessible name and tooltip text. */
  label: string;
  /** Electron accelerator from the command registry, shown in the tooltip. */
  accelerator?: string;
  /** Toggle drawn "on" (--fill-pressed + primary text). */
  isOn?: boolean;
  size?: 'regular' | 'small';
  testId?: string;
  force?: ForcedState[];
}

export function IconButton({ icon, label, accelerator, isOn, size = 'regular', testId, force, ...rest }: IconButtonProps) {
  return (
    <Tooltip label={label} accelerator={accelerator}>
      <AriaButton
        {...rest}
        aria-label={label}
        className={cx('icon-btn', size === 'small' && 'sm')}
        data-on={isOn ? '' : undefined}
        data-testid={testId}
        data-force={force?.join(' ')}
      >
        <Icon icon={icon} size={size === 'small' ? 'sm' : 'md'} />
      </AriaButton>
    </Tooltip>
  );
}
```

`R/ui/Kbd.tsx`:
```tsx
import { acceleratorKeys } from '@learn/domain';
import { currentPlatform } from './platform';

/** Keycaps (design-spec 6 키캡). Decorative: the surrounding label names the shortcut for screen readers. */
export function Kbd({ accelerator, keys }: { accelerator?: string; keys?: readonly string[] }) {
  const list = keys ?? (accelerator ? acceleratorKeys(accelerator, currentPlatform()) : []);
  return (
    <span className="kbd-group" aria-hidden="true">
      {list.map((k, i) => (
        <kbd className="kbd" key={`${k}-${i}`}>
          {k}
        </kbd>
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`R/ui/MenuButton.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChevronDown, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button as AriaButton, Menu, MenuItem, MenuTrigger, Popover } from 'react-aria-components';
import { acceleratorLabel } from '@learn/domain';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { currentPlatform } from './platform';

export interface MenuButtonItem<K extends string> {
  id: K;
  label: string;
  accelerator?: string;
  testId?: string;
  isDisabled?: boolean;
}

/** Web pop-up button menu: only for buttons inside the content (design-spec 6 메뉴, 7.3). */
export function MenuButton<K extends string>(props: {
  label: string;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  items: readonly MenuButtonItem<K>[];
  onAction(id: K): void;
  isDisabled?: boolean;
  testId?: string;
}) {
  const platform = currentPlatform();
  return (
    <MenuTrigger>
      <AriaButton className={cx('btn', 'btn-secondary', props.icon && 'has-icon', 'has-menu')} isDisabled={props.isDisabled} data-testid={props.testId}>
        {props.icon && <Icon icon={props.icon} />}
        {props.label}
        <Icon icon={ChevronDown} size="xs" />
      </AriaButton>
      <Popover className="menu-popover" placement="bottom start" offset={4}>
        <Menu className="menu" onAction={(key) => props.onAction(String(key) as K)} disabledKeys={props.items.filter((i) => i.isDisabled).map((i) => i.id)}>
          {props.items.map((item) => (
            <MenuItem key={item.id} id={item.id} textValue={item.label} className="mi" data-testid={item.testId}>
              <span className="check" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span className="mi-label">{item.label}</span>
              {item.accelerator && <span className="shortcut">{acceleratorLabel(item.accelerator, platform)}</span>}
            </MenuItem>
          ))}
        </Menu>
      </Popover>
    </MenuTrigger>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Radio, RadioGroup } from 'react-aria-components';
import type { ForcedState } from './Button';
import { cx } from './cx';

export interface SegmentOption<V extends string> {
  value: V;
  label: string;
  testId?: string;
}

/** Segmented control = radio group (design-spec 6 세그먼트). */
export function Segmented<V extends string>(props: {
  value: V;
  options: readonly SegmentOption<V>[];
  onChange(value: V): void;
  fill?: boolean;
  isDisabled?: boolean;
  force?: ForcedState[];
  'aria-label'?: string;
  'aria-labelledby'?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <RadioGroup
      aria-label={props['aria-label']}
      aria-labelledby={props['aria-labelledby']}
      value={props.value}
      onChange={(v) => props.onChange(v as V)}
      orientation="horizontal"
      isDisabled={props.isDisabled}
      className={cx('segmented', props.fill && 'fill')}
      data-force={props.force?.join(' ')}
    >
      {props.options.map((o) => (
        <Radio key={o.value} value={o.value} className="seg" data-testid={o.testId}>
          {o.label}
        </Radio>
      ))}
    </RadioGroup>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Icon } from './Icon';

export type NoticeTone = 'neutral' | 'warning' | 'accent';

/**
 * Stage notice (design-spec 6): 56 px tile — neutral = error/unavailable, warning = user-fixable,
 * accent = invitation; title2 heading, paragraph, optional file line, one row of actions.
 */
export function Notice(props: {
  tone?: NoticeTone;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  title: string;
  children?: ReactNode;
  file?: string;
  fileTitle?: string;
  actions?: ReactNode;
  testId?: string;
}) {
  const tone = props.tone ?? 'neutral';
  return (
    <div className="notice" role="alert" data-testid={props.testId}>
      <div className={cx('glyph', tone !== 'neutral' && tone)}>
        <Icon icon={props.icon} size="xl" />
      </div>
      <h2 className="ui-balance">{props.title}</h2>
      {props.children && <p className="ui-balance">{props.children}</p>}
      {props.file && (
        <div className="file mono ellipsis" title={props.fileTitle ?? props.file}>
          {props.file}
        </div>
      )}
      {props.actions && <div className="actions">{props.actions}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`R/ui/toasts.tsx`:
```tsx
import { CircleAlert, CircleCheck, Info } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  Button as AriaButton,
  Text,
  UNSTABLE_Toast as Toast,
  UNSTABLE_ToastContent as ToastContent,
  UNSTABLE_ToastQueue as ToastQueue,
  UNSTABLE_ToastRegion as ToastRegion,
} from 'react-aria-components';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Icon } from './Icon';

export type ToastKind = 'success' | 'error' | 'info';
export interface ToastData {
  kind: ToastKind;
  title: string;
  body?: string;
  testId?: string;
}

/**
 * One queue per window. design-spec 6 토스트: success closes after 5 s, errors stay until closed.
 * RAC's toast API is UNSTABLE_-prefixed in 1.22.1; it is pinned and used only through this module.
 */
export const toastQueue = new ToastQueue<ToastData>({ maxVisibleToasts: 3 });

export function showToast(data: ToastData): string {
  return toastQueue.add(data, data.kind === 'success' ? { timeout: 5000 } : {});
}

export const TOAST_ICON = { success: CircleCheck, error: CircleAlert, info: Info } as const;
export const TOAST_TONE = { success: 'c-success', error: 'c-danger', info: 'c-secondary' } as const;

export function Toasts() {
  return (
    <ToastRegion queue={toastQueue} className="toasts" aria-label={t.common.notifications}>
      {({ toast }) => (
        <Toast toast={toast} className="toast" data-kind={toast.content.kind} data-testid={toast.content.testId ?? 'toast'}>
          <span className={`t-icon ${TOAST_TONE[toast.content.kind]}`}>
            <Icon icon={TOAST_ICON[toast.content.kind]} size="lg" />
          </span>
          <ToastContent className="t-text">
            <Text slot="title" className="t-title">
              {toast.content.title}
            </Text>
            {toast.content.body && (
              <Text slot="description" className="t-body">
                {toast.content.body}
              </Text>
            )}
          </ToastContent>
          <AriaButton slot="close" className="btn btn-secondary btn-small" data-testid="toast-close">
            {t.common.close}
          </AriaButton>
        </Toast>
      )}
    </ToastRegion>
  );
}
```

`R/strings/strings.test.ts`:
```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { COMMANDS } from '@learn/domain';
import { t } from './index';

/** Every string reachable from `t` (functions are called with sample arguments), plus the command labels. */
function collect(node: unknown, at: string, out: Array<{ at: string; text: string }>): void {
  if (typeof node === 'string') out.push({ at, text: node });
  else if (typeof node === 'function') {
    const fn = node as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown;
    collect(fn(...Array.from({ length: fn.length }, () => 3)), `${at}()`, out);
  } else if (Array.isArray(node)) node.forEach((v, i) => collect(v, `${at}[${i}]`, out));
  else if (node && typeof node === 'object') for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(node)) collect(v, `${at}.${k}`, out);
}
const copy: Array<{ at: string; text: string }> = [];
collect(t, 't', copy);
for (const c of COMMANDS) copy.push({ at: `command ${c.id}`, text: c.label });

// Same rules as the mockups' copyLint (docs/design/mockups/assets/mockup.js).
const DETERMINER = /(?:^|[\s(‘“])(이|그|저|새|다른|각|모든|어느) (?=[가-힣])/;
const COMPOUNDS = ['쪽 번호', '디스크 공간', '필기와 태그', '지운 것', '새 위치', 'G 단백질', '새 컬렉션', '어느 쪽'];
const INTERNAL = /SHA-256|해시|staging|관리형|경로만 기록/;
const show = (s: { at: string; text: string }) => `${s.at}: ${s.text}`;

describe('UI copy (design-spec 4, 9)', () => {
  it('binds determiner + noun and the listed compounds with U+00A0', () => {
    expect(copy.filter((s) => DETERMINER.test(s.text) || COMPOUNDS.some((c) => s.text.includes(c))).map(show)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('uses 합니다체, never 해요체', () => {
    expect(copy.filter((s) => /요[.!?]?(\s|$)/.test(s.text) && !s.text.includes('필요')).map(show)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('has no internal jargon', () => {
    expect(copy.filter((s) => INTERNAL.test(s.text)).map(show)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('no renderer source shows a phase placeholder such as "P3 단계"', () => {
    const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
    const files: string[] = [];
    const walk = (d: string) => {
      for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
        const p = path.join(d, e.name);
        if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p);
        // strings/legacy.ts belongs to the pre-M3 screens and is deleted in Task 7.
        else if (/\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(e.name) && !e.name.endsWith('.test.ts') && e.name !== 'legacy.ts') files.push(p);
      }
    };
    walk(root);
    expect(files.filter((f) => /P[0-9]\s*단계/.test(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'))).map((f) => path.relative(root, f))).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```
Run: `pnpm vitest run apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/strings/strings.test.ts` → PASS(아직 문구가 적다. 이후 작업이 문구를 더할 때마다 이 검사가 지킨다). 단, `TutorPanel.tsx`·`Sidebar.tsx`는 `t.tutorPending` 등 legacy 문구만 참조하므로 소스 검사에 걸리지 않는다.
