`R/styles/components/button.css`:
```css
@layer components {
  .btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: var(--space-6);
    height: var(--control-height);
    padding: 0 var(--space-12);
    border: 0;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: var(--bg-control);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e0);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font: var(--type-body);
    white-space: nowrap;
    flex: none;
    cursor: default;
    -webkit-app-region: no-drag;
  }
  .btn .icon {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .btn.has-icon {
    padding-left: var(--space-8);
  }
  .btn.has-menu {
    padding-right: var(--space-8);
  }
  .btn:is([data-hovered], [data-force~='hover']) {
    background-image: linear-gradient(var(--fill-hover), var(--fill-hover));
  }
  .btn:is([data-pressed], [data-force~='pressed']) {
    background-image: linear-gradient(var(--fill-pressed), var(--fill-pressed));
  }
  .btn:is([data-focus-visible], [data-force~='focus']) {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: var(--focus-ring-offset);
  }
  .btn[data-disabled] {
    opacity: 0.45;
  }
  .btn-primary {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
    box-shadow: none;
    font-weight: 500;
  }
  .btn-primary .icon {
    color: inherit;
  }
  .btn-primary:is([data-hovered], [data-force~='hover']) {
    background: var(--accent-hover);
  }
  .btn-primary:is([data-pressed], [data-force~='pressed']) {
    background: var(--accent-pressed);
  }
  .btn:is(.btn-plain, .btn-plain-accent, .btn-plain-danger) {
    background: transparent;
    box-shadow: none;
  }
  .btn:is(.btn-plain, .btn-plain-accent, .btn-plain-danger):is([data-hovered], [data-force~='hover']) {
    background: var(--fill-hover);
  }
  .btn:is(.btn-plain, .btn-plain-accent, .btn-plain-danger):is([data-pressed], [data-force~='pressed']) {
    background: var(--fill-pressed);
  }
  .btn-plain-accent,
  .btn-plain-accent .icon {
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }
  .btn-plain-danger,
  .btn-plain-danger .icon {
    color: var(--danger-text);
  }
  .btn.leading {
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    margin-left: calc(var(--space-8) * -1);
  }
  .btn-small {
    height: var(--control-height-small);
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  }
  .btn-large {
    height: var(--control-height-large);
    padding: 0 var(--space-16);
  }
  .icon-btn {
    display: inline-grid;
    place-items: center;
    width: var(--hit-target);
    height: var(--hit-target);
    padding: 0;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    flex: none;
    -webkit-app-region: no-drag;
  }
  .icon-btn:is([data-hovered], [data-force~='hover']) {
    background: var(--fill-hover);
  }
  .icon-btn:is([data-pressed], [data-force~='pressed']) {
    background: var(--fill-pressed);
  }
  .icon-btn:is([data-on], [data-force~='on']) {
    background: var(--fill-pressed);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
  .icon-btn:is([data-focus-visible], [data-force~='focus']) {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: calc(var(--focus-ring-width) * -1);
  }
  .icon-btn[data-disabled] {
    opacity: 0.4;
  }
  .icon-btn.sm {
    width: 22px; /* local: inline buttons inside fields and rows */
    height: 22px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  }
}
```

```css
  .field {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-6);
    height: var(--control-height);
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    border: 0;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: var(--bg-field);
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--border);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font: var(--type-body);
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .field .icon,
  .field .placeholder {
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
  }
  .field:is([data-focus-visible], [data-focus-within], [data-force~='focus']) {
    outline: none;
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--focus), 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in oklch, var(--focus) 28%, transparent);
  }
  /* ⌘K launcher: a field-shaped button (design-spec 6 검색 필드·실행기). */
  .launcher {
    text-align: left;
    cursor: default;
    -webkit-app-region: no-drag;
  }
  .launcher:is([data-hovered], [data-force~='hover']) {
    background-image: linear-gradient(var(--fill-hover), var(--fill-hover));
  }
  .launcher:is([data-pressed], [data-force~='pressed']) {
    background-image: linear-gradient(var(--fill-pressed), var(--fill-pressed));
  }
  .launcher .kbd-group {
    margin-left: auto;
  }
}
```

`R/styles/components/menu.css`:
```css
@layer components {
  .menu-popover {
    outline: none;
  }
  .menu-popover[data-entering] {
    animation: pop-in var(--duration-base) var(--ease-standard);
  }
  .menu-popover[data-exiting] {
    animation: fade-in calc(var(--duration-base) * 0.75) var(--ease-standard) reverse;
  }
  .menu {
    min-width: 200px;
    padding: 5px; /* local: macOS menu inset */
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    background: var(--bg-raised);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e1);
    font: var(--type-body);
    outline: none;
  }
  .menu .mi {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    height: 24px; /* local: macOS menu row */
    padding: 0 var(--space-8) 0 var(--space-4);
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    white-space: nowrap;
    outline: none;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .menu .mi .check {
    width: 16px;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    flex: none;
  }
  .menu .mi .shortcut {
    margin-left: auto;
    padding-left: var(--space-24);
  }
  .menu .mi:is([data-focused], [data-force~='active']) {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  .menu .mi:is([data-focused], [data-force~='active']) :is(.shortcut, .check) {
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  .menu .mi[data-disabled] {
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
  }
}
```

`R/styles/components/segmented.css`:
```css
@layer components {
  .segmented {
    display: inline-flex;
    padding: 2px;
    gap: 2px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: var(--bg-track);
    flex: none;
  }
  .segmented.fill {
    display: flex;
  }
  .segmented .seg {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    height: 24px; /* local: 28 control − 2×2 track */
    padding: 0 var(--space-12);
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    font-weight: 500;
    white-space: nowrap;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .segmented.fill .seg {
    flex: 1 1 0;
    padding: 0 var(--space-4);
  }
  .segmented .seg:is([data-selected], [data-force~='on']) {
    background: var(--bg-control-selected);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e0);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
  .segmented .seg[data-focus-visible],
  .segmented[data-force~='focus'] .seg[data-selected] {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: 0;
  }
  .segmented[data-disabled] {
    opacity: 0.45;
  }
}
```

`R/styles/components/toast.css`:
```css
@layer components {
  .toasts {
    position: fixed;
    z-index: 45;
    right: var(--space-16);
    bottom: var(--space-16);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    width: 360px;
    outline: none;
  }
  .toast {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 20px minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    column-gap: var(--space-12);
    align-items: start;
    padding: var(--space-12) var(--space-12) var(--space-12) var(--space-16);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    background: var(--bg-raised);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e1);
    outline: none;
    animation: rise-in var(--duration-base) var(--ease-standard);
  }
  .toast[data-focus-visible] {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: var(--focus-ring-offset);
  }
  .toast .t-icon {
    display: grid;
    margin-top: 1px;
  }
  .toast .t-text {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .toast .t-title {
    font: var(--type-headline);
  }
  .toast .t-body {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    white-space: pre-line;
  }
  .spinner {
    display: inline-block;
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    border: 2px solid var(--bg-track);
    border-top-color: var(--meter);
    border-right-color: var(--meter);
    flex: none;
    animation: spin 900ms linear infinite;
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .spinner {
      animation: none;
    }
  }
  @keyframes spin {
    to {
      rotate: 1turn;
    }
  }
}
```

`R/styles/components/list.css` (사이드바 행·표 행 — M5 트리, M4 표도 같은 치수):
```css
@layer components {
  .section-label {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    height: 24px; /* local: section header row */
    padding: 0 var(--space-8) 0 var(--space-16);
    margin-top: var(--space-12);
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
    font: var(--type-caption);
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .section-label:first-child {
    margin-top: 0;
  }
  .nav-list {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    outline: none;
  }
  .nav-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-6);
    height: var(--row-height);
    padding: 0 var(--space-8) 0 calc(var(--space-16) + var(--indent, 0) * 16px);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    min-width: 0;
    outline: none;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .nav-row > .icon {
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }
  .nav-row .label {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
  }
  .nav-row .count {
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .nav-row:is([data-hovered], [data-force~='hover']) {
    background: var(--fill-hover);
  }
  /* Selection: neutral without keyboard focus, accent fill while the list has focus (design-spec 3.1). */
  .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected], [data-force~='selected']) {
    background: var(--fill-selected);
  }
  .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected], [data-force~='selected']) .count {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .nav-list:focus-within .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected]),
  .nav-row[data-force~='focused'] {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  .nav-list:focus-within .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected]) :is(.icon, .count),
  .nav-row[data-force~='focused'] :is(.icon, .count) {
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  :root[data-window-inactive] .nav-list:focus-within .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected]) {
    background: var(--fill-selected);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
  :root[data-window-inactive] .nav-list:focus-within .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected]) > .icon {
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }
  :root[data-window-inactive] .nav-list:focus-within .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected]) .count {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  /* Keyboard cursor: inset 2 px ring; white 75 % on an accent-filled row. */
  .nav-row:is([data-focus-visible], [data-force~='cursor']) {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--focus);
  }
  .nav-list:focus-within .nav-row:is([data-current], [data-selected])[data-focus-visible],
  .nav-row[data-force~='focused'][data-force~='cursor'] {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px color-mix(in oklch, var(--text-on-accent) 75%, transparent);
  }

  .table-head,
  .table-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: var(--cols);
    align-items: center;
  }
  .table-head {
    height: var(--table-header-height);
    flex: none;
    padding: 0 var(--space-16);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--separator);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
  .table-head .th {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    height: 100%;
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .table-head .th:first-child {
    padding-left: 0;
  }
  .table-head .th + .th::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 6px; /* local: 16 px divider centred in the 28 px header */
    bottom: 6px;
    width: 1px;
    background: var(--separator);
  }
  .table-body {
    padding-top: var(--space-4);
  }
  .table-row {
    height: var(--row-height);
    margin: 0 var(--space-8);
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  }
  .table-row:nth-child(even) {
    background: var(--bg-content-alt);
  }
  .table-row:is(:hover, [data-force~='hover']) {
    background: var(--fill-hover);
  }
  .table-row:is(:has(.row-open:focus-visible), [data-force~='cursor']) {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--focus);
  }
  .table-row .td {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    height: 100%;
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    min-width: 0;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .table-row .td:first-child {
    padding-left: 0;
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
  .table-row .td > .icon {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .table-row .td.end,
  .table-head .th.end {
    justify-content: flex-end;
    text-align: right;
  }
  .table-row[data-missing] .row-open {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .row-open {
    all: unset;
    min-width: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .inline-flag {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    flex: none;
    white-space: nowrap;
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
}
```
(목업의 표 머리 구분선은 `top: 7px; bottom: 7px`(14px)이지만 명세 6절 "열 사이 16px 높이 구분선"에 맞춰 6/6(16px)으로 한다 — 명세 우선.)

```css
@layer screens {
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 상태 보드** `R/gallery/Gallery.tsx` (`components.html` 1·3부에 대응. 보드의 견본 문구는 화면에 나가지 않으므로 `strings/` 밖에 둔다)

```tsx
import { Copy, FileText, FolderSearch, LibraryBig, Link, Link2Off, PanelLeft, Search, TriangleAlert, VideoOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { Menu, MenuItem } from 'react-aria-components';
import { Button, IconButton, type ButtonVariant, type ForcedState } from '../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { Kbd } from '../ui/Kbd';
import { Notice } from '../ui/Notice';
import { readWindowParams } from '../ui/platform';
import { Segmented } from '../ui/Segmented';
import { Spinner } from '../ui/Spinner';
import { TOAST_ICON, TOAST_TONE, type ToastData } from '../ui/toasts';

const STATES: Array<{ name: string; force?: ForcedState[]; disabled?: boolean }> = [
  { name: '기본' },
  { name: '호버', force: ['hover'] },
  { name: '누름', force: ['pressed'] },
  { name: '포커스', force: ['focus'] },
  { name: '비활성', disabled: true },
];
const VARIANTS: ButtonVariant[] = ['primary', 'secondary', 'plain', 'plain-accent', 'plain-danger'];
const noop = () => {};

function StaticToast({ data }: { data: ToastData }) {
  return (
    <div className="toast g-static" data-kind={data.kind}>
      <span className={`t-icon ${TOAST_TONE[data.kind]}`}>
        <Icon icon={TOAST_ICON[data.kind]} size="lg" />
      </span>
      <div className="t-text">
        <div className="t-title">{data.title}</div>
        {data.body && <div className="t-body">{data.body}</div>}
      </div>
      <span className="btn btn-secondary btn-small">닫기</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function Part1() {
  return (
    <>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">버튼</h2>
        {VARIANTS.map((v) => (
          <div className="g-row" key={v}>
            <span className="g-label">{v}</span>
            {STATES.map((s) => (
              <Button key={s.name} variant={v} force={s.force} isDisabled={s.disabled}>
                {v === 'primary' ? '가져오기' : s.name}
              </Button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="g-row">
          <span className="g-label">크기·아이콘</span>
          <Button size="small">완료</Button>
          <Button size="large" variant="primary">파일 가져오기…</Button>
          <Button icon={FolderSearch}>Finder에서 보기</Button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">아이콘 단추</h2>
        <div className="g-row">
          {[...STATES, { name: '켜짐', force: ['on'] as ForcedState[] }].map((s) => (
            <IconButton key={s.name} icon={PanelLeft} label={`사이드바 ${s.name}`} force={s.force} isDisabled={s.disabled} />
          ))}
        </div>
        <h2 className="g-title">필드·실행기·키캡</h2>
        <div className="g-row">
          <span className="field launcher g-field">
            <Icon icon={Search} size="sm" />
            <span className="placeholder">검색 및 명령</span>
            <Kbd accelerator="CmdOrCtrl+K" />
          </span>
          <span className="field g-field" data-force="focus">
            <Icon icon={Search} size="sm" />
            <span>신호</span>
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="g-row">
          <Kbd accelerator="Alt+CmdOrCtrl+O" />
          <Kbd accelerator="Ctrl+Cmd+S" />
          <Kbd accelerator="CmdOrCtrl+/" />
          <Kbd keys={['↑', '↓']} />
          <Kbd keys={['↩']} />
          <Kbd keys={['esc']} />
        </div>
      </section>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">세그먼트</h2>
        {([undefined, ['focus'] as ForcedState[]] as const).map((force, i) => (
          <div className="g-row" key={i}>
            <Segmented aria-label="밀도" value="regular" onChange={noop} force={force} options={[{ value: 'compact', label: '촘촘하게' }, { value: 'regular', label: '보통' }, { value: 'comfortable', label: '여유 있게' }]} />
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="g-row">
          <Segmented aria-label="글자 크기" value="13" onChange={noop} isDisabled options={[{ value: '12', label: '작게' }, { value: '13', label: '기본' }, { value: '14', label: '크게' }, { value: '15', label: '아주 크게' }]} />
        </div>
      </section>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">웹 팝업 메뉴</h2>
        <Menu aria-label="가져오기" className="menu" style={{ width: 240 }} onAction={noop}>
          <MenuItem id="managed" className="mi" textValue="파일 가져오기…" data-force="active">
            <span className="check" />
            <span>파일 가져오기…</span>
            <span className="shortcut">⌘O</span>
          </MenuItem>
          <MenuItem id="linked" className="mi" textValue="외부 파일 연결…">
            <span className="check" />
            <span>외부 파일 연결…</span>
            <span className="shortcut">⌥⌘O</span>
          </MenuItem>
        </Menu>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

function Part2() {
  return (
    <>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">토스트·스피너·툴팁</h2>
        <StaticToast data={{ kind: 'success', title: '자료 3개를 가져왔습니다' }} />
        <StaticToast data={{ kind: 'error', title: '파일 1개를 가져오지 못했습니다', body: 'notes.txt: 지원하지 않는 형식입니다.' }} />
        <div className="g-row">
          <Spinner />
          <span className="tooltip">
            사이드바 보기/숨기기<span className="tooltip-shortcut">⌃⌘S</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">안내 타일</h2>
        <div className="g-row">
          <Notice icon={VideoOff} title="재생할 수 없습니다" />
          <Notice tone="warning" icon={Link2Off} title="원본 없음" />
          <Notice tone="accent" icon={LibraryBig} title="빈 라이브러리" />
        </div>
      </section>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">사이드바 행</h2>
        <div className="g-list nav-list">
          {([['기본'], ['호버', 'hover'], ['선택(포커스 없음)', 'selected'], ['선택(포커스)', 'selected focused'], ['키보드 커서', 'cursor'], ['선택 + 커서', 'selected focused cursor']] as const).map(([name, force]) => (
            <div key={name} className="nav-row" data-force={force}>
              <Icon icon={LibraryBig} />
              <span className="label ellipsis">{name}</span>
              <span className="count num">12</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section>
        <h2 className="g-title">표 행</h2>
        <div className="g-table">
          <div className="table-head">
            <div className="th">제목</div>
            <div className="th">종류</div>
            <div className="th end">크기</div>
            <div className="th">추가일</div>
          </div>
          <div className="table-body">
            {([['기본', undefined, false], ['줄무늬', undefined, false], ['호버', 'hover', false], ['파일 없음', undefined, true], ['키보드 커서', 'cursor', false]] as const).map(([name, force, missing]) => (
              <div key={name} className="table-row" data-force={force} data-missing={missing || undefined}>
                <div className="td">
                  <Icon icon={FileText} />
                  <span className="row-open">{name} · 분자생물학 6장</span>
                  {missing ? (
                    <span className="inline-flag c-warning">
                      <Icon icon={TriangleAlert} size="sm" />
                      파일 없음
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <Icon icon={Link} size="sm" className="c-tertiary" />
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="td">PDF</div>
                <div className="td end num">4.2 MB</div>
                <div className="td num">2026. 10. 20.</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="g-row">
          <Icon icon={Copy} />
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

/** Component state board (design-spec 10.8). Opened only by the visual tour: learn://app/index.html?window=gallery&part=1|2. */
export function Gallery() {
  const { part } = readWindowParams(location.search);
  return (
    <div className="gallery" data-testid="gallery">
      {part === 1 ? <Part1 /> : <Part2 />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { StrictMode, type ComponentType } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { I18nProvider } from 'react-aria-components';
import { App } from './App';
import { Gallery } from './gallery/Gallery';
import { readWindowParams, type RendererWindow } from './ui/platform';
import './styles';

// Dropping a file outside a drop target must never navigate the window.
for (const type of ['dragover', 'drop'] as const) document.addEventListener(type, (e) => e.preventDefault());

const params = readWindowParams(location.search);
const root = document.documentElement;
root.dataset['platform'] = params.platform;
if (params.deterministic) root.dataset['deterministic'] = '1';
if (params.vibrancy) root.dataset['vibrancy'] = '1';

const VIEWS: Partial<Record<RendererWindow, ComponentType>> = { main: App, gallery: Gallery };
const View = VIEWS[params.window] ?? App;

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <I18nProvider locale="ko-KR">
      <View />
    </I18nProvider>
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

- [ ] **Step 9: 시각 투어 하네스** `tests/e2e/visual/tour.ts`

```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, type Locator, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { ROOT, launch, type Session } from '../helpers';

/** @2x captures for the design critic (same size as docs/design/renders, 2880×1800). Not committed. */
export const TOUR_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'test-results', 'tour');
const TOUR_2X = process.env['LEARN_TOUR_2X'] === '1';

export type TourTheme = 'light' | 'dark';
export const THEMES: readonly TourTheme[] = ['light', 'dark'];

/** Fresh profile, deterministic rendering, a 1440×900 content area at the screen origin. */
export async function launchTour(dir: string, env: Record<string, string> = {}): Promise<Session> {
  const s = await launch(path.join(dir, 'library'), path.join(dir, 'userdata'), { env: { LEARN_DETERMINISTIC: '1', TZ: 'Asia/Seoul', ...env } });
  await s.app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => {
    const w = BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!;
    w.setContentSize(1440, 900);
    w.setPosition(0, 0);
  });
  await expect.poll(() => s.page.evaluate(() => [innerWidth, innerHeight].join('x'))).toBe('1440x900');
  return s;
}

export async function setTheme(page: Page, theme: TourTheme): Promise<void> {
  await page.evaluate((t) => window.learn.prefs.set({ theme: t }), theme);
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches)).toBe(theme === 'dark');
}

/** Fonts loaded, two frames painted, pointer parked in a corner that hovers nothing. */
export async function settle(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.mouse.move(1439, 899);
  await page.evaluate(async () => {
    await document.fonts.ready;
    await new Promise<void>((r) => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => r())));
  });
}

/** Committed 1x baseline (Linux) + optional @2x capture for the critic (LEARN_TOUR_2X=1). */
export async function shoot(page: Page, name: string, opts: { mask?: Locator[] } = {}): Promise<void> {
  await settle(page);
  await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot(`${name}.png`, { animations: 'disabled', caret: 'hide', mask: opts.mask, maxDiffPixelRatio: 0.002 });
  if (TOUR_2X) await capture2x(page, name);
}

async function capture2x(page: Page, name: string): Promise<void> {
  const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page);
  try {
    const { w, h } = await page.evaluate(() => ({ w: innerWidth, h: innerHeight }));
    await cdp.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: w, height: h, deviceScaleFactor: 2, mobile: false });
    await settle(page);
    const { data } = await cdp.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });
    fs.mkdirSync(TOUR_DIR, { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(TOUR_DIR, `${name}.png`), Buffer.from(data, 'base64'));
  } finally {
    await cdp.send('Emulation.clearDeviceMetricsOverride').catch(() => {});
    await cdp.detach().catch(() => {});
  }
}
```

```ts
// Visual tour: Linux baseline screenshots of every shell state, light and dark (quality spec M3).
// Baselines: tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts-snapshots/*-linux.png. Update with
//   pnpm e2e:xvfb tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts --update-snapshots
// @2x captures for the design critic: LEARN_TOUR_2X=1 pnpm e2e:xvfb tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts → test-results/tour/
import { test } from '@playwright/test';
import { THEMES, launchTour, setTheme, shoot } from './tour';

test.skip(process.platform !== 'linux', 'Linux baselines only; macOS chrome is captured by tests/evidence/shell.evidence.ts');

test('component state board (gallery parts 1–2)', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launchTour(testInfo.outputPath());
  try {
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      for (const part of [1, 2]) {
        await s.page.goto(`learn://app/index.html?window=gallery&part=${part}&platform=linux&det=1`);
        await s.page.getByTestId('gallery').waitFor();
        await shoot(s.page, `gallery${part}-${theme}`);
      }
    }
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 패널 규칙 — 실패하는 테스트** `R/shell/layout.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CONTENT_MIN, SIDEBAR, afterViewportChange, autoCollapses, clampSidebar, parsePanes, serializePanes, sidebarHidden, toggledSidebar, type SidebarState } from './layout';

const s = (over: Partial<SidebarState> = {}): SidebarState => ({ width: 240, collapsed: false, forceShown: false, ...over });

describe('sidebar layout rules (design-spec 2.1)', () => {
  it('clamps the width to 180–360', () => {
    expect([clampSidebar(100), clampSidebar(240.4), clampSidebar(999)]).toEqual([180, 240, 360]);
    expect(SIDEBAR).toEqual({ default: 240, min: 180, max: 360 });
  });
  it('folds away automatically when the content column would drop below 520 px', () => {
    expect(CONTENT_MIN).toBe(520);
    expect(autoCollapses(760, 240)).toBe(false);
    expect(autoCollapses(759, 240)).toBe(true);
    expect(sidebarHidden(s(), 720)).toBe(true); // 1440 window at 200 % zoom
    expect(sidebarHidden(s(), 1440)).toBe(false);
  });
  it('toggling shows a folded sidebar on demand and hides a shown one as the user choice', () => {
    const shownOnDemand = toggledSidebar(s(), 720);
    expect(shownOnDemand).toEqual(s({ forceShown: true }));
    expect(sidebarHidden(shownOnDemand, 720)).toBe(false);
    expect(toggledSidebar(s(), 1440)).toEqual(s({ collapsed: true }));
    expect(toggledSidebar(s({ collapsed: true }), 1440)).toEqual(s());
  });
  it('forgets "shown on demand" once there is room again (automatic folding is never persisted)', () => {
    expect(afterViewportChange(s({ forceShown: true }), 1440)).toEqual(s());
    const same = s({ forceShown: true });
    expect(afterViewportChange(same, 720)).toBe(same);
  });
  it('reads and writes the layout.panes setting', () => {
    expect(parsePanes({ sidebar: 300, sidebarCollapsed: 1 })).toEqual({ width: 300, collapsed: true, forceShown: false });
    expect(parsePanes(null)).toEqual(s());
    expect(parsePanes({ sidebar: 9999 })).toEqual(s({ width: 360 }));
    expect(serializePanes(s({ width: 280, collapsed: true }))).toEqual({ sidebar: 280, sidebarCollapsed: 1 });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 구현** `R/shell/layout.ts`

```ts
import { layout } from '../design/tokens';

/** Sidebar width in px: one width for every view (design-spec 2.1, layout B invariant). */
export const SIDEBAR = { default: layout.sidebarWidth, min: layout.sidebarMin, max: layout.sidebarMax } as const;
/**
 * The content column never gets narrower than this: below it the sidebar folds away by itself (design-spec 2.1
 * "창이 960에 가까워지면 … 자동 접힌다", 8.3 200 % zoom without sideways scrolling). 520 fits the library table's
 * title column plus kind, size and date (88 + 88 + 132) with its 16 px insets. M4 adds the item panel before the
 * sidebar in this order.
 */
export const CONTENT_MIN = 520;

export interface SidebarState {
  width: number;
  /** The user hid it (⌃⌘S or dragged it shut). Persisted. */
  collapsed: boolean;
  /** The user showed it while the window is too narrow for it. Session only. */
  forceShown: boolean;
}

export const clampSidebar = (w: number): number => Math.min(SIDEBAR.max, Math.max(SIDEBAR.min, Math.round(w)));
export const autoCollapses = (viewportWidth: number, sidebarWidth: number): boolean => viewportWidth < sidebarWidth + CONTENT_MIN;
export const sidebarHidden = (s: SidebarState, viewportWidth: number): boolean => s.collapsed || (autoCollapses(viewportWidth, s.width) && !s.forceShown);

export function toggledSidebar(s: SidebarState, viewportWidth: number): SidebarState {
  if (sidebarHidden(s, viewportWidth)) return { ...s, collapsed: false, forceShown: autoCollapses(viewportWidth, s.width) };
  return { ...s, collapsed: true, forceShown: false };
}

export function afterViewportChange(s: SidebarState, viewportWidth: number): SidebarState {
  return s.forceShown && !autoCollapses(viewportWidth, s.width) ? { ...s, forceShown: false } : s;
}

export function parsePanes(raw: unknown): SidebarState {
  const r = (raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const width = typeof r['sidebar'] === 'number' ? clampSidebar(r['sidebar']) : SIDEBAR.default;
  return { width, collapsed: r['sidebarCollapsed'] === 1, forceShown: false };
}

export const serializePanes = (s: SidebarState): Record<string, number> => ({ sidebar: s.width, sidebarCollapsed: s.collapsed ? 1 : 0 });
```

```ts
import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

/** IPC data is pushed fresh by main (libraryChanged, prefsChanged): never refetch on a timer, focus or reconnect. */
export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: Infinity, gcTime: Infinity, retry: false, refetchOnWindowFocus: false, refetchOnReconnect: false } },
});
```
`R/data/queries.ts`:
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { Prefs } from '@learn/domain';
import { api, unwrap } from '../api';
import { useLearnEvent } from '../events';
import { queryClient } from './query-client';

export const keys = {
  info: ['app', 'info'] as const,
  prefs: ['prefs'] as const,
  items: ['library', 'items'] as const,
  item: (itemId: string) => ['library', 'item', itemId] as const,
  detail: (itemId: string) => ['library', 'detail', itemId] as const,
  panes: ['settings', 'layout.panes'] as const,
};

export const useAppInfo = () => useQuery({ queryKey: keys.info, queryFn: () => unwrap(api().app.info()) });
export const usePrefs = () => useQuery({ queryKey: keys.prefs, queryFn: () => unwrap(api().prefs.get()) });
export const useItems = () => useQuery({ queryKey: keys.items, queryFn: () => unwrap(api().library.list()) });
/** `library.get` re-checks the file on disk; main broadcasts libraryChanged when that changed availability. */
export const useItem = (itemId: string) => useQuery({ queryKey: keys.item(itemId), queryFn: () => unwrap(api().library.get({ itemId })) });
export const usePanes = () => useQuery({ queryKey: keys.panes, queryFn: () => unwrap(api().settings.get({ key: 'layout.panes' })) });

/** Every change made through main pushes libraryChanged: drop the cached library data. */
export function useLibrarySync(): void {
  useLearnEvent('libraryChanged', () => void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['library'] }));
}

/** prefsChanged carries the whole new prefs object (any window may have changed them). */
export function usePrefsSync(): void {
  useLearnEvent('prefsChanged', ({ prefs }) => queryClient.setQueryData<Prefs>(keys.prefs, prefs));
}
```

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { SIDEBAR, type SidebarState } from '../shell/layout';

export type View = { kind: 'library' } | { kind: 'reader'; itemId: string };

export interface ShellState {
  view: View;
  sidebar: SidebarState;
  /** An import (dialog, copy, duplicate questions) is running: commands are disabled. */
  importing: boolean;
  setView(view: View): void;
  setSidebar(sidebar: SidebarState): void;
  setImporting(importing: boolean): void;
}

export const useShell = create<ShellState>()((set) => ({
  view: { kind: 'library' },
  sidebar: { width: SIDEBAR.default, collapsed: false, forceShown: false },
  importing: false,
  setView: (view) => set({ view }),
  setSidebar: (sidebar) => set({ sidebar }),
  setImporting: (importing) => set({ importing }),
}));
```

`R/shell/useViewportWidth.ts`:
```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

const subscribe = (cb: () => void) => {
  addEventListener('resize', cb);
  return () => removeEventListener('resize', cb);
};

/** CSS px width of the window (zoom-aware), re-rendering on resize. */
export const useViewportWidth = (): number => useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, () => innerWidth);
```
`R/shell/useSidebar.ts`:
```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { api } from '../api';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { serializePanes, sidebarHidden, toggledSidebar } from './layout';
import { useViewportWidth } from './useViewportWidth';

export function useSidebarHidden(): boolean {
  const sidebar = useShell((s) => s.sidebar);
  return sidebarHidden(sidebar, useViewportWidth());
}

export function useToggleSidebar(): () => void {
  return useCallback(() => {
    const { sidebar, setSidebar } = useShell.getState();
    setSidebar(toggledSidebar(sidebar, innerWidth));
  }, []);
}

/** Saves the width and the user's collapse choice (layout.panes) 300 ms after the last change. */
export function usePanePersistence(): void {
  const width = useShell((s) => s.sidebar.width);
  const collapsed = useShell((s) => s.sidebar.collapsed);
  const first = useRef(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (first.current) {
      first.current = false; // the boot value came from the setting itself
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      void api().settings.set({ key: 'layout.panes', value: serializePanes({ width, collapsed, forceShown: false }) });
    }, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [width, collapsed]);
}
```
`R/commands/CommandProvider.tsx`:
```tsx
import { createContext, useContext, useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { CommandId } from '@learn/domain';
import { isCommandEnabled, runCommand, type CommandEnv, type CommandTarget } from './registry';

interface Commands {
  env: CommandEnv;
  run(id: CommandId, target?: CommandTarget): boolean;
  isEnabled(id: CommandId, target?: CommandTarget): boolean;
}

const CommandContext = createContext<Commands | null>(null);

/** Every in-window control runs registry commands through this (same path as the menu bar and the palette). */
export function CommandProvider({ env, children }: { env: CommandEnv; children: ReactNode }) {
  const value = useMemo<Commands>(
    () => ({ env, run: (id, target) => runCommand(id, env, target), isEnabled: (id, target) => isCommandEnabled(id, env, target) }),
    [env],
  );
  return <CommandContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</CommandContext.Provider>;
}

export function useCommands(): Commands {
  const value = useContext(CommandContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('useCommands() outside <CommandProvider>');
  return value;
}
```

```tsx
import { PanelLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { commandById } from '@learn/domain';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { IconButton } from '../ui/Button';
import { useSidebarHidden } from './useSidebar';

/** In the sidebar top while it is shown; in the toolbar (after the traffic-light inset) while it is hidden. */
export function SidebarToggle({ testId }: { testId: string }) {
  const hidden = useSidebarHidden();
  const { run } = useCommands();
  return (
    <IconButton
      icon={PanelLeft}
      label={t.shell.toggleSidebar}
      accelerator={commandById('view.toggleSidebar').accelerator}
      onPress={() => run('view.toggleSidebar')}
      aria-expanded={!hidden}
      aria-controls="sidebar"
      testId={testId}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { SidebarToggle } from './SidebarToggle';
import { useSidebarHidden } from './useSidebar';

/**
 * Unified toolbar (design-spec 2.1): with a single tab (M3–M6) the title bar carries the view title (15/20 600),
 * a count, and the main actions; there is no tab strip and no 44 px pane header.
 */
export function Toolbar(props: { title: string; count?: string; titleTestId?: string; leading?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; trailing?: ReactNode }) {
  const hidden = useSidebarHidden();
  return (
    <header className="titlebar" data-testid="toolbar">
      {hidden && <SidebarToggle testId="toolbar-sidebar-toggle" />}
      {props.leading}
      <div className="tb-title">
        <h1 className="title ellipsis" title={props.title} data-testid={props.titleTestId ?? 'toolbar-title'}>
          {props.title}
        </h1>
        {props.count && (
          <span className="count num" data-testid="toolbar-count">
            {props.count}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="tb-actions">{props.actions}</div>
      {hidden && props.trailing}
    </header>
  );
}
```
(`trailing`은 사이드바가 접혔을 때 도구 막대 오른쪽 끝에 오는 실행기 자리 — Task 9가 채운다.)

`R/shell/Shell.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Group, Panel, Separator, usePanelRef, type LayoutChangedMeta } from 'react-resizable-panels';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { SIDEBAR, afterViewportChange, clampSidebar, sidebarHidden } from './layout';
import { SidebarToggle } from './SidebarToggle';
import { useViewportWidth } from './useViewportWidth';

/**
 * Layout B (design-spec 2.3): a full-height sidebar whose top 88 px (traffic lights + toggle, ⌘K launcher, 8 gap)
 * is identical in every view, a 1 px boundary with a ±4 px resize handle, and the main column (52 px toolbar + content).
 */
export function Shell(props: { sidebar: ReactNode; launcher?: ReactNode; toolbar: ReactNode; children: ReactNode; overlays?: ReactNode }) {
  const panelRef = usePanelRef();
  const sidebar = useShell((s) => s.sidebar);
  const setSidebar = useShell((s) => s.setSidebar);
  const viewportWidth = useViewportWidth();
  const hidden = sidebarHidden(sidebar, viewportWidth);

  useEffect(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;
    if (hidden && !panel.isCollapsed()) panel.collapse();
    else if (!hidden && panel.isCollapsed()) panel.resize(sidebar.width);
  }, [hidden, sidebar.width, panelRef]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const next = afterViewportChange(useShell.getState().sidebar, viewportWidth);
    if (next !== useShell.getState().sidebar) setSidebar(next);
  }, [viewportWidth, setSidebar]);

  /** Only drags and separator keys count as the user's choice (not window resizes or our own collapse()). */
  const onLayoutChanged = (_layout: unknown, meta: LayoutChangedMeta) => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!meta.isUserInteraction || !panel) return;
    const current = useShell.getState().sidebar;
    if (panel.isCollapsed()) setSidebar({ ...current, collapsed: true, forceShown: false });
    else setSidebar({ ...current, width: clampSidebar(panel.getSize().inPixels), collapsed: false });
  };

  return (
    <div className="window" data-testid="shell" data-sidebar={hidden ? 'hidden' : 'shown'}>
      <Group orientation="horizontal" className="window-panes" onLayoutChanged={onLayoutChanged} resizeTargetMinimumSize={{ fine: 9, coarse: 20 }}>
        <Panel
          id="sidebar"
          panelRef={panelRef}
          className="sidebar"
          defaultSize={hidden ? 0 : sidebar.width}
          minSize={SIDEBAR.min}
          maxSize={SIDEBAR.max}
          collapsible
          collapsedSize={0}
          groupResizeBehavior="preserve-pixel-size"
        >
          {/* inert while folded: nothing in a 0 px panel may take focus or be read out. */}
          <nav className="sidebar-inner" aria-label={t.shell.sidebar} inert={hidden}>
            <div className="sidebar-top" data-testid="sidebar-top">
              <SidebarToggle testId="sidebar-toggle" />
            </div>
            <div className="launcher-wrap">{hidden ? null : props.launcher}</div>
            <div className="sidebar-scroll" data-pane="sidebar">
              {props.sidebar}
            </div>
          </nav>
        </Panel>
        <Separator className="pane-separator" id="pane-separator" />
        <Panel id="main" className="main-column">
          {props.toolbar}
          <main className="content" data-testid="content" data-pane="content">
            {props.children}
          </main>
        </Panel>
      </Group>
      {props.overlays}
    </div>
  );
}
```
(`t.shell.sidebar`는 Step 6의 문구 모듈. `data-testid`는 Panel·Separator가 `id`로 자동으로 붙인다: `sidebar`, `main`, `pane-separator`.)

```tsx
import { LibraryBig } from 'lucide-react';
import { ListBox, ListBoxItem } from 'react-aria-components';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';

/**
 * M3 interim library navigator: one row, 내 라이브러리, always the current location (the reader is still "inside" it).
 * Activating it from a reader goes back through the registry (nav.back). M5 replaces this with the collection tree.

export function LibrarySidebar({ count }: { count: number }) {
  const { run, isEnabled } = useCommands();
  return (
    <>
      <div className="section-label" id="sidebar-library-heading">
        {t.shell.libraryHeading}
      </div>
      <ListBox aria-labelledby="sidebar-library-heading" className="nav-list" onAction={() => isEnabled('nav.back') && run('nav.back')}>
        <ListBoxItem id="library" textValue={t.shell.myLibrary} className="nav-row" data-current="" data-testid="nav-library">
          <Icon icon={LibraryBig} />
          <span className="label ellipsis">{t.shell.myLibrary}</span>
          {count > 0 && <span className="count num">{count}</span>}
        </ListBoxItem>
      </ListBox>
    </>
  );
}
```

`R/app/Boot.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useRef } from 'react';
import type { AppInfo } from '@learn/domain';
import { ApiError } from '../api';
import { FatalScreen } from '../components/FatalScreen';
import { useAppInfo, usePanes, usePrefs, usePrefsSync } from '../data/queries';
import { parsePanes } from '../shell/layout';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { MainWindow } from './MainWindow';

/** Info, prefs and the pane layout before the first paint of the shell (no layout jump, no theme flash). */
export function Boot() {
  const info = useAppInfo();
  const prefs = usePrefs();
  usePrefsSync();
  if (info.isError) {
    const e = info.error;
    return <FatalScreen code={e instanceof ApiError ? e.code : 'INTERNAL'} message={e.message} libraryRoot={null} />;
  }
  if (!info.data || !prefs.data) return null;
  if (info.data.libraryError) return <FatalScreen code={info.data.libraryError.code} message={info.data.libraryError.message} libraryRoot={info.data.libraryRoot} />;
  return <PanesGate info={info.data} />;
}

function PanesGate({ info }: { info: AppInfo }) {
  const panes = usePanes();
  const seeded = useRef(false);
  if (panes.isPending) return null;
  if (!seeded.current) {
    seeded.current = true; // a failed read falls back to the defaults
    useShell.getState().setSidebar(parsePanes(panes.data ?? null));
  }
  return <MainWindow info={info} />;
}
```

`R/app/MainWindow.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { AppInfo, ImportOutcome, Result, StorageMode } from '@learn/domain';
import { api, unwrap } from '../api';
import { CommandProvider } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { runPicked, type CommandEnv } from '../commands/registry';
import { useReportContext } from '../commands/useReportContext';
import { useMenuCommands } from '../commands/useMenuCommands';
import { DuplicateDialog } from '../components/DuplicateDialog';
import { LibraryView } from '../components/LibraryView';
import { keys, useItem, useItems, useLibrarySync } from '../data/queries';
import { queryClient } from '../data/query-client';
import { LibrarySidebar } from '../library/LibrarySidebar';
import { Shell } from '../shell/Shell';
import { Toolbar } from '../shell/Toolbar';
import { usePanePersistence, useToggleSidebar } from '../shell/useSidebar';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { ViewerHost } from '../viewers/ViewerHost';

interface PendingDuplicate {
  path: string;
  existingItemId: string;
  mode: StorageMode;
}

function ReaderTitle({ itemId }: { itemId: string }) {
  const item = useItem(itemId);
  return <Toolbar title={item.data?.title ?? ''} />;
}

export function MainWindow(_props: { info: AppInfo }) {
  useLibrarySync();
  usePanePersistence();
  const items = useItems();
  const view = useShell((s) => s.view);
  const setView = useShell((s) => s.setView);
  const importing = useShell((s) => s.importing);
  const setImporting = useShell((s) => s.setImporting);
  const toggleSidebar = useToggleSidebar();
  const [duplicates, setDuplicates] = useState<PendingDuplicate[]>([]);
  const [failures, setFailures] = useState<Array<{ path: string; message: string }>>([]);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [readyAt] = useState(() => Date.now());

  const runImport = useCallback(
    async (request: () => Promise<Result<ImportOutcome[]>>, mode: StorageMode) => {
      setImporting(true);
      try {
        const outcomes = await unwrap(request());
        setDuplicates((q) => [...q, ...outcomes.flatMap((o) => (o.status === 'duplicate' ? [{ path: o.path, existingItemId: o.existingItemId, mode }] : []))]);
        setFailures(outcomes.flatMap((o) => (o.status === 'failed' ? [{ path: o.path, message: o.message }] : [])));
      } catch (e) {
        setFailures([{ path: '', message: (e as Error).message }]);
      } finally {
        setImporting(false);
      }
    },
    [setImporting],
  );

  const relink = useCallback(async (itemId: string) => {
    try {
      const item = await unwrap(api().library.get({ itemId }));
      if (item.storageMode !== 'linked') return;
      if (item.availability !== 'missing') {
        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.items });
        return;
      }
      await unwrap(api().library.pickAndRelink({ assetId: item.assetId }));
    } catch (e) {
      setActionError((e as Error).message);
    }
  }, []);

  const list = items.data ?? [];
  const env: CommandEnv = {
    view: view.kind === 'reader' ? 'viewer' : 'library',
    blocked: importing || duplicates.length > 0,
    targetsOf: (ids) => ids.flatMap((id) => list.find((i) => i.id === id) ?? []),
    importFiles: (mode) => void runImport(() => api().library.pickAndImport({ mode }), mode),
    back: () => setView({ kind: 'library' }),
    open: (itemId) => setView({ kind: 'reader', itemId }),
    relink: (itemId) => void relink(itemId),
    toggleSidebar,
  };
  useMenuCommands(env);
  useReportContext(env);
  const envRef = useRef(env);
  useEffect(() => {
    envRef.current = env;
  });

  const showItemMenu = useCallback(async (itemId: string, x: number, y: number) => {
    setActionError(null);
    try {
      const picked = await unwrap(api().ui.showContextMenu({ kind: 'item', itemIds: [itemId], x, y }));
      if (picked) runPicked(picked.commandId, envRef.current, [itemId]);
    } catch (e) {
      setActionError((e as Error).message);
    }
  }, []);

  const current = duplicates[0];
  const resolveDuplicate = (action: 'open' | 'add' | 'cancel') => {
    if (!current) return;
    setDuplicates((q) => q.slice(1));
    if (action === 'open') setView({ kind: 'reader', itemId: current.existingItemId });
    if (action === 'add') void runImport(() => api().library.import({ paths: [current.path], mode: current.mode, allowDuplicate: true }), current.mode);
  };

  const count = items.data?.length ?? 0;
  const toolbar = view.kind === 'reader' ? <ReaderTitle itemId={view.itemId} /> : <Toolbar title={t.shell.myLibrary} count={count > 0 ? t.shell.count(count) : undefined} />;

  return (
    <CommandProvider env={env}>
      <Shell sidebar={<LibrarySidebar count={count} />} toolbar={toolbar}>
        {items.isError && <p className="error" role="alert" data-testid="list-error">{t.shell.listFailed}</p>}
        {actionError && <p className="error" role="alert" data-testid="action-error">{actionError}</p>}
        {view.kind === 'reader' ? (
          <ViewerHost key={view.itemId} itemId={view.itemId} onBack={() => setView({ kind: 'library' })} />
        ) : (
          <LibraryView
            items={items.data ?? []}
            failures={failures}
            busy={importing}
            onPick={(mode) => void runImport(() => api().library.pickAndImport({ mode }), mode)}
            onImportPaths={(paths, mode) => void runImport(() => api().library.import({ paths, mode }), mode)}
            onOpen={(itemId) => setView({ kind: 'reader', itemId })}
            onItemContextMenu={(itemId, x, y) => void showItemMenu(itemId, x, y)}
          />
        )}
      </Shell>
      {current && (
        <DuplicateDialog path={current.path} onOpenExisting={() => resolveDuplicate('open')} onAddAnyway={() => resolveDuplicate('add')} onCancel={() => resolveDuplicate('cancel')} />
      )}
      {(items.isSuccess || items.isError) && <div data-testid="app-ready" data-at={readyAt} hidden />}
    </CommandProvider>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: 셸 CSS** `R/styles/screens/shell.css` (+ `index.ts`에 `import './screens/shell.css';`)

```css
@layer screens {
  .window {
    position: relative;
    height: 100vh;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: var(--bg-content);
  }
  .window-panes {
    height: 100%;
  }
  /* Sidebar: full height, opaque fallback colour; transparent over macOS vibrancy (Task 8). */
  .sidebar {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    height: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    background: var(--bg-sidebar);
    box-shadow: inset -1px 0 0 var(--separator); /* the 1 px pane boundary, inside the 240 */
  }
  :root[data-vibrancy='1'] .sidebar {
    background: transparent;
  }
  .sidebar-inner {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    height: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  /* Top 88 px, identical in every view: 0–52 traffic lights (84 inset) + toggle, 52–80 launcher, 80–88 gap. */
  .sidebar-top {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    height: var(--toolbar-height);
    flex: none;
    padding: 0 var(--space-12) 0 var(--traffic-light-inset);
    -webkit-app-region: drag;
  }
  .launcher-wrap {
    flex: none;
    height: 36px; /* local: 28 launcher + 8 gap, so the view navigator starts at y 88 */
    padding: 0 var(--space-8) var(--space-8);
  }
  .sidebar-scroll {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-height: 0;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 0 var(--space-8) var(--space-12);
  }
  /* Resize handle: zero width on the boundary, ±4 px hit area; the line itself is the sidebar's inset shadow. */
  .pane-separator {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 3;
    flex: 0 0 0px;
    outline: none;
  }
  .pane-separator::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0 -4px;
  }
  .pane-separator:focus-visible::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0 -1px 0 -2px;
    background: var(--focus);
  }
  .window[data-sidebar='hidden'] .pane-separator {
    display: none;
  }
  .main-column {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    height: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    background: var(--bg-content);
  }
  /* Unified toolbar (design-spec 2.1): 52 px, --bg-window, hairline below. */
  .titlebar {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    height: var(--toolbar-height);
    flex: none;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 0 calc(var(--space-12) + var(--titlebar-controls, 0px)) 0 var(--titlebar-inset, var(--space-12));
    background: var(--bg-window);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--separator);
    -webkit-app-region: drag;
  }
  .tb-title {
    display: flex;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .tb-title .title {
    font: var(--type-title3);
  }
  .tb-title .count {
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
  .tb-actions {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    margin-left: auto;
  }
  .content {
    position: relative;
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-height: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow: hidden;
  }
}
```
(Task 8이 `--titlebar-inset`(macOS 접힘 84)과 `--titlebar-controls`(Linux 창 조절 단추 폭)를 채운다.)

- [ ] **Step 9: E2E — 셸 불변 조건** `tests/e2e/shell.spec.ts`

```ts
// Layout B shell (design-spec 2.1, 2.3): fixed sidebar top, shared width, resize/collapse/persist, auto-fold.
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { FIXTURES, importPaths, launch, openItem, runMenuCommand } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');
const MP4 = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample-h264-aac.mp4');

const box = async (page: Page, testId: string) => {
  const b = await page.getByTestId(testId).first().boundingBox();
  if (!b) throw new Error(`${testId} has no box`);
  return b;
};
const sidebarWidth = async (page: Page) => Math.round((await box(page, 'sidebar')).width);
const dirs = (testInfo: { outputPath(...p: string[]): string }) => [testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata')] as const;

test('layout B: full-height 240 px sidebar, 52 px bars, navigator from y 88', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    expect(await sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(240);
    expect((await box(page, 'sidebar')).height).toBe(await page.evaluate(() => innerHeight));
    expect((await box(page, 'sidebar-top')).height).toBe(52);
    expect(await box(page, 'toolbar')).toMatchObject({ x: 240, y: 0, height: 52 });
    expect(Math.round((await page.locator('.sidebar .section-label').boundingBox())!.y)).toBe(88);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('toolbar-title')).toHaveText('내 라이브러리');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('⌃⌘S hides and shows the sidebar; the toolbar takes the toggle while it is hidden', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(0);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('shell')).toHaveAttribute('data-sidebar', 'hidden');
    expect((await box(page, 'toolbar')).x).toBe(0);
    await page.getByTestId('toolbar-sidebar-toggle').click();
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(240);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('toolbar-sidebar-toggle')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('sidebar-toggle')).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('the separator resizes within 180–360, collapses past the minimum, and both survive a restart', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const [lib, ud] = dirs(testInfo);
  let { app, page } = await launch(lib, ud);
  const drag = async (from: number, to: number) => {
    await page.mouse.move(from, 400);
    await page.mouse.down();
    await page.mouse.move(to, 400, { steps: 10 });
    await page.mouse.up();
  };
  try {
    await drag(240, 300);
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(300);
    await drag(300, 520);
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(360);
    await drag(360, 280);
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(280);
    await page.waitForTimeout(600); // debounced layout.panes save (300 ms)
    await app.close();

    ({ app, page } = await launch(lib, ud));
    expect(await sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(280);
    await drag(280, 40);
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(0);
    await page.waitForTimeout(600);
    await app.close();

    ({ app, page } = await launch(lib, ud));
    expect(await sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(0);
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(280); // the width is remembered while hidden
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('200 % zoom folds the sidebar away without sideways scrolling; it comes back at 100 % (not a user choice)', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  const zoom = (f: number) => app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }, f) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.webContents.setZoomFactor(f), f);
  try {
    await zoom(2);
    await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => innerWidth)).toBeLessThan(760); // 720 at 1440 px (Linux), 512 on the 1024 px macOS runner
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(0);
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(true);
    await zoom(1);
    await expect.poll(() => sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(240);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('layout B invariant: the sidebar top 88 px is pixel-identical in every view, and the width is shared', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  const top88 = async () => {
    await page.mouse.move(900, 700);
    return page.screenshot({ clip: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 240, height: 88 }, animations: 'disabled', caret: 'hide' });
  };
  try {
    const empty = await top88();
    await importPaths(page, [PDF, MP4]);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(2);
    const library = await top88();
    await openItem(page, 'sample');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('pdf-canvas')).toHaveAttribute('data-rendered', /^[1-9]\d*$/);
    const pdf = await top88();
    const pdfWidth = await sidebarWidth(page);
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'nav.back');
    await openItem(page, 'sample-h264-aac');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('video')).toBeVisible();
    const video = await top88();
    for (const [name, shot] of [['library', library], ['pdf', pdf], ['video', video]] as const) expect(shot.equals(empty), name).toBe(true);
    expect(pdfWidth).toBe(240);
    expect(await sidebarWidth(page)).toBe(240);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```

`tests/e2e/library.spec.ts`: 튜터 단언을 지운다 — `await expect(page.getByTestId('tutor-panel')).toContainText('P3');` 한 줄과, `tutor-close` 클릭부터 `tutor-panel` visible까지의 다섯 줄. `grep -n tutor tests/e2e/*.ts tests/evidence/*.ts` → 빈 결과.

### Task 5: 라이브러리 임시 화면 — 목록·빈 상태·가져오기·토스트

**임시 화면의 정의(디자인 비평 기준):** M4의 가상화 표가 오기 전까지 라이브러리 내용 열은 다음과 같다.
- **채워진 라이브러리**: 통합 도구 막대(제목 "내 라이브러리" + 개수 "N개" + `가져오기 ▾` 보조 단추) 바로 아래, 패널 머리 없이 **목업 표의 치수를 그대로 쓴 4열 목록** — 열은 `제목 | 종류 | 크기 | 추가일`(격자 `minmax(0,1fr) 88px 88px 132px`), 머리 28(footnote 2차색, 열 사이 16px 구분선, 정렬 표시 없음), 행 `--row-height`(보통 28), 좌우 8 안쪽의 반경 6 행, 짝수 행 `--bg-content-alt`, 호버 `--fill-hover`. 첫 열은 종류 아이콘 16(`file-text`/`clapperboard`) + 8 + 제목(본문 1차색, 한 줄 말줄임 + 전체 제목 툴팁), 외부 연결이면 `link` 14 3차색, 파일 없음이면 `triangle-alert` 14 + "파일 없음"(`--warning-text`, footnote). 크기는 오른쪽 정렬 tabular, 추가일은 명세 9절 형식("오늘 오전 9:24", "어제 오후 8:40", "2026. 10. 12."). 지금의 "보관"·"상태" 열은 위 아이콘·표시로 바뀐다.
- **상호작용(오늘 동작 유지)**: 제목을 클릭하거나 Tab으로 가서 ↩/스페이스 → 열기, 행 더블클릭 → 열기, 오른쪽 클릭 → M2 네이티브 항목 메뉴. **선택·정렬·열 구성·빠른 검색·항목 패널은 없다**(M4). 키보드 포커스가 제목 단추에 있으면 행 전체에 안쪽 2px `--focus` 고리(키보드 커서 문법).
- **빈 라이브러리**: 목업 `library-empty.html` 그대로 — 56px 강조 soft 타일 안 `library-big` 32, 제목(title2) "아직 자료가 없습니다", 설명(callout, 두 줄), 큰 단추 두 개("파일 가져오기…" 기본, "외부 파일 연결…" 보조), 힌트("파일 가져오기 ⌘O", 지원 형식). 팔레트 힌트 "명령 팔레트 ⌘K"는 팔레트가 생기는 Task 9에서 더한다. 도구 막대는 제목 + `가져오기 ▾`(개수 없음).
- **가져오기 결과**: 토스트 — 성공 "자료 N개를 가져왔습니다"(외부 연결이면 "…연결했습니다", 5초 뒤 사라짐), 실패 "파일 N개를 가져오지 못했습니다" + 파일별 "이름: 이유"(최대 3줄 + "외 N개", 닫을 때까지 남음). 가져오는 동안 가져오기 단추·빈 상태 단추는 비활성(잠시 지나가는 상태).
- **창 전체 드롭**(오버레이 없이): Finder에서 끌어 놓으면 사본으로 가져오고 ⌥를 누르고 놓으면 외부 연결. 오버레이는 Task 11.

- [ ] **Step 1: 날짜 형식 — 실패하는 테스트** `R/format.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { formatDate, formatSize } from './format';

const at = (y: number, m: number, d: number, h: number, min: number) => new Date(y, m - 1, d, h, min).toISOString();
const now = new Date(2026, 9, 21, 15, 0);

describe('format (design-spec 9)', () => {
  it('writes today and yesterday with the time, older dates as Y. M. D.', () => {
    expect(formatDate(at(2026, 10, 21, 9, 24), now)).toBe('오늘 오전 9:24');
    expect(formatDate(at(2026, 10, 20, 20, 40), now)).toBe('어제 오후 8:40');
    expect(formatDate(at(2026, 10, 12, 10, 0), now)).toBe('2026. 10. 12.');
    expect(formatDate(at(2025, 12, 31, 23, 59), new Date(2026, 0, 1, 8, 0))).toBe('어제 오후 11:59');
  });
  it('keeps one decimal below 10 units', () => {
    expect(formatSize(4_404_019)).toBe('4.2 MB');
    expect(formatSize(2_576_980_378)).toBe('2.4 GB');
    expect(formatSize(512)).toBe('512 B');
  });
});
```
Run: `pnpm vitest run apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/format.test.ts` → FAIL(오늘/어제 형식 없음).

- [ ] **Step 2: 구현** — `R/format.ts`의 `formatDate`를 바꾸고 `R/strings/common.ts`에 `today: '오늘', yesterday: '어제',`를 더한다.

```ts
import { t } from './strings';

const dayKey = (d: Date) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth()}-${d.getDate()}`;

/** design-spec 9: "오늘 오전 9:24", "어제 오후 8:40", otherwise "2026. 10. 12.". */
export function formatDate(iso: string, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const yesterday = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 1);
  const time = d.toLocaleTimeString('ko-KR', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
  if (dayKey(d) === dayKey(now)) return `${t.common.today} ${time}`;
  if (dayKey(d) === dayKey(yesterday)) return `${t.common.yesterday} ${time}`;
  return `${d.getFullYear()}. ${d.getMonth() + 1}. ${d.getDate()}.`;
}
```
Run → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 3: 가져오기 흐름 — 실패하는 테스트** `R/library/import-flow.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { ImportOutcome, LibraryItem } from '@learn/domain';
import { failureLines, settleImport } from './import-flow';

const item = (id: string) => ({ id }) as LibraryItem;
const imported = (path: string, id: string): ImportOutcome => ({ status: 'imported', path, item: item(id) });
const dup = (path: string, existingItemId: string): ImportOutcome => ({ status: 'duplicate', path, existingItemId });
const failed = (path: string, message: string): ImportOutcome => ({ status: 'failed', path, code: 'UNSUPPORTED_TYPE', message });

describe('settleImport', () => {
  it('counts imports and failures and asks about each duplicate in order', async () => {
    const confirm = vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce('skip').mockResolvedValueOnce('add');
    const importAgain = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([imported('/b.pdf', 'n2')]);
    const report = await settleImport([imported('/a.pdf', 'n1'), dup('/x.pdf', 'e1'), dup('/b.pdf', 'e2'), failed('/dir/notes.txt', '지원하지 않는 형식입니다.')], 'managed', { confirm, importAgain });
    expect(confirm.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0])).toEqual([{ path: '/x.pdf', existingItemId: 'e1' }, { path: '/b.pdf', existingItemId: 'e2' }]);
    expect(importAgain).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith('/b.pdf', 'managed');
    expect(report).toEqual({ imported: 2, failed: [{ name: 'notes.txt', message: '지원하지 않는 형식입니다.' }], openItemId: null });
  });
  it('remembers the existing item to open', async () => {
    const report = await settleImport([dup('/x.pdf', 'e1')], 'linked', { confirm: async () => 'open', importAgain: vi.fn() });
    expect(report.openItemId).toBe('e1');
    expect(report.imported).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('failureLines', () => {
  it('lists up to three files and counts the rest', () => {
    const f = ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e'].map((n) => ({ name: `${n}.txt`, message: '지원하지 않는 형식입니다.' }));
    expect(failureLines(f)).toBe('a.txt: 지원하지 않는 형식입니다.\nb.txt: 지원하지 않는 형식입니다.\nc.txt: 지원하지 않는 형식입니다.\n외 2개');
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 구현** `R/library/import-flow.ts`

```ts
import type { ImportOutcome, StorageMode } from '@learn/domain';
import { t } from '../strings';

export type DuplicateChoice = 'open' | 'add' | 'skip';

export interface ImportReport {
  imported: number;
  failed: Array<{ name: string; message: string }>;
  /** The user chose 기존 자료 열기 for this existing item (the last such choice wins). */
  openItemId: string | null;
}

export interface ImportFlowDeps {
  /** Ask about one duplicate: Task 5 in-window dialog, Task 6 native sheet. */
  confirm(dup: { path: string; existingItemId: string }): Promise<DuplicateChoice>;
  /** Import one path again with allowDuplicate (새 자료로 추가). */
  importAgain(path: string, mode: StorageMode): Promise<ImportOutcome[]>;
}

const fileName = (p: string) => p.split(/[\\/]/).pop() || p;

/** One import's outcomes → questions about duplicates (in order) → a report for the toasts. */
export async function settleImport(outcomes: readonly ImportOutcome[], mode: StorageMode, deps: ImportFlowDeps): Promise<ImportReport> {
  const report: ImportReport = { imported: 0, failed: [], openItemId: null };
  const take = (list: readonly ImportOutcome[]) => {
    for (const o of list) {
      if (o.status === 'imported') report.imported++;
      else if (o.status === 'failed') report.failed.push({ name: fileName(o.path), message: o.message });
    }
  };
  take(outcomes);
  for (const o of outcomes) {
    if (o.status !== 'duplicate') continue;
    const choice = await deps.confirm({ path: o.path, existingItemId: o.existingItemId });
    if (choice === 'open') report.openItemId = o.existingItemId;
    if (choice === 'add') take(await deps.importAgain(o.path, mode));
  }
  return report;
}

export function failureLines(failed: ImportReport['failed'], max = 3): string {
  const lines = failed.slice(0, max).map((f) => `${f.name}: ${f.message}`);
  if (failed.length > max) lines.push(t.toast.more(failed.length - max));
  return lines.join('\n');
}
```

`R/library/LibraryTable.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Clapperboard, FileText, Link, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import type { LibraryItem } from '@learn/domain';
import { formatDate, formatSize } from '../format';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';

/**
 * M3 interim library list (M4 replaces it with the virtualized RAC table and keeps these metrics).
 * Open = click/↩ on a title or double-click on the row; right-click = native item menu. No selection or sorting yet.
 */
export function LibraryTable(props: { items: readonly LibraryItem[]; onOpen(itemId: string): void; onContextMenu(itemId: string, x: number, y: number): void }) {
  return (
    <div className="table library-table" role="table" aria-label={t.library.table} data-testid="library-table">
      <div className="table-head" role="row">
        <div className="th" role="columnheader">{t.library.colTitle}</div>
        <div className="th" role="columnheader">{t.library.colType}</div>
        <div className="th end" role="columnheader">{t.library.colSize}</div>
        <div className="th" role="columnheader">{t.library.colAdded}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="table-body" role="rowgroup">
        {props.items.map((item) => {
          const missing = item.availability === 'missing';
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="table-row"
              role="row"
              data-testid="item-row"
              data-missing={missing || undefined}
              onDoubleClick={() => props.onOpen(item.id)}
              onContextMenu={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                props.onContextMenu(item.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
              }}
            >
              <div className="td" role="cell">
                <Icon icon={item.type === 'video' ? Clapperboard : FileText} />
                <button type="button" className="row-open" data-testid="open-item" data-title={item.title} title={item.title} onClick={() => props.onOpen(item.id)}>
                  {item.title}
                </button>
                {item.storageMode === 'linked' && (
                  <span className="linked-mark c-tertiary" title={t.library.linked}>
                    <Icon icon={Link} size="sm" label={t.library.linked} />
                  </span>
                )}
                {missing && (
                  <span className="inline-flag c-warning" title={t.library.missingTitle} data-testid="missing-flag">
                    <Icon icon={TriangleAlert} size="sm" />
                    {t.library.missing}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="td" role="cell">{t.library.type[item.type]}</div>
              <div className="td end num" role="cell">{formatSize(item.size)}</div>
              <div className="td num" role="cell" data-col="added">{formatDate(item.createdAt)}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { commandById, type CommandId } from '@learn/domain';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { MenuButton } from '../ui/MenuButton';

const IDS = ['file.importManaged', 'file.importLinked'] as const satisfies readonly CommandId[];
const TEST_IDS = { 'file.importManaged': 'import-menu-managed', 'file.importLinked': 'import-menu-linked' } as const;

/** 가져오기 ▾ (design-spec 7.3): 파일 가져오기… ⌘O · 외부 파일 연결… ⌥⌘O — the registry's own items. */
export function ImportMenuButton() {
  const { run, isEnabled } = useCommands();
  return (
    <MenuButton
      label={t.library.importButton}
      icon={Plus}
      testId="import-menu"
      isDisabled={!IDS.some((id) => isEnabled(id))}
      items={IDS.map((id) => ({ id, label: commandById(id).label, accelerator: commandById(id).accelerator, testId: TEST_IDS[id], isDisabled: !isEnabled(id) }))}
      onAction={(id) => run(id)}
    />
  );
}
```

`R/library/useImport.ts`:
```ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { ImportOutcome, Result, StorageMode } from '@learn/domain';
import { api, unwrap } from '../api';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { showToast } from '../ui/toasts';
import { failureLines, settleImport, type ImportFlowDeps } from './import-flow';

/** Runs one import end to end: dialog/copy in main, duplicate questions, result toasts; commands stay disabled meanwhile. */
export function useImport(confirm: ImportFlowDeps['confirm']) {
  const setImporting = useShell((s) => s.setImporting);
  const setView = useShell((s) => s.setView);
  return useCallback(
    async (request: () => Promise<Result<ImportOutcome[]>>, mode: StorageMode) => {
      setImporting(true);
      try {
        const outcomes = await unwrap(request());
        const report = await settleImport(outcomes, mode, {
          confirm,
          importAgain: (path, m) => unwrap(api().library.import({ paths: [path], mode: m, allowDuplicate: true })),
        });
        if (report.imported > 0) showToast({ kind: 'success', title: (mode === 'linked' ? t.toast.linked : t.toast.imported)(report.imported), testId: 'import-done' });
        if (report.failed.length > 0) showToast({ kind: 'error', title: t.toast.failedTitle(report.failed.length), body: failureLines(report.failed), testId: 'import-failures' });
        if (report.openItemId) setView({ kind: 'reader', itemId: report.openItemId });
      } catch (e) {
        showToast({ kind: 'error', title: t.toast.importFailed, body: (e as Error).message, testId: 'import-failures' });
      } finally {
        setImporting(false);
      }
    },
    [confirm, setImporting, setView],
  );
}
```
`R/shell/useWindowDrop.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { StorageMode } from '@learn/domain';
import { api } from '../api';

const hasFiles = (e: DragEvent) => e.dataTransfer?.types.includes('Files') ?? false;

/** Finder → window: copy, or link with ⌥ held (design-spec 7.2). The overlay arrives in Task 11. */
export function useWindowDrop(enabled: boolean, onDrop: (paths: string[], mode: StorageMode) => void): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const over = (e: DragEvent) => {
      if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.dataTransfer!.dropEffect = e.altKey ? 'link' : 'copy';
    };
    const drop = (e: DragEvent) => {
      if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const paths = Array.from(e.dataTransfer!.files).map((f) => api().pathForFile(f)).filter((p) => p.length > 0);
      onDrop(paths, e.altKey ? 'linked' : 'managed');
    };
    addEventListener('dragover', over);
    addEventListener('drop', drop);
    return () => {
      removeEventListener('dragover', over);
      removeEventListener('drop', drop);
    };
  }, [enabled, onDrop]);
}
```

```tsx
  /** Task 5: the pre-M3 dialog, promise-based. Task 6 replaces it with the native duplicate sheet. */
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState<{ path: string; resolve(choice: DuplicateChoice): void } | null>(null);
  const confirm = useCallback((dup: { path: string; existingItemId: string }) => new Promise<DuplicateChoice>((resolve) => setAsking({ path: dup.path, resolve })), []);
  const answer = (choice: DuplicateChoice) => {
    asking?.resolve(choice);
    setAsking(null);
  };
  const runImport = useImport(confirm);

  const onDrop = useCallback(
    (paths: string[], mode: StorageMode) => {
      if (paths.length === 0) {
        showToast({ kind: 'error', title: t.toast.dropNoPathTitle, body: t.toast.dropNoPathBody, testId: 'drop-failed' });
        return;
      }
      void runImport(() => api().library.import({ paths, mode }), mode);
    },
    [runImport],
  );
  useWindowDrop(view.kind === 'library' && !importing, onDrop);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (items.isError) showToast({ kind: 'error', title: t.shell.listFailed, body: items.error.message, testId: 'list-error' });
  }, [items.isError, items.error]);
```
