```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { importFiles, launch, openItem, stubOpenDialog, type Session } from './helpers';

function fakePdf(dir: string, name: string, body: string): string {
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  const p = path.join(dir, name);
  fs.writeFileSync(p, `%PDF-1.4\n% ${body}\n%%EOF\n`);
  return p;
}

test('import, duplicate prompt, failure toast, missing linked file and relink', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const lib = testInfo.outputPath('library');
  const ud = testInfo.outputPath('userdata');
  const src = testInfo.outputPath('src');
  const a = fakePdf(src, 'Chapter 06.pdf', 'six');
  const b = fakePdf(src, '신호 전달 (2).pdf', 'two');

  let s: Session = await launch(lib, ud);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('empty-state')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('toolbar-count')).toHaveCount(0);

  await importFiles(s, [a]);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-done')).toContainText('자료 1개를 가져왔습니다');
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('toolbar-count')).toHaveText('1개');

  await importFiles(s, [a]);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('duplicate-dialog')).toBeVisible();
  await s.page.getByTestId('dup-cancel').click();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('duplicate-dialog')).toBeHidden();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(1);

  await importFiles(s, [a]);
  await s.page.getByTestId('dup-add').click();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(2);

  await importFiles(s, [a]);
  await s.page.getByTestId('dup-open').click();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('viewer-title')).toHaveText('Chapter 06');
  await s.page.getByTestId('viewer-back').click();

  const notes = path.join(src, 'notes.txt');
  fs.writeFileSync(notes, 'plain text');
  await importFiles(s, [notes]);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-failures')).toContainText('notes.txt');
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-failures')).toContainText('지원하지 않는 형식');
  await s.page.getByTestId('import-failures').getByTestId('toast-close').click();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-failures')).toHaveCount(0);

  // The 가져오기 ▾ menu runs the same registry command (linked).
  await stubOpenDialog(s.app, [b]);
  await s.page.getByTestId('import-menu').click();
  await s.page.getByTestId('import-menu-linked').click();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-done')).toContainText('연결했습니다');
  await s.app.close();

  const moved = path.join(src, 'moved.pdf');
  fs.renameSync(b, moved);
  s = await launch(lib, ud);
  const row = s.page.getByTestId('item-row').filter({ hasText: '신호 전달 (2)' });
  await expect(row.getByTestId('missing-flag')).toHaveText('파일 없음');
  await openItem(s.page, '신호 전달 (2)');
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('missing-notice')).toBeVisible();
  await stubOpenDialog(s.app, [moved]);
  await s.page.getByTestId('relink').click();
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('missing-notice')).toBeHidden();
  await s.page.getByTestId('viewer-back').click();
  await expect(row.getByTestId('missing-flag')).toHaveCount(0);
  await s.page.screenshot({ path: testInfo.outputPath('library.png') });
  await s.app.close();
});

test('the empty-state buttons and the drop path need a real file location', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-managed')).toHaveText('파일 가져오기…');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-linked')).toHaveText('외부 파일 연결…');
    // A synthetic drop has no Finder path: nothing is imported and the toast says what to do instead.
    await s.page.evaluate(() => {
      const dt = new DataTransfer();
      dt.items.add(new File(['%PDF-1.4'], 'x.pdf', { type: 'application/pdf' }));
      document.body.dispatchEvent(new DragEvent('dragover', { dataTransfer: dt, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
      document.body.dispatchEvent(new DragEvent('drop', { dataTransfer: dt, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    });
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('drop-failed')).toContainText('파일 › 파일 가져오기…');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('empty-state')).toBeVisible();
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```
(`tests/e2e/first-session.spec.ts`와 증거 테스트의 가져오기는 모두 빈 라이브러리에서 한 번 `import-managed`를 누르므로 빈 상태 단추로 그대로 동작한다. 확인: `grep -n "getByTestId('import-" tests/e2e/*.ts tests/evidence/*.ts`의 결과가 각 테스트의 첫 가져오기뿐이어야 한다.)

```ts
const LIBRARY = ['sample.pdf', '한글 경로 (테스트) #1.pdf', 'sample-h264-aac.mp4', 'sample-mpeg4part2.mp4'].map((f) => path.join(FIXTURES, f));

test('library: empty, list, collapsed sidebar, failure toast', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launchTour(testInfo.outputPath());
  const dates = () => [s.page.locator('[data-col="added"]')];
  try {
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      await shoot(s.page, `empty-${theme}`);
    }
    await importPaths(s.page, LIBRARY);
    // A linked file that went missing: the row shows the link mark and the 파일 없음 flag.
    const gone = testInfo.outputPath('gone.pdf');
    fs.copyFileSync(LIBRARY[0]!, gone);
    await s.page.evaluate((p) => window.learn.library.import({ paths: [p], mode: 'linked', allowDuplicate: true }), gone);
    fs.rmSync(gone);
    await s.page.evaluate(async () => {
      const list = await window.learn.library.list();
      if (list.ok) for (const i of list.data) await window.learn.library.get({ itemId: i.id });
    });
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('missing-flag')).toHaveCount(1);
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      await shoot(s.page, `library-${theme}`, { mask: dates() });
      await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
      await shoot(s.page, `library-collapsed-${theme}`, { mask: dates() });
      await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
    }
    await setTheme(s.page, 'light');
    await importFiles(s, [path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf')]); // duplicate → the pre-M3 dialog, skip it
    await s.page.getByTestId('dup-cancel').click();
    const txt = testInfo.outputPath('notes.txt');
    fs.writeFileSync(txt, 'x');
    await importFiles(s, [txt]);
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('import-failures')).toBeVisible();
    await shoot(s.page, 'toast-failure-light', { mask: dates() });
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```

- Produces (tests): `expectWindow(app, kind: 'settings' | 'sheet', trigger: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<Page>`.

```ts
import type { ItemType, StorageMode } from './library';

/** Modal sheet windows (design-spec 6 시트). Types only (no zod): the preload never needs the schemas. */
export type DuplicateChoice = 'open' | 'add' | 'skip';

export interface DuplicateCard {
  title: string;
  type: ItemType;
  size: number;
  createdAt: string;
  storageMode: StorageMode;
}

export type SheetPayload = { kind: 'duplicate'; fileName: string; existing: DuplicateCard } | { kind: 'shortcuts' };
export type SheetResult = { kind: 'duplicate'; choice: DuplicateChoice } | { kind: 'shortcuts' };
```

`M/measure.ts`:
```ts
import type { WebContents } from 'electron';

/**
 * Natural height (CSS px, rounded up) of the first element matching `selector` in a not-yet-shown window.
 * Polls with timers (hidden windows get no animation frames); 0 → `fallback`.
 */
export async function contentHeight(contents: WebContents, selector: string, fallback = 240, timeoutMs = 5000): Promise<number> {
  const script = `new Promise((resolve) => {
    const started = Date.now();
    const tick = () => {
      const el = document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(selector)});
      const h = el ? el.getBoundingClientRect().height : 0;
      if (h > 0) resolve(Math.ceil(h));
      else if (Date.now() - started > ${timeoutMs}) resolve(0);
      else setTimeout(tick, 16);
    };
    tick();
  })`;
  const height = Number(await contents.executeJavaScript(script, true));
  return height > 0 ? height : fallback;
}

/** After sizing: the page may now cap itself to the window and scroll inside (CSS :root[data-sized]). */
export async function markSized(contents: WebContents): Promise<void> {
  await contents.executeJavaScript("document.documentElement.dataset.sized = '1'", true);
}
```
`M/sheets.ts`:
```ts
import { BrowserWindow, nativeTheme, type WebContents } from 'electron';
import { LearnError, type SheetPayload, type SheetResult } from '@learn/domain';
import { themeHex } from '../renderer/src/design/tokens.generated';
import { contentHeight, markSized } from './measure';

export const SHEET_WIDTH: Readonly<Record<SheetPayload['kind'], number>> = { duplicate: 440, shortcuts: 560 };
/** 8 px under the 52 px toolbar (macOS does this itself through setSheetOffset; elsewhere we place it). */
export const SHEET_TOP = 60;

export interface SheetHost {
  /** A modal sheet over `parent`; resolves with the sheet's result, or null when it closed without one. */
  open(parent: BrowserWindow, payload: SheetPayload): Promise<SheetResult | null>;
  payloadFor(contents: WebContents): SheetPayload;
  resolve(contents: WebContents, result: SheetResult): void;
}

interface Pending {
  payload: SheetPayload;
  settle(result: SheetResult | null): void;
}

export function createSheetHost(opts: { preload: string; url(): string; platform: NodeJS.Platform }): SheetHost {
  const pending = new Map<number, Pending>();
  const find = (contents: WebContents): Pending => {
    const p = pending.get(contents.id);
    if (!p) throw new LearnError('PERMISSION_DENIED', '허용되지 않은 호출자입니다.');
    return p;
  };

  return {
    open(parent, payload) {
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        const width = SHEET_WIDTH[payload.kind];
        const room = parent.getContentBounds();
        const maxHeight = Math.max(160, room.height - SHEET_TOP - 16);
        const win = new BrowserWindow({
          parent,
          modal: true, // macOS: attached as a sheet below parent.setSheetOffset(52)
          show: false,
          width,
          height: maxHeight, // measured at full height first, then shrunk to the content
          useContentSize: true,
          resizable: false,
          minimizable: false,
          maximizable: false,
          fullscreenable: false,
          frame: opts.platform === 'darwin',
          title: '',
          backgroundColor: themeHex[nativeTheme.shouldUseDarkColors ? 'dark' : 'light'].bgRaised,
          webPreferences: {
            preload: opts.preload,
            sandbox: true,
            contextIsolation: true,
            nodeIntegration: false,
            webSecurity: true,
            spellcheck: false,
            backgroundThrottling: false,
          },
        });
        const id = win.webContents.id;
        let done = false;
        const settle = (result: SheetResult | null) => {
          if (done) return;
          done = true;
          pending.delete(id);
          resolve(result);
          if (!win.isDestroyed()) win.close();
        };
        pending.set(id, { payload, settle });
        win.on('closed', () => settle(null));
        win.once('ready-to-show', () => {
          void (async () => {
            const height = Math.min(await contentHeight(win.webContents, '[data-sheet-root]'), maxHeight);
            if (win.isDestroyed()) return;
            win.setContentSize(width, height);
            if (opts.platform !== 'darwin') win.setPosition(Math.round(room.x + (room.width - width) / 2), room.y + SHEET_TOP);
            await markSized(win.webContents);
            win.show();
          })();
        });
        void win.loadURL(opts.url());
      });
    },
    payloadFor: (contents) => find(contents).payload,
    resolve(contents, result) {
      const p = find(contents);
      if (p.payload.kind !== result.kind) throw new LearnError('VALIDATION_FAILED', '잘못된 요청입니다.');
      p.settle(result);
    },
  };
}
```
`M/ipc/sheet.ts`:
```ts
import { Channels, emptyInput, sheetResultInput } from '@learn/domain';
import type { Handle, IpcDeps } from './register';

/** Sheet windows only: the host rejects any other sender (PERMISSION_DENIED). */
export function registerSheetIpc(handle: Handle, deps: IpcDeps): void {
  handle(Channels.sheet.payload, emptyInput, (_input, event) => deps.sheets.payloadFor(event.sender));
  handle(Channels.sheet.resolve, sheetResultInput, (result, event) => deps.sheets.resolve(event.sender, result));
}
```
`M/ipc/register.ts`의 `IpcDeps`에 `sheets: SheetHost;`(`import type { SheetHost } from '../sheets';`). `M/ipc/test-deps.ts`의 기본값에 `sheets: { open: async () => null, payloadFor: () => ({ kind: 'shortcuts' }), resolve: () => {} },`.
`M/ipc/ui.ts` 끝에(`import path from 'node:path';`, `confirmDuplicateInput`, `emptyInput` import):
```ts
  handle(Channels.ui.confirmDuplicate, confirmDuplicateInput, async ({ path: file, existingItemId }, event) => {
    const parent = BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender);
    const existing = deps.getContext().repo.getItem(existingItemId);
    if (!parent || !existing) return { choice: 'skip' as const };
    const result = await deps.sheets.open(parent, {
      kind: 'duplicate',
      fileName: path.basename(file),
      existing: { title: existing.title, type: existing.type, size: existing.size, createdAt: existing.createdAt, storageMode: existing.storageMode },
    });
    return { choice: result?.kind === 'duplicate' ? result.choice : ('skip' as const) };
  });

  handle(Channels.ui.showShortcuts, emptyInput, async (_input, event) => {
    const parent = BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender);
    if (parent) await deps.sheets.open(parent, { kind: 'shortcuts' });
  });
```

`R/sheets/shortcuts.ts`:
```ts
import { COMMANDS, acceleratorKeys, acceleratorLabel, keySpecKeys, type CommandContext } from '@learn/domain';
import { t } from '../strings';

export interface ShortcutRow {
  label: string;
  keys: string[];
  /** Read by screen readers instead of the decorative keycaps. */
  spoken: string;
}
export interface ShortcutGroup {
  title: string;
  rows: ShortcutRow[];
}

const ORDER: CommandContext[] = ['app', 'library', 'viewer'];

/** The ⌘/ sheet: every registry shortcut by its first context, then the keys every dialog understands. */
export function shortcutGroups(platform: string): ShortcutGroup[] {
  const groups = ORDER.map((ctx) => ({
    title: t.sheets.groups[ctx],
    rows: COMMANDS.filter((c) => c.contexts[0] === ctx && (c.accelerator !== undefined || c.key !== undefined)).map((c): ShortcutRow => {
      const keys = c.accelerator ? acceleratorKeys(c.accelerator, platform) : keySpecKeys(c.key!, platform);
      return { label: c.label, keys, spoken: c.accelerator ? acceleratorLabel(c.accelerator, platform) : keys.join('+') };
    }),
  })).filter((g) => g.rows.length > 0);
  const dialogs: ShortcutGroup = { title: t.sheets.groups.dialogs, rows: t.sheets.dialogKeys.map((r) => ({ label: r.label, keys: [...r.keys], spoken: r.keys.join(' ') })) };
  return [...groups, dialogs];
}
```
`R/sheets/SheetApp.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { SheetResult } from '@learn/domain';
import { api, unwrap } from '../api';
import { DuplicateSheet } from './DuplicateSheet';
import { ShortcutsSheet } from './ShortcutsSheet';

export const resolveSheet = (result: SheetResult): void => void api().sheet.resolve(result);

/** Renderer of a modal sheet window (?window=sheet). Main sizes the window to [data-sheet-root]. */
export function SheetApp() {
  const payload = useQuery({ queryKey: ['sheet', 'payload'], queryFn: () => unwrap(api().sheet.payload()) });
  if (!payload.data) return null;
  return payload.data.kind === 'duplicate' ? <DuplicateSheet payload={payload.data} /> : <ShortcutsSheet />;
}
```
`R/sheets/DuplicateSheet.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Clapperboard, Copy, FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import type { DuplicateChoice, SheetPayload } from '@learn/domain';
import { formatDate, formatSize } from '../format';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { resolveSheet } from './SheetApp';

/** dialogs.html?scene=duplicate. ↩ = 건너뛰기 (the safe default), esc = 건너뛰기, 기존 자료 열기 on the left. */
export function DuplicateSheet({ payload }: { payload: Extract<SheetPayload, { kind: 'duplicate' }> }) {
  const choose = (choice: DuplicateChoice) => resolveSheet({ kind: 'duplicate', choice });
  const e = payload.existing;
  return (
    <div
      className="sheet"
      role="alertdialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="sheet-title"
      aria-describedby="sheet-text"
      data-sheet-root=""
      data-kind="duplicate"
      data-testid="sheet-root"
      onKeyDown={(ev) => {
        if (ev.key === 'Escape' && !ev.nativeEvent.isComposing) choose('skip');
      }}
    >
      <div className="d-body">
        <div className="d-title">
          <span className="badge-icon">
            <Icon icon={Copy} />
          </span>
          <h2 id="sheet-title">{t.sheets.duplicateTitle}</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="d-text ui-prose" id="sheet-text">
          <b data-testid="dup-file">{payload.fileName}</b>
          {t.sheets.duplicateBody}
        </p>
        <div className="card item-card" data-testid="dup-card">
          <span className="thumb">
            <Icon icon={e.type === 'video' ? Clapperboard : FileText} />
          </span>
          <div className="card-text">
            <div className="t-headline ellipsis" title={e.title}>
              {e.title}
            </div>
            <div className="t-footnote c-secondary ellipsis">{t.sheets.cardMeta(t.library.type[e.type], formatSize(e.size), formatDate(e.createdAt))}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="d-foot">
        <Button variant="plain-accent" leading testId="dup-open" onPress={() => choose('open')}>
          {t.sheets.openExisting}
        </Button>
        <span className="spacer" />
        <Button testId="dup-add" onPress={() => choose('add')}>
          {t.sheets.addAnyway}
        </Button>
        <Button variant="primary" autoFocus testId="dup-skip" onPress={() => choose('skip')}>
          {t.sheets.skip}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`R/sheets/ShortcutsSheet.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Keyboard } from 'lucide-react';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { Kbd } from '../ui/Kbd';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';
import { shortcutGroups } from './shortcuts';
import { resolveSheet } from './SheetApp';

/** ⌘/ (design-spec 7.4), generated from the command registry; ↩ or esc closes it. */
export function ShortcutsSheet() {
  const done = () => resolveSheet({ kind: 'shortcuts' });
  return (
    <div
      className="sheet wide"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="sheet-title"
      data-sheet-root=""
      data-kind="shortcuts"
      data-testid="sheet-root"
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') done();
      }}
    >
      <div className="d-body">
        <div className="d-title">
          <span className="badge-icon">
            <Icon icon={Keyboard} />
          </span>
          <h2 id="sheet-title">{t.sheets.shortcutsTitle}</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="shortcut-groups">
          {shortcutGroups(currentPlatform()).map((g) => (
            <section key={g.title} aria-label={g.title}>
              <div className="group-label">{g.title}</div>
              <div className="card shortcut-list">
                {g.rows.map((r) => (
                  <div className="shortcut-row" key={r.label} data-testid="shortcut-row">
                    <span className="sr-label">{r.label}</span>
                    <Kbd keys={r.keys} />
                    <span className="visually-hidden">{r.spoken}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="d-foot">
        <span className="spacer" />
        <Button variant="primary" autoFocus testId="shortcuts-done" onPress={done}>
          {t.common.done}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`tests/e2e/helpers.ts`:
```ts
/** Run `trigger` and return the window it opens (`?window=<kind>`), once its React root has rendered. */
export async function expectWindow(app: ElectronApplication, kind: 'settings' | 'sheet', trigger: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<Page> {
  const opened = app.waitForEvent('window', { timeout: 15_000 });
  await trigger();
  const page = await opened;
  await expect.poll(() => page.url(), { message: `a ${kind} window` }).toContain(`window=${kind}`);
  await page.locator(kind === 'sheet' ? '[data-sheet-root]' : '[data-settings-root]').waitFor();
  return page;
}
```
`tests/e2e/sheets.spec.ts`:
```ts
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { COMMANDS } from '../../packages/domain/src/commands';
import { FIXTURES, expectWindow, importFiles, importPaths, launch, menuItemEnabled, runMenuCommand } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');

test('a duplicate import asks in a sheet: ↩ skips, 새 자료로 추가 adds, esc skips, 기존 자료 열기 opens', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  const rows = s.page.getByTestId('item-row');
  try {
    await importPaths(s.page, [PDF]);
    await expect(rows).toHaveCount(1);

    let sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [PDF]));
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('sheet-root')).toHaveAttribute('data-kind', 'duplicate');
    await expect(sheet.getByRole('heading')).toHaveText('이미 라이브러리에 있는 파일입니다');
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('dup-file')).toHaveText('sample.pdf');
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('dup-card')).toContainText('sample');
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('dup-card')).toContainText('PDF ·');
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('dup-skip')).toBeFocused();
    expect(await menuItemEnabled(s.app, 'file.importManaged')).toBe(false); // the main window waits for the answer
    const closed = sheet.waitForEvent('close');
    await sheet.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await closed;
    await expect(rows).toHaveCount(1);

    sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [PDF]));
    await sheet.getByTestId('dup-add').click();
    await expect(rows).toHaveCount(2);

    sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [PDF]));
    await sheet.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await expect.poll(() => menuItemEnabled(s.app, 'file.importManaged')).toBe(true);
    await expect(rows).toHaveCount(2);

    sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [PDF]));
    await sheet.getByTestId('dup-open').click();
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('viewer-title')).toHaveText('sample');
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('the sheet is a 440 px modal child of the main window, 8 px under the toolbar', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await importPaths(s.page, [PDF]);
    const sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [PDF]));
    const geo = await s.app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => {
      const all = BrowserWindow.getAllWindows();
      const child = all.find((w) => w.getParentWindow() !== null)!;
      const parent = child.getParentWindow()!;
      return { modal: child.isModal(), main: parent.getContentBounds(), sheet: child.getContentBounds() };
    });
    expect(geo.modal).toBe(true);
    expect(geo.sheet.width).toBe(440);
    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {
      expect(geo.sheet.y - geo.main.y).toBe(60);
      expect(Math.abs(geo.sheet.x + geo.sheet.width / 2 - (geo.main.x + geo.main.width / 2))).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    }
    await sheet.keyboard.press('Escape');
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('⌘/ lists every registry shortcut in a sheet; ↩ closes it', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    const sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.shortcuts'));
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('sheet-root')).toHaveAttribute('data-kind', 'shortcuts');
    for (const c of COMMANDS.filter((x) => x.accelerator || x.key)) {
      await expect(sheet.getByTestId('shortcut-row').filter({ hasText: c.label }), c.id).toHaveCount(1);
    }
    await expect(sheet.getByTestId('shortcuts-done')).toBeFocused();
    expect(await menuItemEnabled(s.app, 'app.shortcuts')).toBe(false);
    await sheet.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect.poll(() => menuItemEnabled(s.app, 'app.shortcuts')).toBe(true);
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('sheet IPC refuses a caller that is not an open sheet', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    expect(await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.sheet.payload())).toMatchObject({ ok: false, code: 'PERMISSION_DENIED' });
    expect(await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.sheet.resolve({ kind: 'shortcuts' }))).toMatchObject({ ok: false, code: 'PERMISSION_DENIED' });
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```

```ts
test('sheets: duplicate and shortcuts', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launchTour(testInfo.outputPath());
  try {
    await importPaths(s.page, [path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf')]);
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      const dup = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf')]));
      await shoot(dup, `sheet-duplicate-${theme}`, { mask: [dup.getByTestId('dup-card').locator('.t-footnote')] });
      await dup.keyboard.press('Escape');
      await expect.poll(() => menuItemEnabled(s.app, 'app.shortcuts')).toBe(true);
      const keys = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.shortcuts'));
      await shoot(keys, `sheet-shortcuts-${theme}`);
      await keys.keyboard.press('Escape');
      await expect.poll(() => menuItemEnabled(s.app, 'app.shortcuts')).toBe(true);
    }
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```
(카드의 날짜 줄은 실행 시각에 따라 바뀌므로 마스크한다.)

  - 짝: `sheet-duplicate-{light,dark}.png` ↔ `dialogs-{light,dark}-B.png`(시트 부분만); `sheet-shortcuts-{light,dark}.png` ↔ `dialogs--backup-light-A.png`, `dialogs--backup-dark-B.png`(시트 골격: 제목 줄·배지·카드·아래 단추 줄). 단축키 시트 자체의 목업은 없으므로 명세 6(시트)·7.4(키 맵)·6(키캡)으로 판정한다.
  - 명세 절: 6(시트, 버튼 — 맨 왼쪽 링크형 단추의 음수 여백, 기본 버튼 하나·오른쪽 끝·↩), 7.4, 9.
  - 비평 범위: 시트 폭·안쪽 여백 20·제목 17/22 600 + 32 배지·설명 paragraph·카드·단추 줄 배치·포커스(건너뛰기·완료), 단축키 행 구성(레이블 왼쪽, 키캡 오른쪽, 행 구분선, 묶음 머리). 범위 밖: 시트 둘레의 그림자·모서리(macOS 네이티브 시트가 그린다), "남은 중복에 같은 선택" 체크박스와 진행 토스트(M6).

### Task 7: 리더를 새 크롬으로 — 안내 화면, Finder에서 보기·기본 앱, legacy CSS 폐기

리더 v2는 M7이다. 이 작업은 **지금 리더의 동작(한 쪽 캔버스, 이전/다음 쪽, 배율 50/100/200%, 위치 기억, `<video controls>`)을 그대로** 새 크롬 안에 둔다: 제목은 통합 도구 막대로(뒤로 단추 + 자료 제목 + "PDF · 4.2 MB"), PDF 조작은 44px 리더 도구 막대(`--bg-content`), 쪽은 `--bg-sunken` 바탕 위 `--shadow-page`, 영상은 `--video-stage`. 끝난 상태는 명세 6의 안내로 바꾼다: 원본 없음(M3 카탈로그 10.3 "디자인만 M3"), 재생 불가(10.7, Finder에서 보기 · 기본 앱으로 열기), PDF 열기 실패. 화면의 모든 단추는 레지스트리 명령으로 실행한다(R3). 마지막으로 `legacy.css`와 `strings/legacy.ts`를 지운다 — `styles.css` 폐기 완료.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/domain/src/commands.ts`, `packages/domain/test/commands.test.ts`, `packages/domain/src/ipc-channels.ts`, `packages/domain/src/ipc.ts`
- Modify: `M/ipc/library.ts`
- Modify: `R/viewers/ViewerHost.tsx`, `R/viewers/PdfViewer.tsx`, `R/viewers/VideoViewer.tsx`; Create: `R/viewers/ReaderNotices.tsx`, `R/viewers/ReaderToolbar.tsx`
- Modify: `R/components/FatalScreen.tsx`, `R/app/MainWindow.tsx`, `R/commands/registry.ts`, `R/commands/registry.test.ts`
- Create: `R/strings/reader.ts`, `R/strings/fatal.ts`; Modify: `R/strings/index.ts`, `R/strings/strings.test.ts`
- Create: `R/styles/screens/reader.css`; Modify: `R/styles/index.ts`, `R/styles/layers.css`
- Delete: `R/styles/legacy.css`, `R/strings/legacy.ts`
- Create: `tests/e2e/readers.spec.ts`; Modify: `tests/e2e/first-session.spec.ts`, `tests/evidence/baseline.evidence.ts`, `tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (domain): 명령 `item.showInFolder`(`'Finder에서 보기'`, `labelOff: '폴더에서 보기'`, 맥락 `['item']`, 컨텍스트 메뉴 `{ kinds: ['item'], group: 2, order: 1 }`), `item.openExternally`(`'기본 앱으로 열기'`, `{ kinds: ['item'], group: 2, order: 2 }`) — 둘 다 대상 1개 + 파일 있음일 때만 가용. 가속기는 없다(⇧⌘R은 표 선택이 생기는 M4).
- Produces (IPC): `library.showInFolder({ itemId })`, `library.openExternally({ itemId })` — main이 파일을 다시 확인하고(`refreshItemAvailability`) 경로를 풀어 `shell.showItemInFolder` / `shell.openPath`. 파일이 없으면 `NOT_FOUND`, 열 앱이 없으면 `INTERNAL`(한국어 안내).
- Produces (renderer): `CommandEnv.showInFolder(itemId)`, `CommandEnv.openExternally(itemId)`; `ViewerHost({ itemId })`; `MissingNotice`, `CodecNotice`, `ReaderToolbar({ item })`; testid `viewer-title`(리더 화면의 도구 막대 제목), `viewer-back`, `missing-notice`, `relink`, `missing-back`, `video-error`, `codec-reveal`, `codec-open`, `pdf-error`, `fatal-screen`.

- [ ] **Step 1: 명령과 IPC**

`packages/domain/src/commands.ts`: `CommandId`에 `| 'item.showInFolder' | 'item.openExternally'`, `COMMANDS`의 `item.open` 다음에:
```ts
  { id: 'item.showInFolder', label: 'Finder에서 보기', labelOff: '폴더에서 보기', contexts: ['item'], contextMenu: { kinds: ['item'], group: 2, order: 1 } },
  { id: 'item.openExternally', label: '기본 앱으로 열기', contexts: ['item'], contextMenu: { kinds: ['item'], group: 2, order: 2 } },
```
`RULES`에:
```ts
  'item.showInFolder': (_ctx, t) => t.length === 1 && t[0]!.availability === 'available',
  'item.openExternally': (_ctx, t) => t.length === 1 && t[0]!.availability === 'available',
```
`commands.test.ts`: id 목록에 두 id, 그리고
```ts
  it('Finder에서 보기 / 기본 앱으로 열기 need one item whose file exists', () => {
    for (const id of ['item.showInFolder', 'item.openExternally'] as const) {
      expect(isCommandAvailable(id, LIB, [managedOk])).toBe(true);
      expect(isCommandAvailable(id, LIB, [linkedMissing])).toBe(false);
      expect(isCommandAvailable(id, LIB, [managedOk, managedOk])).toBe(false);
    }
    expect(commandLabel(commandById('item.showInFolder'), 'linux')).toBe('폴더에서 보기');
  });
```
`M/menu/context-menu.test.ts`의 첫 기대값을 바꾼다: 사용 가능한 파일 `['item.open', '-', 'item.showInFolder', 'item.openExternally']`, 없는 외부 파일 `['item.relink', '-', 'item.open', '-', 'item.showInFolder', 'item.openExternally']`(뒤 둘은 비활성). `tests/e2e/platform.spec.ts`의 컨텍스트 메뉴 기대값도 `['열기', 'Finder에서 보기'/'폴더에서 보기', '기본 앱으로 열기']`(구분선 제외 레이블)로.

`ipc-channels.ts`의 `library`에 `showInFolder: 'library:showInFolder', openExternally: 'library:openExternally',`, `ipc.ts`의 `LearnApi.library`에:
```ts
    /** Reveal the item's file in Finder / the file manager. */
    showInFolder(input: ItemIdInput): Promise<Result<void>>;
    /** Open the file with the system's default app. */
    openExternally(input: ItemIdInput): Promise<Result<void>>;
```
`M/ipc/library.ts` 끝에(`import { LearnError } from '@learn/domain'; import { resolveAssetPath } from '@learn/library'; import { shell } from 'electron';` 추가):
```ts
  /** The file on disk for an item, re-checked first (a missing file is reported, never revealed). */
  async function existingFile(itemId: string): Promise<string> {
    const ctx = deps.getContext();
    const item = await refreshItemAvailability(ctx, itemId);
    if (item.availability === 'missing') throw new LearnError('NOT_FOUND', '원본 파일을 찾을 수 없습니다. 파일을 옮겼다면 다시 연결하세요.');
    const asset = ctx.repo.getAsset(item.assetId);
    if (!asset) throw new LearnError('NOT_FOUND', '자료를 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    return resolveAssetPath(ctx.layout, asset);
  }

  handle(Channels.library.showInFolder, itemIdInput, async ({ itemId }) => {
    shell.showItemInFolder(await existingFile(itemId));
  });

  handle(Channels.library.openExternally, itemIdInput, async ({ itemId }) => {
    const failure = await shell.openPath(await existingFile(itemId));
    if (failure) throw new LearnError('INTERNAL', '기본 앱으로 열지 못했습니다. 이 형식을 여는 앱이 설치되어 있는지 확인하세요.');
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 리더 크롬과 안내**

`R/viewers/ReaderToolbar.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { commandById } from '@learn/domain';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { useItem } from '../data/queries';
import { formatSize } from '../format';
import { Toolbar } from '../shell/Toolbar';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { IconButton } from '../ui/Button';

/** Unified toolbar while reading (no tab strip until M7): back to the library (⌘1), title, kind · size. */
export function ReaderToolbar({ itemId }: { itemId: string }) {
  const item = useItem(itemId).data;
  const { run } = useCommands();
  const back = commandById('nav.back');
  const meta = item ? [t.library.type[item.type], formatSize(item.size), item.storageMode === 'linked' ? t.reader.linkedMeta : null].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') : undefined;
  return (
    <Toolbar
      title={item?.title ?? ''}
      titleTestId="viewer-title"
      count={meta}
      leading={<IconButton icon={ChevronLeft} label={back.label} accelerator={back.accelerator} testId="viewer-back" onPress={() => run('nav.back')} />}
    />
  );
}
```
`R/viewers/ReaderNotices.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ExternalLink, FolderSearch, Link2Off, VideoOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { commandById, commandLabel, type LibraryItem } from '@learn/domain';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { formatSize } from '../format';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { Notice } from '../ui/Notice';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';

/** Original missing (design-spec 10.3 "원본 없음", M3 design): warning tile, the path, 다시 연결… for linked files. */
export function MissingNotice({ item, externalPath }: { item: LibraryItem; externalPath: string | null }) {
  const { run, isEnabled } = useCommands();
  const target = { itemIds: [item.id] };
  const linked = item.storageMode === 'linked';
  return (
    <div className="reader-stage">
      <div className="stage-center">
        <Notice
          tone="warning"
          icon={Link2Off}
          title={t.reader.missingTitle}
          file={linked ? (externalPath ?? undefined) : undefined}
          testId="missing-notice"
          actions={
            <>
              {linked && (
                <Button variant="primary" testId="relink" isDisabled={!isEnabled('item.relink', target)} onPress={() => run('item.relink', target)}>
                  {commandById('item.relink').label}
                </Button>
              )}
              <Button testId="missing-back" onPress={() => run('nav.back')}>
                {t.reader.showLibrary}
              </Button>
            </>
          }
        >
          {linked ? t.reader.missingLinkedBody : t.reader.missingManagedBody}
        </Notice>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Unplayable video (dialogs.html?scene=codec): the stage stays dark; Finder에서 보기 · 기본 앱으로 열기. */
export function CodecNotice({ item, unsupported }: { item: LibraryItem; unsupported: boolean }) {
  const { run, isEnabled } = useCommands();
  const target = { itemIds: [item.id] };
  const platform = currentPlatform();
  return (
    <div className="codec-notice" role="alert" data-testid="video-error">
      <div className="glyph">
        <Icon icon={VideoOff} size="xl" />
      </div>
      <h2 className="ui-balance">{unsupported ? t.reader.videoUnsupportedTitle : t.reader.videoErrorTitle}</h2>
      <p className="ui-balance">{unsupported ? t.reader.videoUnsupportedBody : t.reader.videoErrorBody}</p>
      <div className="file">
        {item.originalName} · {formatSize(item.size)}
      </div>
      <div className="actions">
        <Button icon={FolderSearch} testId="codec-reveal" isDisabled={!isEnabled('item.showInFolder', target)} onPress={() => run('item.showInFolder', target)}>
          {commandLabel(commandById('item.showInFolder'), platform)}
        </Button>
        <Button icon={ExternalLink} testId="codec-open" isDisabled={!isEnabled('item.openExternally', target)} onPress={() => run('item.openExternally', target)}>
          {commandById('item.openExternally').label}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`R/viewers/ViewerHost.tsx` 전체:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { FileX } from 'lucide-react';
import { api, unwrap } from '../api';
import { keys, useItem } from '../data/queries';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Notice } from '../ui/Notice';
import { PdfViewer } from './PdfViewer';
import { MissingNotice } from './ReaderNotices';
import { VideoViewer } from './VideoViewer';

/** Picks the reader or the notice for one item. Relink and back go through the registry; the cache follows libraryChanged. */
export function ViewerHost({ itemId }: { itemId: string }) {
  const item = useItem(itemId);
  const missing = item.data?.availability === 'missing';
  const detail = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.detail(itemId), queryFn: () => unwrap(api().library.detail({ itemId })), enabled: missing });
  if (item.isError) {
    return (
      <div className="reader-stage">
        <div className="stage-center">
          <Notice icon={FileX} title={t.reader.openFailedTitle}>
            {item.error.message}
          </Notice>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (!item.data) return null;
  if (missing) return <MissingNotice item={item.data} externalPath={detail.data?.file.externalPath ?? null} />;
  return item.data.type === 'pdf' ? <PdfViewer key={item.data.id} item={item.data} /> : <VideoViewer key={item.data.id} item={item.data} />;
}
```
`R/viewers/PdfViewer.tsx`: **위치 기억·렌더 파이프라인 훅은 M2 그대로 두고** `return (…)`만 바꾼다. `pdf-prev`/`pdf-next`의 `onClick` 식은 M2 코드의 식을 그대로 `onPress`로 옮긴다(M2 b5cefdd: `() => setPageNum((p) => p - 1)` / `+ 1`). `error` 상태에 pdf.js 메시지를 넣던 `setError(\`${t.pdfError} …\`)` 두 곳은 `console.warn(\`[pdf] open/render failed: ${(e as Error)?.name ?? 'Error'}\`); setError('failed');`로 바꾼다(사용자 문장에 기술 문자열을 넣지 않는다).
```tsx
  const total = doc?.numPages ?? 0;
  return (
    <div className="reader">
      <div className="reader-toolbar" role="toolbar" aria-label={t.reader.pdfTools}>
        <IconButton icon={ChevronLeft} label={t.reader.prevPage} testId="pdf-prev" isDisabled={pageNum <= 1} onPress={() => setPageNum((p) => p - 1)} />
        <span className="page-indicator num" data-testid="pdf-page-indicator">
          {total > 0 && restored ? t.reader.page(pageNum, total) : '…'}
        </span>
        <IconButton icon={ChevronRight} label={t.reader.nextPage} testId="pdf-next" isDisabled={pageNum >= total} onPress={() => setPageNum((p) => p + 1)} />
        <span className="spacer" />
        <label className="zoom-select">
          <span>{t.reader.zoom}</span>
          <select data-testid="pdf-zoom" value={String(zoom)} onChange={(e) => setZoom(Number(e.target.value))}>
            {ZOOMS.map((z) => (
              <option key={z} value={String(z)}>
                {z * 100}%
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
      <div className="pdf-stage" tabIndex={0} aria-label={t.reader.pdfStage}>
        {error ? (
          <div className="stage-center">
            <Notice icon={FileX} title={t.reader.pdfErrorTitle} testId="pdf-error">
              {t.reader.pdfErrorBody}
            </Notice>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="pdf-page-wrap">
            <canvas ref={canvasRef} data-testid="pdf-canvas" data-rendered={rendered} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
```
(import: `ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, FileX`(lucide), `IconButton`, `Notice`, `t`(`../strings`).)
`R/viewers/VideoViewer.tsx`: 위치 기억 훅·`<video>`의 이벤트 속성은 M2 그대로. 오류 상태를 `{ unsupported: boolean } | null`로 바꾸고 화면만 교체한다.
```tsx
  const [error, setError] = useState<{ unsupported: boolean } | null>(null);
  const onError = (e: SyntheticEvent<HTMLVideoElement>) => {
    const err = e.currentTarget.error;
    console.warn(`[video] playback error code ${err?.code ?? 0}`);
    setError({ unsupported: err?.code === MediaError.MEDIA_ERR_SRC_NOT_SUPPORTED });
  };
  return (
    <div className="video-stage">
      <video
        ref={(el) => { if (el) videoRef.current = el; }}
        data-testid="video"
        src={assetUrl(item.assetId)}
        controls
        preload="metadata"
        hidden={error !== null}
        onError={onError}
        onLoadedMetadata={(e) => tryRestore(e.currentTarget)}
        onTimeUpdate={(e) => record(e.currentTarget, false)}
        onPause={(e) => record(e.currentTarget, true)}
        onSeeked={(e) => record(e.currentTarget, true)}
      />
      {error && <CodecNotice item={item} unsupported={error.unsupported} />}
    </div>
  );
```
(`<video>`는 숨기기만 하므로 증거 하네스가 `video.error`를 계속 읽을 수 있다.)

`R/components/FatalScreen.tsx` 전체:
```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { DatabaseZap } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../api';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Notice } from '../ui/Notice';

/** The library could not be opened: nothing else to show; the window can still be moved (52 px drag strip). */
export function FatalScreen({ code, message, libraryRoot }: { code: string; message: string; libraryRoot: string | null }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    void api().ui.reportContext({ view: 'library', blocked: true });
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="fatal" data-testid="fatal-screen">
      <div className="fatal-drag" />
      <div className="stage-center">
        <Notice icon={DatabaseZap} title={t.fatal.title} file={libraryRoot ?? undefined} fileTitle={libraryRoot ? `${libraryRoot} (${code})` : code} testId="fatal-notice">
          {`${message} ${t.fatal.hint}`}
        </Notice>
      </div>
      <div data-testid="app-ready" hidden />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--separator);
  }
  .reader-toolbar .page-indicator {
    min-width: 72px; /* local: "12 / 340쪽" without reflow */
    text-align: center;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
  .zoom-select {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-6);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
  .zoom-select select {
    height: var(--control-height-small);
    padding: 0 var(--space-8);
    border: 0;
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    background: var(--bg-control);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e0);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .zoom-select select:focus-visible {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: var(--focus-ring-offset);
  }
  .pdf-stage,
  .reader-stage {
    position: relative;
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-height: 0;
    overflow: auto;
    background: var(--bg-sunken);
  }
  .pdf-stage:focus-visible {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: calc(var(--focus-ring-width) * -1);
  }
  .pdf-page-wrap {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: var(--space-24);
  }
  .pdf-page-wrap canvas {
    background: var(--page-original-bg);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-page);
  }
  .stage-center {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    padding: var(--space-24);
  }
  .video-stage {
    position: relative;
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-height: 0;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: var(--video-stage);
  }
  .video-stage video {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: contain;
  }
  /* The stage is near-black in both themes, so this notice uses fixed light-on-dark values (mockup.css). */
  .codec-notice {
    max-width: 460px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-12);
    text-align: center;
    color: oklch(92% 0.004 250); /* local: always-dark video stage */
  }
  .codec-notice .glyph {
    width: 52px; /* local: mockup codec tile */
    height: 52px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.08); /* local: always-dark video stage */
    color: oklch(85% 0.004 250); /* local: always-dark video stage */
  }
  .codec-notice h2 {
    font: var(--type-title2);
    color: oklch(97% 0 0); /* local: always-dark video stage */
  }
  .codec-notice p {
    font: var(--type-paragraph);
    color: oklch(78% 0.006 250); /* local: always-dark video stage */
  }
  .codec-notice .file {
    padding: var(--space-8) var(--space-12);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.06); /* local: always-dark video stage */
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    color: oklch(78% 0.006 250); /* local: always-dark video stage */
  }
  .codec-notice .actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    margin-top: var(--space-4);
  }
  .codec-notice .btn {
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.12); /* local: always-dark video stage */
    color: oklch(96% 0 0); /* local: always-dark video stage */
    box-shadow: none;
  }
  .codec-notice .btn .icon {
    color: oklch(85% 0 0); /* local: always-dark video stage */
  }
  .fatal {
    position: relative;
    height: 100vh;
    background: var(--bg-window);
  }
  .fatal-drag {
    height: var(--toolbar-height);
    -webkit-app-region: drag;
  }
  .fatal .notice p {
    max-width: 440px;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: E2E**

`tests/e2e/first-session.spec.ts` 127행과 `tests/evidence/baseline.evidence.ts` 272행의 `toContainText('코덱')` → `toContainText('재생할 수 없습니다')`.
`tests/e2e/readers.spec.ts`:
```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test, type ElectronApplication } from '@playwright/test';
import { COMMANDS, MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS, type CommandId } from '../../packages/domain/src/commands';
import { FIXTURES, importPaths, launch, menuSnapshot, openItem, recordedPopups, stubContextMenu, stubOpenDialog, type MenuNode } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');
const MPEG4 = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample-mpeg4part2.mp4');

/** Record shell calls in main instead of opening Finder / another app. */
async function stubShell(app: ElectronApplication): Promise<void> {
  await app.evaluate(({ shell }) => {
    const g = globalThis as { __learnShell?: Array<[string, string]> };
    g.__learnShell = [];
    shell.showItemInFolder = (p: string) => void g.__learnShell!.push(['reveal', p]);
    shell.openPath = async (p: string) => {
      g.__learnShell!.push(['open', p]);
      return '';
    };
  });
}
const shellCalls = (app: ElectronApplication) => app.evaluate(() => (globalThis as { __learnShell?: Array<[string, string]> }).__learnShell ?? []);

test('reader chrome: back button and ⌘1 path, title and kind · size in the toolbar', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await importPaths(s.page, [PDF]);
    await openItem(s.page, 'sample');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('viewer-title')).toHaveText('sample');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('toolbar-count')).toHaveText(/^PDF · \d/);
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('pdf-page-indicator')).toHaveText('1 / 3쪽');
    await s.page.getByTestId('viewer-back').click();
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('library-table')).toBeVisible();
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('unplayable video: notice with Finder에서 보기 and 기본 앱으로 열기 through the registry', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await stubShell(s.app);
    await importPaths(s.page, [MPEG4]);
    await openItem(s.page, 'sample-mpeg4part2');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('video-error')).toContainText('재생할 수 없습니다');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('video-error')).toContainText('sample-mpeg4part2.mp4 ·');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('codec-reveal')).toHaveText(process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'Finder에서 보기' : '폴더에서 보기');
    await s.page.getByTestId('codec-reveal').click();
    await s.page.getByTestId('codec-open').click();
    await expect.poll(async () => (await shellCalls(s.app)).map(([k]) => k)).toEqual(['reveal', 'open']);
    const [[, revealed]] = await shellCalls(s.app);
    expect(revealed).toMatch(/assets/); // the managed copy inside the library
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('missing linked original: 다시 연결… first in the context menu and in the notice; 라이브러리에서 보기 goes back', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const src = testInfo.outputPath('src');
  fs.mkdirSync(src, { recursive: true });
  const file = path.join(src, 'linked.pdf');
  fs.copyFileSync(PDF, file);
  const s = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await s.page.evaluate((p) => window.learn.library.import({ paths: [p], mode: 'linked' }), file);
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(1);
    const moved = path.join(src, 'moved.pdf');
    fs.renameSync(file, moved);
    await stubContextMenu(s.app, null);
    await s.page.getByTestId('item-row').click({ button: 'right' });
    await expect.poll(async () => (await recordedPopups(s.app)).length).toBe(1);
    const [items] = await recordedPopups(s.app);
    expect(items!.filter((i) => i.type !== 'separator').map((i) => [i.label, i.enabled])).toEqual([
      ['다시 연결…', true],
      ['열기', true],
      [process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'Finder에서 보기' : '폴더에서 보기', false],
      ['기본 앱으로 열기', false],
    ]);
    await openItem(s.page, 'linked');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('missing-notice')).toContainText('원본 파일을 찾을 수 없습니다');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('missing-notice')).toContainText('linked.pdf');
    await s.page.getByTestId('missing-back').click();
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('library-table')).toBeVisible();
    await openItem(s.page, 'linked');
    await stubOpenDialog(s.app, [moved]);
    await s.page.getByTestId('relink').click();
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('pdf-canvas')).toHaveAttribute('data-rendered', /^[1-9]\d*$/);
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('a library that cannot be opened shows the fatal notice and disables every registry command', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const lib = testInfo.outputPath('library');
  fs.mkdirSync(lib, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(lib, 'library.sqlite'), 'not a database');
  const s = await launch(lib, testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('fatal-notice')).toContainText('라이브러리를 열 수 없습니다');
    const flat = (nodes: MenuNode[]): MenuNode[] => nodes.flatMap((n) => [n, ...flat(n.submenu ?? [])]);
    const registry = new Set<string>(COMMANDS.map((c) => c.id));
    const enabled = flat(await menuSnapshot(s.app)).filter((n) => n.id !== null && registry.has(n.id) && n.enabled && !MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS.has(n.id as CommandId));
    expect(enabled.map((n) => n.id)).toEqual([]);
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```
(M2 도우미 `recordedPopups`의 항목에는 `type`이 있다. 라이브러리 DB 파일 이름 `library.sqlite`는 `packages/library/src/layout.ts`의 `dbFile`이다.)

### Task 8: Mac 창 크롬·창 상태 기억·About — macOS 증거 1회

명세 2.1: `titleBarStyle: 'hidden'`, `trafficLightPosition: {x: 18, y: 18}`(러너로 확인), `vibrancy: 'sidebar'`, Linux/Windows `titleBarOverlay`(색은 `tokens.generated.ts`). 흰 화면 깜빡임 없는 테마별 배경, 창 크기·위치 기억(Electron 44 `windowStatePersistence` — 이 계획 작성 중 Linux/Xvfb에서 재시작 후 bounds가 정확히 복원됨을 확인), About 패널, 드래그 영역, 전체 화면·비활성 창 표시. 끝에 macOS 러너 1회(`evidence`)로 신호등 위치·비브런시(투명 배경 필요 여부)·메뉴 막대를 확인하고 보정한다.

**비브런시와 창 배경(위험 대응, 이 작업이 확정):** macOS에서 비브런시를 켜면 창 배경을 `'#00000000'`으로 두고 페이지는 사이드바와 `body`만 투명하게 그린다(`<html data-vibrancy="1">`). 첫 프레임은 흰색이 아니라 `nativeTheme`을 따르는 사이드바 재질이다. 다른 면(도구 막대·내용·떠 있는 면)은 모두 불투명 토큰 색이다. `LEARN_VIBRANCY=0`이면 끄고 불투명 `windowBackground[theme]`을 쓴다(증거 비교용·문제 시 대체).

**Files:**
- Create: `M/window-options.ts`, `M/window-options.test.ts`, `M/about.ts`, `M/about.test.ts`
- Modify: `M/window.ts`, `M/theme.ts`, `M/index.ts`, `M/menu/app-menu.ts`, `M/menu/app-menu.test.ts`
- Create: `R/shell/useWindowState.ts`; Modify: `R/app/MainWindow.tsx`, `R/styles/screens/shell.css`
- Create: `tests/evidence/shell.evidence.ts`; Modify: `tests/evidence/capture.ts`, `tests/evidence/spikes.evidence.ts`, `tests/e2e/shell.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/platform.spec.ts`
- Modify: `docs/mac-verification.md`, `apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/design/tokens.ts`(보정이 필요할 때만)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (main): `interface ChromeOptions { platform: NodeJS.Platform; theme: 'light' | 'dark'; vibrancy: boolean }`, `mainWindowChrome(o): Pick<BrowserWindowConstructorOptions, 'backgroundColor' | 'titleBarStyle' | 'trafficLightPosition' | 'vibrancy' | 'visualEffectState' | 'titleBarOverlay'>`, `applyChromeTheme(win, o)`, `shouldUseVibrancy(platform, env): boolean`; `aboutPanelOptions(version, electronVersion)`; `createMainWindow({ preload, url, chrome })`(이름 `'main'`, `windowStatePersistence: { bounds: true, displayMode: true }`). Linux/Windows 도움말 메뉴 끝에 `Learn 정보`(role about).
- Produces (renderer): `useWindowState()` — `<html data-fullscreen>`, `<html data-window-inactive>`(M2 `windowState` 푸시 + 시작 시 `ui.windowState()` 조회). CSS 변수 `--titlebar-inset`(macOS·전체 화면 아님·사이드바 접힘: 84), `--titlebar-controls`(Linux/Windows 창 조절 단추 폭, `env(titlebar-area-*)`).
- Produces (evidence): `tests/evidence/shell.evidence.ts`(`@evidence`), `evidence-results/m3-shell.json`; `capture.ts`에서 `captureOpenMenu`, `pngPixels`, `pngCentroid` export.

- [ ] **Step 1: 창 옵션 — 실패하는 테스트** `M/window-options.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { titleBarOverlay, trafficLightPosition, windowBackground } from '../renderer/src/design/tokens.generated';
import { applyChromeTheme, mainWindowChrome, shouldUseVibrancy } from './window-options';

describe('main window chrome (design-spec 2.1)', () => {
  it('macOS: hidden title bar, traffic lights at the token position, sidebar vibrancy over a clear background', () => {
    expect(mainWindowChrome({ platform: 'darwin', theme: 'dark', vibrancy: true })).toEqual({
      titleBarStyle: 'hidden',
      trafficLightPosition: { ...trafficLightPosition },
      vibrancy: 'sidebar',
      visualEffectState: 'followWindow',
      backgroundColor: '#00000000',
    });
  });
  it('macOS without vibrancy: the opaque window colour of the theme', () => {
    expect(mainWindowChrome({ platform: 'darwin', theme: 'dark', vibrancy: false })).toEqual({ titleBarStyle: 'hidden', trafficLightPosition: { ...trafficLightPosition }, backgroundColor: windowBackground.dark });
  });
  it('Linux/Windows: window controls overlay in the 52 px bar, token colours, themed background', () => {
    expect(mainWindowChrome({ platform: 'linux', theme: 'light', vibrancy: false })).toEqual({ titleBarStyle: 'hidden', titleBarOverlay: { ...titleBarOverlay.light }, backgroundColor: windowBackground.light });
    expect(titleBarOverlay.light.height).toBe(52);
  });
  it('re-applies the theme to a live window', () => {
    const win = { setBackgroundColor: vi.fn(), setTitleBarOverlay: vi.fn() };
    applyChromeTheme(win, { platform: 'linux', theme: 'dark', vibrancy: false });
    expect(win.setBackgroundColor).toHaveBeenCalledWith(windowBackground.dark);
    expect(win.setTitleBarOverlay).toHaveBeenCalledWith(titleBarOverlay.dark);
    const mac = { setBackgroundColor: vi.fn(), setTitleBarOverlay: vi.fn() };
    applyChromeTheme(mac, { platform: 'darwin', theme: 'dark', vibrancy: true });
    expect(mac.setBackgroundColor).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); // the vibrancy material follows nativeTheme itself
    expect(mac.setTitleBarOverlay).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('vibrancy is macOS only and can be turned off for comparisons', () => {
    expect(shouldUseVibrancy('darwin', {})).toBe(true);
    expect(shouldUseVibrancy('darwin', { LEARN_VIBRANCY: '0' })).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldUseVibrancy('linux', {})).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 구현** `M/window-options.ts`

```ts
import type { BrowserWindow, BrowserWindowConstructorOptions } from 'electron';
import { titleBarOverlay, trafficLightPosition, windowBackground } from '../renderer/src/design/tokens.generated';

export interface ChromeOptions {
  platform: NodeJS.Platform;
  theme: 'light' | 'dark';
  vibrancy: boolean;
}

type Chrome = Pick<BrowserWindowConstructorOptions, 'backgroundColor' | 'titleBarStyle' | 'trafficLightPosition' | 'vibrancy' | 'visualEffectState' | 'titleBarOverlay'>;

/** design-spec 2.1. The first frame is the theme colour (or the sidebar material on macOS), never white. */
export function mainWindowChrome(o: ChromeOptions): Chrome {
  if (o.platform === 'darwin') {
    const base: Chrome = { titleBarStyle: 'hidden', trafficLightPosition: { ...trafficLightPosition } };
    // Vibrancy only shows through a clear window background; the page keeps its sidebar and body transparent.
    return o.vibrancy ? { ...base, vibrancy: 'sidebar', visualEffectState: 'followWindow', backgroundColor: '#00000000' } : { ...base, backgroundColor: windowBackground[o.theme] };
  }
  return { titleBarStyle: 'hidden', titleBarOverlay: { ...titleBarOverlay[o.theme] }, backgroundColor: windowBackground[o.theme] };
}

/** Keeps the native frame in step with nativeTheme (overlay colours and background; macOS vibrancy follows by itself). */
export function applyChromeTheme(win: Pick<BrowserWindow, 'setBackgroundColor' | 'setTitleBarOverlay'>, o: ChromeOptions): void {
  if (o.platform === 'darwin') {
    if (!o.vibrancy) win.setBackgroundColor(windowBackground[o.theme]);
    return;
  }
  win.setBackgroundColor(windowBackground[o.theme]);
  win.setTitleBarOverlay({ ...titleBarOverlay[o.theme] });
}

export const shouldUseVibrancy = (platform: NodeJS.Platform, env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): boolean => platform === 'darwin' && env['LEARN_VIBRANCY'] !== '0';
```

```ts
import type { AboutPanelOptionsOptions } from 'electron';

/** Bundled third-party code and fonts (README "런타임 의존성 라이선스"). macOS shows `credits` in the panel. */
const CREDITS = [
  '글꼴: Pretendard Variable — SIL Open Font License 1.1',
  '아이콘: Lucide — ISC License',
  '컴포넌트: React Aria Components — Apache License 2.0',
  'PDF: PDF.js — Apache License 2.0',
  'React, react-resizable-panels, TanStack Query, zustand, Electron, better-sqlite3 — MIT License',
].join('\n');

export function aboutPanelOptions(version: string, electronVersion: string): AboutPanelOptionsOptions {
  return { applicationName: 'Learn', applicationVersion: version, version: `Electron ${electronVersion}`, copyright: '© 2026 Learn', credits: CREDITS };
}
```
`M/menu/app-menu.ts`의 `help`를 `submenu: join(reg('help'), mac ? [] : [{ role: 'about', label: 'Learn 정보' }])`로. `app-menu.test.ts`에 `it('Linux and Windows get Learn 정보 at the end of 도움말', …)`(`shape(build('linux')).find((m) => m.id === 'menu.help')!.submenu!.map((s) => s.label ?? s.type)` = `['단축키 도움말', 'separator', 'Learn 정보']`). `platform.spec.ts`의 도움말 기대값을 `IS_MAC ? [[null, '단축키 도움말']] : [[null, '단축키 도움말'], ['-', ''], ['about', 'Learn 정보']]`로.

`M/window.ts`의 `MainWindowOptions`에 `chrome: ChromeOptions;`를 더하고 `new BrowserWindow({...})`를 다음으로 바꾼다(`webPreferences`·이벤트·시트 오프셋은 그대로).
```ts

    // Electron 44: bounds and maximized/fullscreen restored across restarts (stored per name in userData).
    name: 'main',

    height: 900,

```
`M/theme.ts`에:
```ts
/** 'light' | 'dark' as the window frame should be drawn now. */
export const currentTheme = (): 'light' | 'dark' => (nativeTheme.shouldUseDarkColors ? 'dark' : 'light');
/** Runs `cb` whenever the effective theme changes (prefs or the system appearance). */
export function onThemeChange(cb: () => void): void {
  nativeTheme.on('updated', cb);
}
```
`M/index.ts`:
```ts
const vibrancy = shouldUseVibrancy(process.platform, process.env);
const urlOptions = { platform: process.platform, deterministic, vibrancy, demo: false };
const chrome = (): ChromeOptions => ({ platform: process.platform, theme: currentTheme(), vibrancy });
```
`openMainWindow`에서 `createMainWindow({ preload, url: windowUrl(rendererBase, 'main', urlOptions), chrome: chrome() })`. `whenReady` 안 `applyTheme(...)` 다음:
```ts
    app.setAboutPanelOptions(aboutPanelOptions(app.getVersion(), process.versions.electron));
    onThemeChange(() => {
      for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) if (win === mainWindow) applyChromeTheme(win, chrome());
    });
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 렌더러 — 창 상태 표시와 CSS**

`R/shell/useWindowState.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { LearnEventMap } from '@learn/domain';
import { api } from '../api';
import { useLearnEvent } from '../events';

const apply = ({ fullscreen, focused }: LearnEventMap['windowState']) => {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  root.toggleAttribute('data-fullscreen', fullscreen); // no traffic lights → no 84 px inset
  root.toggleAttribute('data-window-inactive', !focused); // selections fall back to the neutral fill
};

/** Initial state from ui.windowState() (M2), then main's windowState pushes. */
export function useWindowState(): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    void api().ui.windowState().then((r) => {
      if (r.ok) apply(r.data);
    });
  }, []);
  useLearnEvent('windowState', apply);
}
```
`R/app/MainWindow.tsx` 첫 줄들에 `useWindowState();`.
`R/styles/screens/shell.css`에 추가:
```css
  /* macOS: the traffic lights sit in the toolbar while the sidebar is hidden (84 px), not in full screen. */
  :root[data-platform='darwin']:not([data-fullscreen]) .window[data-sidebar='hidden'] .titlebar {
    --titlebar-inset: var(--traffic-light-inset);
  }
  :root[data-fullscreen] .sidebar-top {
    padding-left: var(--space-12);
  }
  /* Linux/Windows: room for the window controls overlay at the right end (0 on macOS: no env values). */
  .titlebar {
    --titlebar-controls: calc(100vw - env(titlebar-area-x, 0px) - env(titlebar-area-width, 100vw));
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: E2E(Linux) — 크롬** `tests/e2e/shell.spec.ts`에 추가

```ts
test('frame: themed background (no white flash) that follows the theme; overlay room on Linux; drag regions', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const ud = testInfo.outputPath('userdata');
  fs.mkdirSync(ud, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ud, 'preferences.json'), JSON.stringify({ theme: 'dark' }));
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), ud);
  const bg = () => app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.getBackgroundColor().toLowerCase());
  try {
    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') {
      expect(await bg()).toBe('#242527'); // windowBackground.dark before the first paint
      await page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ theme: 'light' }));
      await expect.poll(bg).toBe('#f6f7f8');
      const room = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const probe = document.createElement('div');
        probe.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:env(titlebar-area-x,0px);width:env(titlebar-area-width,100vw)';
        document.body.append(probe);
        const r = probe.getBoundingClientRect();
        probe.remove();
        return { controls: innerWidth - (r.left + r.width), padding: parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('[data-testid="toolbar"]')!).paddingRight) };
      });
      expect(room.controls).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(room.padding).toBeCloseTo(12 + room.controls, 0);
    }
    const region = (sel: string) => page.locator(sel).first().evaluate((el) => getComputedStyle(el).getPropertyValue('-webkit-app-region') || getComputedStyle(el).getPropertyValue('app-region'));
    expect(await region('[data-testid="toolbar"]')).toBe('drag');
    expect(await region('[data-testid="sidebar-top"]')).toBe('drag');
    expect(await region('[data-testid="sidebar-toggle"]')).toBe('no-drag');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('window bounds survive a restart (windowStatePersistence)', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const [lib, ud] = dirs(testInfo);
  let s = await launch(lib, ud);
  const bounds = { x: 40, y: 40, width: 1200, height: 760 };
  await s.app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }, b) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.setBounds(b), bounds);
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(1500);
  await s.app.close();
  s = await launch(lib, ud);
  try {
    const restored = await s.app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.getBounds());
    expect(restored.width).toBe(bounds.width);
    expect(restored.height).toBe(bounds.height);
    expect(Math.abs(restored.x - bounds.x)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(Math.abs(restored.y - bounds.y)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30); // a macOS menu bar may push it down
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});

test('full screen drops the traffic-light inset (data-fullscreen)', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    await app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.setFullScreen(true));
    await expect(page.locator('html')).toHaveAttribute('data-fullscreen', '');
    await app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.setFullScreen(false));
    await expect(page.locator('html')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-fullscreen', '');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```
(`import fs from 'node:fs';` 추가. 전체 화면 테스트는 macOS에서 전환 애니메이션이 끝날 때까지 기다리므로(`toHaveAttribute` 15초) M0의 빠른 토글 멈춤 조건(진입 직후 해제)을 피한다.)
기존 투어 기준 PNG는 Linux 도구 막대 오른쪽 여백(창 조절 단추 자리)이 바뀌므로 모두 다시 만든다: `pnpm e2e:xvfb tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts --update-snapshots`.

- [ ] **Step 7: macOS 증거 테스트** — 먼저 `tests/evidence/spikes.evidence.ts`의 `menuBarNames`, `pressEscape`, `captureOpenMenu`를 **본문 그대로** `tests/evidence/capture.ts`로 옮겨 export하고(spikes는 import), `capture.ts`에 PNG 읽기 도우미를 더한다.

```ts
/** RGB at points of a PNG (device pixels), decoded by the app page's own canvas (no image library needed). */
export async function pngPixels(page: Page, rel: string, points: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>): Promise<number[][]> {
  const b64 = fs.readFileSync(path.join(EVIDENCE_ROOT, rel)).toString('base64');
  return page.evaluate(async ({ b64, points }) => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.src = `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`;
    await img.decode();
    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = img.naturalWidth;
    c.height = img.naturalHeight;
    const g = c.getContext('2d')!;
    g.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
    return points.map(([x, y]) => [...g.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data.slice(0, 3)]);
  }, { b64, points });
}

/** Centroid (device px) and size of the pixels in `box` whose RGB satisfies `match` (e.g. the red close button). */
export async function pngCentroid(page: Page, rel: string, box: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, match: 'red' | 'green'): Promise<{ x: number; y: number; n: number; width: number } | null> {
  const b64 = fs.readFileSync(path.join(EVIDENCE_ROOT, rel)).toString('base64');
  return page.evaluate(async ({ b64, box, match }) => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.src = `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`;
    await img.decode();
    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = img.naturalWidth;
    c.height = img.naturalHeight;
    const g = c.getContext('2d')!;
    g.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
    const d = g.getImageData(box.x, box.y, box.w, box.h).data;
    // Fixed predicates (the page's CSP has no unsafe-eval): macOS close (#ff5f57-ish) and zoom (#28c840-ish) buttons.
    const test = (r: number, g: number, b: number) => (match === 'red' ? r > 200 && g > 60 && g < 130 && b > 50 && b < 120 : r < 120 && g > 150 && b < 120);
    let sx = 0, sy = 0, n = 0;
    for (let y = 0; y < box.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < box.w; x++) {
      const i = (y * box.w + x) * 4;
      if (test(d[i]!, d[i + 1]!, d[i + 2]!)) { sx += x; sy += y; n++; }
    }
    return n ? { x: box.x + sx / n, y: box.y + sy / n, n, width: img.naturalWidth } : null;
  }, { b64, box, match });
}
```

`tests/evidence/shell.evidence.ts`:
```ts
// @evidence — M3 window chrome on macOS: traffic lights in the 52 px bar, sidebar vibrancy, hidden title bar,
// Korean menu bar, sheets under the toolbar, real accelerators, window state. Linux CI runs it with captures skipped.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test, type ElectronApplication, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { FIXTURES, NativeInputNotPermitted, expectWindow, importFiles, importPaths, launch, menuSnapshot, nativeKeystroke, openItem, runMenuCommand, type Session } from '../e2e/helpers';
import { IS_MAC, allShots, captureOpenMenu, fullScreenShot, nativeWindowShot, platformFonts, pngCentroid, pngPixels, writeJson } from './capture';

const TAG = { tag: '@evidence' };
const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');
const MP4 = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample-h264-aac.mp4');
const MPEG4 = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample-mpeg4part2.mp4');
const results: Record<string, unknown> = {};
const save = () => writeJson('m3-shell.json', { ...results, platform: `${process.platform}-${process.arch}`, at: new Date().toISOString() }, 'M3 shell chrome measurements');

let tmp = '';
let s: Session;
const fresh = async (env: Record<string, string> = {}) => {
  tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'learn-m3-'));
  return launch(path.join(tmp, 'library'), path.join(tmp, 'userdata'), { env });
};
const windowWidth = (app: ElectronApplication) => app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.getBounds().width);

test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' });
test.beforeAll(async () => {
  s = await fresh();
});
test.afterAll(async () => {
  await s?.app.close().catch(() => {});
  if (tmp) fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('M3 chrome: empty library — traffic lights centred in the 52 px bar inside the 84 px inset', TAG, async () => {
  const [, win] = await allShots(s.app, s.page, 'm3-01-empty', 'empty library, layout B, light');
  results['fonts'] = await platformFonts(s.page, '[data-testid="toolbar-title"]');
  if (IS_MAC && win?.file) {
    const red = await pngCentroid(s.page, win.file, { x: 0, y: 0, w: 120, h: 60 }, 'red');
    const green = await pngCentroid(s.page, win.file, { x: 0, y: 0, w: 120, h: 60 }, 'green');
    const px = red ? red.width / (await windowWidth(s.app)) : 1;
    results['trafficLights'] = { red, green, devicePixelsPerPoint: px, expect: { redCentre: [24, 26], greenRightEdgeBelow: 84 } };
    expect.soft(red, 'close button found').not.toBeNull();
    if (red) expect.soft(Math.abs(red.y / px - 26), 'close button centred in the 52 px bar').toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.5);
    if (green) expect.soft(green.x / px + 6, 'lights inside the 84 px inset').toBeLessThan(84);
  }
  save();
});

test('M3 chrome: library light/dark and vibrancy on/off', TAG, async () => {
  await importPaths(s.page, [PDF, MP4, MPEG4]);
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(3);
  const [, light] = await allShots(s.app, s.page, 'm3-02-library-light', 'library, light');
  await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ theme: 'dark' }));
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(500);
  await allShots(s.app, s.page, 'm3-03-library-dark', 'library, dark');
  await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ theme: 'light' }));
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(500);
  if (IS_MAC && light?.file) {
    const opaque = await fresh({ LEARN_VIBRANCY: '0' });
    try {
      await importPaths(opaque.page, [PDF, MP4, MPEG4]);
      const shot = await nativeWindowShot(opaque.app, 'm3-02-library-light-opaque', 'library, light, LEARN_VIBRANCY=0');
      if (shot.file) {
        const points = [[120, 500], [700, 500]] as const; // sidebar, content (points; runner is 1x)
        const [withVib, without] = [await pngPixels(s.page, light.file, points), await pngPixels(s.page, shot.file, points)];
        const delta = (a: number[], b: number[]) => Math.max(...a.map((v, i) => Math.abs(v - b[i]!)));
        results['vibrancy'] = { sidebar: { vibrancy: withVib[0], opaque: without[0] }, content: { vibrancy: withVib[1], opaque: without[1] }, sidebarDelta: delta(withVib[0]!, without[0]!), contentDelta: delta(withVib[1]!, without[1]!) };
        expect.soft(delta(withVib[1]!, without[1]!), 'content stays opaque').toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
      }
    } finally {
      await opaque.app.close();
    }
  }
  save();
});

test('M3 chrome: collapsed sidebar, PDF, unplayable video, sheets', TAG, async () => {
  await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
  await allShots(s.app, s.page, 'm3-04-collapsed', 'sidebar hidden: traffic lights over the toolbar, 84 px inset');
  await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
  await openItem(s.page, 'sample');
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('pdf-canvas')).toHaveAttribute('data-rendered', /^[1-9]\d*$/);
  await allShots(s.app, s.page, 'm3-05-pdf', 'PDF in the new chrome');
  await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'nav.back');
  await openItem(s.page, 'sample-mpeg4part2');
  await expect(s.page.getByTestId('video-error')).toBeVisible();
  await allShots(s.app, s.page, 'm3-06-codec', 'unplayable video notice');
  await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'nav.back');
  const dup = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => importFiles(s, [PDF]));
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(800); // sheet slide-in
  await fullScreenShot('m3-07-sheet-duplicate', 'duplicate sheet attached under the toolbar');
  await dup.keyboard.press('Escape');
  const keys = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.shortcuts'));
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(800);
  await fullScreenShot('m3-08-sheet-shortcuts', '⌘/ shortcut sheet');
  await keys.keyboard.press('Escape');
});

test('M3 chrome: Korean menu bar and the open 보기 menu', TAG, async () => {
  results['menu'] = (await menuSnapshot(s.app)).map((m) => m.label);
  if (IS_MAC) results['menuView'] = await captureOpenMenu(s.app, 'Electron', 5, 'm3-09-menu-view', '보기 menu open');
  save();
});

test('M3 chrome: real ⌃⌘S and ⌘1 reach the native menu (System Events)', TAG, async () => {
  test.skip(!IS_MAC, 'OS-level keystrokes are macOS only');
  try {
    await nativeKeystroke(s.app, 's', ['control', 'command']);
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('shell')).toHaveAttribute('data-sidebar', 'hidden');
    await nativeKeystroke(s.app, 's', ['control', 'command']);
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('shell')).toHaveAttribute('data-sidebar', 'shown');
    await openItem(s.page, 'sample');
    await nativeKeystroke(s.app, '1', ['command']);
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('library-table')).toBeVisible();
    results['nativeAccelerators'] = 'ok';
  } catch (e) {
    if (!(e instanceof NativeInputNotPermitted)) throw e;
    results['nativeAccelerators'] = `not permitted: ${e.message}`;
  }
  save();
});

test('M3 chrome: window bounds survive a restart on macOS', TAG, async () => {
  const lib = path.join(tmp, 'library');
  const ud = path.join(tmp, 'userdata');
  const bounds = { x: 40, y: 60, width: 1000, height: 640 };
  await s.app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }, b) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.setBounds(b), bounds);
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(1500);
  await s.app.close();
  s = await launch(lib, ud);
  results['windowState'] = { requested: bounds, restored: await s.app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]!.getBounds()) };
  save();
});
```
(`devicePixelsPerPoint`는 PNG 폭 ÷ 창 폭(pt) — 러너는 1, Retina 실기는 2.)

- [ ] **Step 9: macOS 러너 1회(M3 첫 번째)**

```bash

```
Expected: 녹색. `evidence/m3-chrome/<run>/evidence/evidence-results/m3-shell.json`과 `m3-chrome/m3-0*.window.png`·`.screen.png`. 확인할 것과 대응:
1. `trafficLights.red` 중심 y가 26 ± 1.5(포인트)가 아니면 `tokens.ts`의 `layout.trafficLightY`를 `18 + (26 − 측정 y)`로 고치고 `pnpm tokens` → 커밋(검증은 Task 14 실행). x도 같은 방식으로 24 기준.
2. `vibrancy.sidebarDelta`가 2 이하이면(사이드바가 불투명 대체색 그대로) 비브런시가 보이지 않는 것이다: `m3-02-library-light.window.png`로 확인한 뒤 `shouldUseVibrancy`의 기본을 끄고(불투명 `--bg-sidebar`, 명세의 대체값) 그 사실을 기록한다. `transparent: true` 창(그림자·크기 조절 동작이 달라진다)을 시험하려면 macOS 실행이 하나 더 필요하므로 소유자에게 묻는다. `contentDelta`가 2를 넘으면 내용 면이 비치는 것이므로 그 면의 배경을 불투명 토큰으로 고친다.
3. `menu` 레이블이 `Learn(앱 이름은 비패키지에서 Electron) 파일 편집 보기 이동 윈도우 도움말`인지, `m3-09-menu-view-*.screen.png`에 "사이드바 보기/숨기기 ⌃⌘S"가 보이는지.
4. `nativeAccelerators`가 `ok`인지(아니면 기록만 하고 Task 14에서 다시 본다).
5. `windowState.restored`가 요청과 같은지. 다르면 `windowStatePersistence`가 macOS에서 동작하지 않는 것이므로 Step 10의 대체 구현을 넣는다.
`docs/mac-verification.md`에 "M3 창 크롬(run <id>)" 절을 더한다: 실행 표 한 줄(분), 위 다섯 값, 보정 내용. macOS 분 합계를 갱신한다.

- [ ] **Step 10: (Step 9의 5번이 실패했을 때만) 창 상태를 환경설정에 직접 저장**

`M/window-state.ts`:
```ts
import { screen, type BrowserWindow, type Rectangle } from 'electron';
import type { PrefsStore } from './prefs';

/** Fallback for windowStatePersistence: bounds in preferences.json under `window`, clamped onto a visible display. */
export function restoreBounds(prefs: PrefsStore): Rectangle | null {
  const saved = (prefs.get() as { window?: Rectangle }).window;
  if (!saved) return null;
  const area = screen.getDisplayMatching(saved).workArea;
  const width = Math.min(saved.width, area.width);
  const height = Math.min(saved.height, area.height);
  return { width, height, x: Math.min(Math.max(saved.x, area.x), area.x + area.width - width), y: Math.min(Math.max(saved.y, area.y), area.y + area.height - height) };
}

export function rememberBounds(win: BrowserWindow, save: (b: Rectangle) => void): void {
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  const later = () => {
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => !win.isDestroyed() && !win.isFullScreen() && save(win.getNormalBounds()), 500);
  };
  win.on('resize', later);
  win.on('move', later);
  win.on('close', () => !win.isFullScreen() && save(win.getNormalBounds()));
}
```
이 경우 `prefsSchema`에 `window: z.object({ x, y, width, height: z.number().int() }).nullable().default(null)`을 더하는 M2 도메인 변경이 필요하다(`prefsSetInput`에서는 뺀다 — 렌더러가 쓰지 않는다). `window.ts`에서 `name`/`windowStatePersistence`를 지우고 `restoreBounds`의 값을 `x/y/width/height`로 넘기고 `rememberBounds(win, (b) => prefs.set({ window: b }))`. 이 단계를 쓰면 ADR-0007에 한 줄을 남긴다.

### Task 9: 명령 팔레트(⌘K)·실행기·초성 검색·IME

명세 10.5(M3): 명령 결과, 키 힌트 줄, 초성 검색, 결과 없음. 자료·컬렉션 결과는 M4·M5다. 팔레트는 레지스트리의 `inPalette` 명령 중 **지금 가용한 것만** 보여 주고(가짜 금지), 레이블 → 검색어(keywords) 순으로 찾는다. 실행기(⌘K 필드 모양 단추)는 사이드바 y 52–80(좌우 8, 돋보기가 행 아이콘 열 x 24)에, 사이드바를 접으면 도구 막대 오른쪽 끝(232×28)에 있다(레이아웃 B 불변 조건). 한국어 조합 중의 Enter·Escape는 아무것도 실행하거나 닫지 않는다.
