```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { initialOf, isChoseong, matchKorean, splitFinal } from './hangul';

describe('Korean matching for the palette (ADR-0005 §5)', () => {
  it('knows initial consonants and syllables', () => {
    expect(initialOf('가')).toBe('ㄱ');
    expect(initialOf('져')).toBe('ㅈ');
    expect(initialOf('a')).toBe('a');
    expect(isChoseong('ㄲ')).toBe(true);
    expect(isChoseong('ㅏ')).toBe(false);
    expect(splitFinal('갖')).toEqual(['가', 'ㅈ']);
    expect(splitFinal('가')).toBeNull();
    expect(splitFinal('값')).toBeNull(); // ㅄ cannot start a syllable
  });
  it('matches 초성 sequences, whole syllables, and both mixed', () => {
    expect(matchKorean('ㄱㅈ', '파일 가져오기…')).toEqual({ start: 3, end: 5, kind: 'initials' });
    expect(matchKorean('ㅅㅇㄷㅂ', '사이드바 보기/숨기기')).toEqual({ start: 0, end: 4, kind: 'initials' });
    expect(matchKorean('가ㅈ', '파일 가져오기…')).toEqual({ start: 3, end: 5, kind: 'initials' });
  });
  it('ranks literal matches: exact, prefix, word start, substring', () => {
    expect(matchKorean('파일 가져오기…', '파일 가져오기…')?.kind).toBe('exact');
    expect(matchKorean('파일', '파일 가져오기…')?.kind).toBe('prefix');
    expect(matchKorean('가져', '파일 가져오기…')).toEqual({ start: 3, end: 5, kind: 'word' });
    expect(matchKorean('져오', '파일 가져오기…')?.kind).toBe('substring');
  });
  it('treats a syllable still being composed (갖 on the way to 가져) as syllable + initial', () => {
    expect(matchKorean('갖', '파일 가져오기…')).toEqual({ start: 3, end: 5, kind: 'initials' });
  });
  it('is case-insensitive, NFC-normalised, and finds nothing when nothing matches', () => {
    expect(matchKorean('IMPORT', 'import')?.kind).toBe('exact');
    expect(matchKorean('가'.normalize('NFD'), '파일 가져오기…')?.start).toBe(3);
    expect(matchKorean('효소', '파일 가져오기…')).toBeNull();
    expect(matchKorean('   ', '파일')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 구현** `R/palette/hangul.ts`

```ts
// Korean palette matching: NFC substring, 초성 (initial consonant) and mixed queries (ADR-0005 §5).
const S_BASE = 0xac00;
const S_COUNT = 11172;
const N_COUNT = 588; // 21 vowels × 28 finals
const T_COUNT = 28;

export const CHOSEONG = ['ㄱ', 'ㄲ', 'ㄴ', 'ㄷ', 'ㄸ', 'ㄹ', 'ㅁ', 'ㅂ', 'ㅃ', 'ㅅ', 'ㅆ', 'ㅇ', 'ㅈ', 'ㅉ', 'ㅊ', 'ㅋ', 'ㅌ', 'ㅍ', 'ㅎ'] as const;
/** Final consonants (jongseong index) that can also start a syllable, as compatibility jamo. */
const FINAL_AS_INITIAL: Readonly<Record<number, string>> = {
  1: 'ㄱ', 2: 'ㄲ', 4: 'ㄴ', 7: 'ㄷ', 8: 'ㄹ', 16: 'ㅁ', 17: 'ㅂ', 19: 'ㅅ', 20: 'ㅆ', 21: 'ㅇ', 22: 'ㅈ', 23: 'ㅊ', 24: 'ㅋ', 25: 'ㅌ', 26: 'ㅍ', 27: 'ㅎ',
};

export type MatchKind = 'exact' | 'prefix' | 'word' | 'substring' | 'initials';
export interface Match {
  /** Code-point offsets into the NFC text. */
  start: number;
  end: number;
  kind: MatchKind;
}
export const MATCH_RANK: Readonly<Record<MatchKind, number>> = { exact: 0, prefix: 1, word: 2, substring: 3, initials: 4 };

const isSyllable = (c: number) => c >= S_BASE && c < S_BASE + S_COUNT;
export const isChoseong = (ch: string): boolean => (CHOSEONG as readonly string[]).includes(ch);

export function initialOf(ch: string): string {
  const c = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
  return isSyllable(c) ? CHOSEONG[Math.floor((c - S_BASE) / N_COUNT)]! : ch;
}

/** "갖" → ["가", "ㅈ"] (a syllable still being composed); null when there is no final that can start a syllable. */
export function splitFinal(ch: string): [string, string] | null {
  const c = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
  if (!isSyllable(c)) return null;
  const t = (c - S_BASE) % T_COUNT;
  const initial = FINAL_AS_INITIAL[t];
  return initial ? [String.fromCodePoint(c - t), initial] : null;
}

const chars = (s: string) => [...s.normalize('NFC').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')];
const WORD_START = /[\s(‘“/·-]/;

function find(query: readonly string[], text: readonly string[]): { start: number; literal: boolean } | null {
  for (let i = 0; i + query.length <= text.length; i++) {
    let literal = true;
    let ok = true;
    for (let j = 0; j < query.length; j++) {
      const q = query[j]!;
      const t = text[i + j]!;
      if (q === t) continue;
      if (isChoseong(q) && initialOf(t) === q) {
        literal = false;
        continue;
      }
      ok = false;
      break;
    }
    if (ok) return { start: i, literal };
  }
  return null;
}

export function matchKorean(query: string, text: string): Match | null {
  const q = chars(query.trim());
  if (q.length === 0) return null;
  const t = chars(text);
  const last = splitFinal(q.at(-1)!);
  const variants = last ? [q, [...q.slice(0, -1), ...last]] : [q];
  let best: Match | null = null;
  for (const v of variants) {
    const hit = find(v, t);
    if (!hit) continue;
    const end = hit.start + v.length;
    const kind: MatchKind = !hit.literal
      ? 'initials'
      : hit.start === 0 && end === t.length
        ? 'exact'
        : hit.start === 0
          ? 'prefix'
          : WORD_START.test(t[hit.start - 1]!)
            ? 'word'
            : 'substring';
    const m: Match = { start: hit.start, end, kind };
    if (!best || MATCH_RANK[m.kind] < MATCH_RANK[best.kind]) best = m;
  }
  return best;
}
```
Run → PASS.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { CommandSpec } from '@learn/domain';
import { rankCommands } from './rank';

const C: CommandSpec[] = [
  { id: 'file.importManaged', label: '파일 가져오기…', keywords: ['가져오기', 'import'], contexts: ['library'] },
  { id: 'file.importLinked', label: '외부 파일 연결…', keywords: ['연결', 'link'], contexts: ['library'] },
  { id: 'nav.back', label: '라이브러리로 돌아가기', keywords: ['뒤로', 'back'], contexts: ['viewer'] },
];
const ids = (q: string) => rankCommands(C, q, 'darwin').map((r) => r.command.id);

describe('rankCommands', () => {
  it('keeps registry order for an empty query', () => {
    expect(ids('')).toEqual(['file.importManaged', 'file.importLinked', 'nav.back']);
  });
  it('puts label matches before keyword-only matches, best kind first', () => {
    expect(ids('파일')).toEqual(['file.importManaged', 'file.importLinked']); // prefix before word
    expect(ids('ㄱㅈ')).toEqual(['file.importManaged']);
    expect(ids('import')).toEqual(['file.importManaged']);
    expect(ids('ㄷㄹ')).toEqual(['nav.back']); // keyword 뒤로 by 초성 (the label has no ㄷㄹ run)
  });
  it('highlights only label matches', () => {
    expect(rankCommands(C, '가져', 'darwin')[0]!.match).toEqual({ start: 3, end: 5, kind: 'word' });
    expect(rankCommands(C, 'back', 'darwin')[0]!.match).toBeNull();
  });
  it('finds nothing for an unrelated query', () => {
    expect(ids('효소 억제제')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```
`R/palette/rank.ts`:
```ts
import { commandLabel, type CommandSpec } from '@learn/domain';
import { MATCH_RANK, matchKorean, type Match } from './hangul';

export interface RankedCommand {
  command: CommandSpec;
  /** Where the query matched the label (for <mark>); null for keyword-only matches and the empty query. */
  match: Match | null;
}

/** Label matches by kind then position, then keyword-only matches; ties keep registry order. */
export function rankCommands(commands: readonly CommandSpec[], query: string, platform: string): RankedCommand[] {
  if (query.trim() === '') return commands.map((command) => ({ command, match: null }));
  return commands
    .flatMap((command, index) => {
      const onLabel = matchKorean(query, commandLabel(command, platform));
      if (onLabel) return [{ command, match: onLabel, score: MATCH_RANK[onLabel.kind] * 100 + onLabel.start, index }];
      const kinds = (command.keywords ?? []).flatMap((k) => {
        const m = matchKorean(query, k);
        return m ? [MATCH_RANK[m.kind]] : [];
      });
      return kinds.length ? [{ command, match: null, score: 1000 + Math.min(...kinds), index }] : [];
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score || a.index - b.index)
    .map(({ command, match }) => ({ command, match }));
}
```
`R/palette/highlight.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { Match } from './hangul';

/** The matched code points of a label in <mark> (accent text, 600 — palette.css). */
export function highlight(label: string, match: Match | null): ReactNode {
  if (!match) return label;
  const chars = [...label.normalize('NFC')];
  return (
    <>
      {chars.slice(0, match.start).join('')}
      <mark>{chars.slice(match.start, match.end).join('')}</mark>
      {chars.slice(match.end).join('')}
    </>
  );
}
```
Run: `pnpm vitest run apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/palette` → PASS.

```ts
import { useLearnEvent } from '../events';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { runCommand, type CommandEnv } from './registry';

/** Menu clicks and accelerators. A menu shortcut while the palette is open closes it, then runs (ADR-0005 §5). */
export function useMenuCommands(env: CommandEnv): void {
  useLearnEvent('menuCommand', ({ commandId }) => {
    const shell = useShell.getState();
    if (commandId !== 'app.palette' && shell.paletteOpen) shell.setPaletteOpen(false);
    runCommand(commandId, env);
  });
}
```

```ts
  footMove: '이동',
  footRun: '실행',
  footClose: '닫기',
  footHint: '초성 검색 지원 · ㄱㅈ → 가져오기',
} as const;
```

```tsx
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button as AriaButton } from 'react-aria-components';
import { acceleratorLabel, commandById } from '@learn/domain';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { Kbd } from '../ui/Kbd';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';

/** ⌘K launcher: a field-shaped button (design-spec 6). Sidebar y 52–80, or the toolbar's right end when folded. */
export function Launcher() {
  const { run } = useCommands();
  const accelerator = commandById('app.palette').accelerator!;
  return (
    <AriaButton className="field launcher" aria-label={`${t.shell.launcher} (${acceleratorLabel(accelerator, currentPlatform())})`} data-testid="launcher" onPress={() => run('app.palette')}>
      <Icon icon={Search} size="sm" />
      <span className="placeholder ellipsis">{t.shell.launcher}</span>
      <Kbd accelerator={accelerator} />
    </AriaButton>
  );
}
```

`R/palette/Palette.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { Autocomplete, Dialog, Header, Input, Menu, MenuItem, MenuSection, Modal, ModalOverlay, TextField } from 'react-aria-components';
import { COMMANDS, commandLabel, inPalette, type CommandId } from '@learn/domain';
import { useCommands } from '../commands/CommandProvider';
import { COMMAND_ICONS } from '../commands/icons';
import { useShell } from '../state/shell-store';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { Kbd } from '../ui/Kbd';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';
import { highlight } from './highlight';
import { rankCommands } from './rank';

/**
 * A key that only finishes a Korean composition (isComposing, or keyCode 229) must not reach RAC's handlers:
 * no command runs and the palette does not close (ADR-0005 §3; RAC's own Enter check misses keyCode 229 and Escape).
 */
export function stopWhileComposing(e: KeyboardEvent): void {
  if (e.nativeEvent.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229) e.stopPropagation();
}

/** ⌘K command palette (palette.html, design-spec 10.5): available registry commands only, 초성 search, ↩ runs once. */
export function Palette() {
  const open = useShell((s) => s.paletteOpen);
  const setOpen = useShell((s) => s.setPaletteOpen);
  const { run, isEnabled } = useCommands();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const platform = currentPlatform();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) setQuery('');
  }, [open]);
  const availableKey = COMMANDS.filter((c) => inPalette(c) && isEnabled(c.id)).map((c) => c.id).join(' ');
  const ranked = useMemo(() => {
    const ids = new Set(availableKey.split(' '));
    return rankCommands(COMMANDS.filter((c) => ids.has(c.id)), query, platform);
  }, [availableKey, query, platform]);
  const execute = (id: CommandId) => {
    setOpen(false);
    queueMicrotask(() => run(id)); // after the palette let go of focus
  };
  return (
    <ModalOverlay className="palette-scrim" isOpen={open} onOpenChange={setOpen} isDismissable>
      <Modal className="palette-modal">
        <Dialog className="palette" aria-label={t.palette.dialogLabel} data-testid="palette">
          <div className="p-body" onKeyDownCapture={stopWhileComposing}>
            <Autocomplete inputValue={query} onInputChange={setQuery}>
              <TextField className="p-input" aria-label={t.palette.inputLabel} autoFocus>
                <Icon icon={Search} size="lg" />
                <Input className="p-field" placeholder={t.palette.placeholder} data-testid="palette-input" />
              </TextField>
              <Menu
                className="p-list"
                aria-label={t.palette.group}
                onAction={(key) => execute(String(key) as CommandId)}
                renderEmptyState={() => (
                  <div className="p-empty" data-testid="palette-empty">
                    <div className="pe-title">{t.palette.emptyTitle(query)}</div>
                    <div className="pe-text">{t.palette.emptyBody}</div>
                  </div>
                )}
              >
                {ranked.length > 0 && (
                  <MenuSection>
                    <Header className="p-group">{t.palette.group}</Header>
                    {ranked.map(({ command, match }) => (
                      <MenuItem key={command.id} id={command.id} textValue={commandLabel(command, platform)} className="p-item" data-testid="palette-item" data-command-id={command.id}>
                        <Icon icon={COMMAND_ICONS[command.id]} />
                        <span className="p-title ellipsis">{highlight(commandLabel(command, platform), match)}</span>
                        {command.accelerator && (
                          <span className="p-meta">
                            <Kbd accelerator={command.accelerator} />
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </MenuItem>
                    ))}
                  </MenuSection>
                )}
              </Menu>
            </Autocomplete>
          </div>
          <div className="p-foot">
            <span className="row">
              <Kbd keys={['↑', '↓']} />
              {t.palette.footMove}
            </span>
            <span className="row">
              <Kbd keys={['↩']} />
              {t.palette.footRun}
            </span>
            <span className="row">
              <Kbd keys={['esc']} />
              {t.palette.footClose}
            </span>
            <span className="spacer" />
            <span className="row">{t.palette.footHint}</span>
          </div>
        </Dialog>
      </Modal>
    </ModalOverlay>
  );
}
```

```css
@layer screens {
  .launcher-wrap .launcher {
    width: 100%;
    padding-left: var(--space-16); /* glyph on the row-icon column (x 24) */
  }
  .titlebar .launcher {
    width: 232px;
    flex: none;
  }
  .palette-scrim {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 40;
    background: color-mix(in oklch, var(--bg-scrim) 55%, transparent);
  }
  .palette-scrim[data-entering] {
    animation: fade-in var(--duration-base) var(--ease-standard);
  }
  .palette-modal {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 112px; /* local: palette.html */
    translate: -50% 0;
    width: 640px;
    max-width: calc(100vw - var(--space-32));
    outline: none;
  }
  .palette-modal[data-entering] {
    animation: pop-in var(--duration-base) var(--ease-standard);
  }
  .palette {
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    background: var(--bg-raised);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e2);
    overflow: hidden;
    outline: none;
  }
  /* Everything starts on the row-icon column: 8 (list inset) + 12 (row padding) = 20. */
  .palette .p-input {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-12);
    height: var(--toolbar-height);
    padding: 0 var(--space-16) 0 var(--space-20);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--separator);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .palette .p-field {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-width: 0;
    border: 0;
    outline: none;
    background: none;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font: var(--type-title3);
    font-weight: 400;
    caret-color: var(--accent);
  }
  .palette .p-field::placeholder {
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
  }
  .palette .p-list {
    max-height: 360px; /* local: 9 rows + header */
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: var(--space-8);
    outline: none;
  }
  .palette .p-group {
    padding: var(--space-8) var(--space-12) var(--space-4);
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
    font: var(--type-caption);
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .palette .p-item {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-12);
    height: 36px; /* local: palette row */
    padding: 0 var(--space-8) 0 var(--space-12);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    outline: none;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .palette .p-item > .icon {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }
  .palette .p-item .p-title {
    flex: 0 1 auto;
  }
  .palette .p-item .p-meta {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    margin-left: auto;
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .palette .p-item mark {
    background: none;
    color: var(--accent-text);
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .palette .p-item[data-focused] {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  .palette .p-item[data-focused] :is(.icon, .p-meta, mark) {
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  .palette .p-item[data-focused] .kbd {
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.18); /* local: keycap on the accent fill */
    box-shadow: none;
    color: var(--text-on-accent);
  }
  .palette .p-empty {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    padding: var(--space-24) var(--space-12) var(--space-16);
  }
  .palette .p-empty .pe-title {
    font: var(--type-headline);
  }
  .palette .p-empty .pe-text {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
  .palette .p-foot {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-16);
    height: 36px; /* local: palette footer */
    padding: 0 var(--space-16) 0 var(--space-20);
    border-top: 1px solid var(--separator);
    color: var(--text-tertiary);
    font: var(--type-caption);
  }
  .palette .p-foot .row {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-6);
  }
}
```

`tests/e2e/palette.spec.ts`:
```ts
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { FIXTURES, importPaths, launch, openDialogCalls, openItem, runMenuCommand, stubOpenDialog } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');
const dirs = (i: { outputPath(...p: string[]): string }) => [i.outputPath('library'), i.outputPath('userdata')] as const;

test('the launcher opens the palette; 초성 finds 파일 가져오기…; ↩ runs it exactly once', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    await stubOpenDialog(app, []);
    await page.getByTestId('launcher').click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette-input')).toBeFocused();
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette-item')).toHaveText([/파일 가져오기…/, /외부 파일 연결…/, /사이드바 보기\/숨기기/, /단축키 도움말/]);
    await page.getByTestId('palette-input').fill('ㄱㅈ');
    const first = page.getByTestId('palette-item').first();
    await expect(first).toHaveAttribute('data-command-id', 'file.importManaged');
    await expect(first.locator('mark')).toHaveText('가져');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeHidden();
    await expect.poll(() => openDialogCalls(app)).toBe(1);
    await page.waitForTimeout(300);
    expect(await openDialogCalls(app)).toBe(1);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('keywords, no results, esc, and a menu command while open', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette');
    const input = page.getByTestId('palette-input');
    await input.fill('import');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette-item').first()).toHaveAttribute('data-command-id', 'file.importManaged');
    await input.fill('ㅅㅇㄷㅂ');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette-item').first()).toHaveAttribute('data-command-id', 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await input.fill('효소 억제제');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette-empty')).toContainText('‘효소 억제제’에 맞는 명령이 없습니다');
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeHidden();
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette');
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.toggleSidebar'); // closes the palette, then runs
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeHidden();
    await expect(page.getByTestId('shell')).toHaveAttribute('data-sidebar', 'hidden');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('folded sidebar: the launcher is 232 × 28 at the toolbar end, centred in the 52 px bar', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    const inSidebar = (await page.getByTestId('launcher').boundingBox())!;
    expect(inSidebar).toMatchObject({ x: 8, y: 52, width: 224, height: 28 });
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('launcher')).toHaveCount(1);
    const b = (await page.getByTestId('launcher').boundingBox())!;
    const bar = (await page.getByTestId('toolbar').boundingBox())!;
    const padRight = await page.getByTestId('toolbar').evaluate((el) => parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).paddingRight));
    expect(b.width).toBe(232);
    expect(b.height).toBe(28);
    expect(Math.abs(b.y + b.height / 2 - 26)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5); // 52 px bar incl. its 1 px hairline
    expect(Math.round(b.x + b.width)).toBe(Math.round(bar.x + bar.width - padRight));
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('the reader lists reader commands and not library ones', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    await importPaths(page, [PDF]);
    await openItem(page, 'sample');
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette');
    const ids = await page.getByTestId('palette-item').evaluateAll((els) => els.map((e) => e.getAttribute('data-command-id')));
    expect(ids).toContain('nav.back');
    expect(ids).not.toContain('file.importManaged');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```
`tests/e2e/ime.spec.ts`:
```ts
// IME: keys that belong to a Korean composition must not act (quality spec M3 `ime`, ADR-0004 risk).
// CDP Input.imeSetComposition starts a real composition: Chromium then marks keydown events isComposing.
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { launch, openDialogCalls, runMenuCommand, stubOpenDialog } from './helpers';

test('palette: Enter and Escape during a composition neither run nor close; after commit ↩ runs once', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await stubOpenDialog(app, []);
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette');
    const input = page.getByTestId('palette-input');
    await expect(input).toBeFocused();
    const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page);
    await cdp.send('Input.imeSetComposition', { text: 'ㄱㅈ', selectionStart: 2, selectionEnd: 2 });
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette-item').first()).toHaveAttribute('data-command-id', 'file.importManaged');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeVisible();
    expect(await openDialogCalls(app)).toBe(0);
    await cdp.send('Input.insertText', { text: 'ㄱㅈ' }); // commits the composition
    await expect(input).toHaveValue('ㄱㅈ');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeHidden();
    await expect.poll(() => openDialogCalls(app)).toBe(1);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('palette: a keyCode 229 Enter (macOS IME confirm) is ignored even without isComposing', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await stubOpenDialog(app, []);
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette');
    await page.getByTestId('palette-input').fill('ㄱㅈ');
    const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page);
    await cdp.send('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'keyDown', key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', windowsVirtualKeyCode: 229, nativeVirtualKeyCode: 229 });
    await cdp.send('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'keyUp', key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', windowsVirtualKeyCode: 229, nativeVirtualKeyCode: 229 });
    await page.waitForTimeout(300);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('palette')).toBeVisible();
    expect(await openDialogCalls(app)).toBe(0);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```

```ts
test('palette: 초성 results and no results', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launchTour(testInfo.outputPath());
  try {
    await importPaths(s.page, [path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf')]);
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.palette');
      await s.page.getByTestId('palette-input').fill('ㄱㅈ');
      await shoot(s.page, `palette-${theme}`, { mask: [s.page.locator('[data-col="added"]')] });
      await s.page.getByTestId('palette-input').fill('효소 억제제');
      await shoot(s.page, `palette-none-${theme}`, { mask: [s.page.locator('[data-col="added"]')] });
      await s.page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    }
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```

### Task 10: 설정 창(⌘,) — 테마·밀도·글자 크기·라이브러리 위치

명세 6 "설정 창": ⌘,로 여는 **별도 창**(시트가 아니다 — 품질 명세의 "설정 시트"는 디자인 명세의 최종 결정 "설정은 별도 창"으로 대체된다), 표준 제목 막대 "설정", 폭 600, 높이는 내용에 맞춤, 크기 조절 없음, 변경 즉시 적용, "완료"·× 단추 없음(닫기는 신호등·⌘W). 10.6: 테마(시스템/라이트/다크 미리보기), 밀도(촘촘하게/보통/여유 있게 = 24/28/32), 글자 크기(12·13·14·15pt), 라이브러리 위치 · Finder에서 보기 · 다른 폴더 선택…. M2가 prefs에 `density`·`fontSize`를 이미 명세대로 두었으므로 여기서는 **적용**만 한다: 메인 창의 `--row-height`와 글자 크기 토큰(`--text-scale`), `prefsChanged`마다 다시 적용. ⌘+/⌘−/⌘0(명세 7.4 라이브러리 범위)도 같은 prefs를 바꾼다.

**R4 — 라이브러리 위치:** 렌더러는 경로를 보내지 않는다. `prefs.set`의 입력에서 `libraryRoot`를 빼고(`prefsSetInput`), 새 명령 `app.chooseLibraryFolder`가 main에서 폴더 대화상자를 띄워 **존재·디렉터리·쓰기 가능**을 확인한 뒤, "다시 시작해야 적용된다"는 네이티브 확인(시트)을 받고 저장·재시작한다. ADR-0007과 README에 적는다.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (domain): 명령 `app.settings`(`'설정…'`, `'CmdOrCtrl+,'`, `{ menu: 'app', group: 1, order: 1 }` → Linux/Windows는 파일 메뉴 끝, `['app']`, **main 소유**: `MAIN_OWNED_COMMANDS = new Set(['app.settings'])`), `view.textBigger`(`'글자 크게'`, `'CmdOrCtrl+Plus'` + 숨은 `⌘=`), `view.textSmaller`(`'글자 작게'`, `'CmdOrCtrl+-'`), `view.textReset`(`'기본 글자 크기'`, `'CmdOrCtrl+0'`) — 세 개 모두 `{ menu: 'view', group: 3 }`, 맥락 `['library']`; `CommandContextState.textSize: 12 | 13 | 14 | 15`(크게는 15 미만, 작게는 12 초과, 기본은 13이 아닐 때).
- Produces (IPC): `ui.openSettings()`, `app.showLibraryFolder()`, `app.chooseLibraryFolder() → { relaunching: boolean }`; `prefsSetInput`(렌더러용)에서 `libraryRoot` 제외, main 내부용 `prefsPatchInput` + `type PrefsPatch`; `AppInfo.libraryRootDisplay: string`(홈 → `~`).
- Produces (main): `createSettingsWindow({ preload, url, main }): { open(): BrowserWindow; current(): BrowserWindow | null }`(`SETTINGS_WIDTH = 600`), `checkLibraryFolder(dir): { ok: true } | { ok: false; message: string }`, `displayPath(p, home)`; `IpcDeps.windows.openSettings()`, `IpcDeps.relaunch()`(`LEARN_E2E=1`이면 `globalThis.__learnRelaunchRequested = true`만 남긴다 — Playwright는 재시작한 프로세스를 따라갈 수 없다).
- Produces (renderer): 창 종류 `settings`; `useApplyPrefs()`(메인 창 `<html data-density data-font-size>`); `CommandEnv.openSettings()`, `CommandEnv.textSize`, `CommandEnv.setTextSize(size)`; 토큰 `--text-scale`(`:root[data-font-size='12'|'14'|'15']`).
- testid: `settings-root`, `theme-tile-system|light|dark`, `density-compact|regular|comfortable`, `font-size-12|13|14|15`, `library-path`, `library-reveal`, `library-choose`, `library-error`, `fatal-settings`.

```ts
/** Main-internal prefs patch (the store's own input). */
export const prefsPatchInput = z.object(prefsFields).partial().strict();
export type PrefsPatch = z.input<typeof prefsPatchInput>;
/** What the renderer may change: everything but the library location, which only app.chooseLibraryFolder sets (R4). */
export const prefsSetInput = z.object(prefsFields).omit({ libraryRoot: true }).partial().strict();
```

`tokens.ts` 끝에:
```ts
/** UI text sizes in pt (prefs.fontSize, settings 글자 크기). Type roles scale by size / 13, rounded to whole px. */
export const uiTextSizes = [12, 13, 14, 15] as const;
export const defaultUiTextSize = 13;
```
`apps/desktop/scripts/build-tokens.mjs`의 타입 부분을 바꾼다.
```js
  section('Typography (roles scale with --text-scale: settings 글자 크기, main window only)');
  decl('font-system', tokens.fonts.system);
  decl('font-pretendard', tokens.fonts.pretendard);
  decl('font-mono', tokens.fonts.mono);
  decl('font-ui', 'var(--font-system)');
  decl('text-scale', '1');
  const scaled = (n, step) => `round(calc(${px(n)} * var(--text-scale)), ${step}px)`;
  for (const [role, t] of Object.entries(tokens.typeScale)) {
    const name = kebab(role);
    decl(`text-${name}-size`, scaled(t.size, 1));
    decl(`text-${name}-line`, scaled(t.line, 2));
    decl(`text-${name}-weight`, String(t.weight));
    decl(`type-${name}`, `${t.weight} ${scaled(t.size, 1)}/${scaled(t.line, 2)} ${role === 'mono' ? 'var(--font-mono)' : 'var(--font-ui)'}`);
  }
```
`renderCss()`의 `densities` 다음에:
```js

    .filter((s) => s !== tokens.defaultUiTextSize)
    .map((s) => `  :root[data-font-size='${s}'] {\n    --text-scale: calc(${s} / ${tokens.defaultUiTextSize});\n  }`);
```

`M/display-path.ts`:
```ts
import path from 'node:path';

export function displayPath(p: string, home: string): string {
  if (p === home) return '~';
  return p.startsWith(home + path.sep) ? `~${p.slice(home.length)}` : p;
}
```
`M/library-folder.test.ts`:
```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { checkLibraryFolder } from './library-folder';

describe('checkLibraryFolder (R4)', () => {
  const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'learn-folder-'));
  it('accepts an existing writable folder', () => {
    expect(checkLibraryFolder(tmp)).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });
  it('rejects a missing path and a file', () => {
    expect(checkLibraryFolder(path.join(tmp, 'nope'))).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
    const file = path.join(tmp, 'f.txt');
    fs.writeFileSync(file, 'x');
    expect(checkLibraryFolder(file)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
  });
  it.skipIf(process.getuid?.() === 0)('rejects a folder this user cannot write to', () => {
    const ro = path.join(tmp, 'ro');
    fs.mkdirSync(ro, { mode: 0o555 });
    expect(checkLibraryFolder(ro)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, message: expect.stringContaining('쓸 수 없습니다') });
  });
});
```
`M/library-folder.ts`:
```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';

export type FolderCheck = { ok: true } | { ok: false; message: string };

/** A library folder must exist, be a directory and be writable by this user (R4). */
export function checkLibraryFolder(dir: string): FolderCheck {
  let stat: fs.Stats;
  try {
    stat = fs.statSync(dir);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: '폴더를 찾을 수 없습니다. 다른 폴더를 고르세요.' };
  }
  if (!stat.isDirectory()) return { ok: false, message: '폴더가 아닙니다. 다른 폴더를 고르세요.' };
  try {
    fs.accessSync(dir, fs.constants.W_OK);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: '이 폴더에는 쓸 수 없습니다. 쓰기 권한이 있는 폴더를 고르세요.' };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}
```
`M/settings-window.ts`:
```ts
import { BrowserWindow } from 'electron';
import { windowBackground } from '../renderer/src/design/tokens.generated';
import { contentHeight, markSized } from './measure';
import { currentTheme } from './theme';

export const SETTINGS_WIDTH = 600;

/** ⌘, (design-spec 6 설정 창): one standard window, 600 wide, as tall as its content, no resizing. */
export function createSettingsWindow(deps: { preload: string; url(): string; main(): BrowserWindow | null }) {
  let win: BrowserWindow | null = null;
  return {
    current: () => win,
    open(): BrowserWindow {
      if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
        win.show();
        win.focus();
        return win;
      }
      const created = new BrowserWindow({
        width: SETTINGS_WIDTH,
        height: 560,
        useContentSize: true,
        show: false,
        title: '설정',
        resizable: false,
        minimizable: false,
        maximizable: false,
        fullscreenable: false,
        autoHideMenuBar: true,
        backgroundColor: windowBackground[currentTheme()],
        webPreferences: { preload: deps.preload, sandbox: true, contextIsolation: true, nodeIntegration: false, webSecurity: true, spellcheck: false, backgroundThrottling: false },
      });
      win = created;
      created.on('closed', () => {
        if (win === created) win = null;
      });
      created.once('ready-to-show', () => {
        void (async () => {
          const height = await contentHeight(created.webContents, '[data-settings-root]', 480);
          if (created.isDestroyed()) return;
          created.setContentSize(SETTINGS_WIDTH, height);
          const main = deps.main();
          if (main && !main.isDestroyed()) {
            const b = main.getBounds();
            created.setPosition(Math.round(b.x + (b.width - SETTINGS_WIDTH) / 2), b.y + 96);
          }
          await markSized(created.webContents);
          created.show();
        })();
      });
      void created.loadURL(deps.url());
      return created;
    },
  };
}
```
`M/ipc/register.ts`의 `IpcDeps`에 `windows: { openSettings(): void };`, `relaunch(): void;`. `M/ipc/test-deps.ts` 기본값에 `windows: { openSettings: () => {} }, relaunch: () => {},`, `uiContext.current`를 `({ view: 'library', blocked: false, textSize: 13 })`로.
`M/ipc/ui.ts`에 `handle(Channels.ui.openSettings, emptyInput, () => { deps.windows.openSettings(); });`.
`M/ipc/app.ts` 전체:
```ts
import path from 'node:path';
import { BrowserWindow, dialog, shell, type MessageBoxOptions, type OpenDialogOptions } from 'electron';
import { Channels, LearnError, emptyInput } from '@learn/domain';
import { checkLibraryFolder } from '../library-folder';
import type { Handle, IpcDeps } from './register';

export function registerAppIpc(handle: Handle, deps: IpcDeps): void {
  handle(Channels.app.info, emptyInput, () => deps.appInfo());

  handle(Channels.app.showLibraryFolder, emptyInput, () => {
    shell.showItemInFolder(deps.appInfo().libraryRoot);
  });

  // R4: the renderer never sends a path. Main asks, checks, confirms the restart, saves and relaunches.
  handle(Channels.app.chooseLibraryFolder, emptyInput, async (_input, event) => {
    const owner = BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender);
    const pick: OpenDialogOptions = { title: '라이브러리 폴더 선택', buttonLabel: '선택', properties: ['openDirectory', 'createDirectory'] };
    const res = owner ? await dialog.showOpenDialog(owner, pick) : await dialog.showOpenDialog(pick);
    const picked = res.canceled ? undefined : res.filePaths[0];
    if (!picked) return { relaunching: false };
    const target = path.resolve(picked);
    if (target === path.resolve(deps.appInfo().libraryRoot)) return { relaunching: false };
    const check = checkLibraryFolder(target);
    if (!check.ok) throw new LearnError('VALIDATION_FAILED', check.message);
    const ask: MessageBoxOptions = {
      type: 'question',
      message: '다른 라이브러리를 열려면 Learn을 다시 시작해야 합니다',
      detail: `${target}\n이 폴더에 라이브러리가 없으면 새로 만듭니다. 지금 라이브러리는 그대로 남아 있습니다.`,
      buttons: ['다시 시작', '취소'],
      defaultId: 0,
      cancelId: 1,
    };
    const { response } = owner ? await dialog.showMessageBox(owner, ask) : await dialog.showMessageBox(ask);
    if (response !== 0) return { relaunching: false };
    deps.prefs.set({ libraryRoot: target });
    deps.relaunch();
    return { relaunching: true };
  });
}
```
`M/index.ts`:
- `appInfo`에 `libraryRootDisplay: displayPath(libraryRoot, os.homedir()),`(`import os from 'node:os'`).
- 초기 상황 `let uiContext: CommandContextState = { view: 'library', blocked: true, textSize: 13 };`.

```ts
const settingsWindow = createSettingsWindow({ preload: path.join(here, '../preload/index.cjs'), url: () => windowUrl(rendererBase, 'settings', urlOptions), main: () => mainWindow });

function dispatchMenuCommand(commandId: CommandId): void {
  if (commandId === 'app.settings') {
    settingsWindow.open(); // main-owned: works even when the library failed to open
    return;
  }
  const target = mainWindow;
  if (!target || target.isDestroyed()) return;
  if (!target.isFocused()) target.focus();
  sendEvent(target.webContents, 'menuCommand', { commandId });
}
```
- `deps`에 `windows: { openSettings: () => void settingsWindow.open() },`와
```ts

        if (process.env['LEARN_E2E'] === '1') {
          (globalThis as { __learnRelaunchRequested?: boolean }).__learnRelaunchRequested = true; // Playwright cannot follow a relaunch
          return;
        }
        setImmediate(() => {
          app.relaunch();
          app.quit();
        });
      },
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 렌더러 — 적용, 명령, 설정 화면**

`R/settings/useApplyPrefs.ts`:
```ts
import { useLayoutEffect } from 'react';
import { usePrefs } from '../data/queries';

/** Main window only: row height (density) and UI text size, before paint and on every prefsChanged. */
export function useApplyPrefs(): void {
  const prefs = usePrefs().data;
  const density = prefs?.density;
  const fontSize = prefs?.fontSize;
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (density) root.dataset['density'] = density;
    if (fontSize) root.dataset['fontSize'] = String(fontSize);
  }, [density, fontSize]);
}
```
`R/commands/registry.ts`: `CommandEnv`에 `/** ⌘, (main-owned). */ openSettings(): void;`, `/** ⌘+ / ⌘− / ⌘0 (Task 10). */ setTextSize(size: 12 | 13 | 14 | 15): void;`; `contextOf`가 `textSize: env.textSize`를 돌려준다; 핸들러:
```ts
  'app.settings': (env) => env.openSettings(),
  'view.textBigger': (env) => env.setTextSize(Math.min(15, env.textSize + 1) as 12 | 13 | 14 | 15),
  'view.textSmaller': (env) => env.setTextSize(Math.max(12, env.textSize - 1) as 12 | 13 | 14 | 15),
  'view.textReset': (env) => env.setTextSize(13),
```
`registry.test.ts`: `env()`에 `textSize: 13, openSettings: vi.fn(), setTextSize: vi.fn(),`, `contextOf` 기대값에 `textSize: 13`, 그리고 `it('steps the text size', …)`(`runCommand('view.textBigger', env())` → `setTextSize(14)`).
`R/commands/icons.ts`: `'app.settings': Settings, 'view.textBigger': AArrowUp, 'view.textSmaller': AArrowDown, 'view.textReset': Type`.
`R/app/MainWindow.tsx`: `useApplyPrefs();`를 맨 위에, `const prefs = usePrefs().data;`, `env`에
```ts
    textSize: prefs?.fontSize ?? 13,
    openSettings: () => void api().ui.openSettings(),
    setTextSize: (fontSize) => void api().prefs.set({ fontSize }),
```
`R/components/FatalScreen.tsx`: 보고를 `{ view: 'library', blocked: true, textSize: 13 }`으로, `Notice`에 단추를 더한다(라이브러리를 열지 못해도 위치를 바꿀 수 있다; `app.settings`는 main 소유 명령이고 이 버튼은 그 구현인 `ui.openSettings`를 부른다).
```tsx
          actions={
            <Button variant="primary" testId="fatal-settings" onPress={() => void api().ui.openSettings()}>
              {t.fatal.openSettings}
            </Button>
          }
```

`R/settings/ThemePreview.tsx`:
```tsx
import { themeHex } from '../design/tokens.generated';

const LIGHT = { side: themeHex.light.bgSidebar, main: themeHex.light.bgContent, line: themeHex.light.separator };
const DARK = { side: themeHex.dark.bgSidebar, main: themeHex.dark.bgContent, line: themeHex.dark.separator };

function Pane({ c, clip }: { c: typeof LIGHT; clip?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="pv" style={clip ? { clipPath: clip } : undefined}>
      <div className="pv-side" style={{ background: c.side }}>
        <i className="pv-accent" />
        <i style={{ background: c.line, width: '60%' }} />
        <i style={{ background: c.line, width: '70%' }} />
      </div>
      <div className="pv-main" style={{ background: c.main }}>
        {['90%', '70%', '80%', '50%'].map((w) => (
          <i key={w} style={{ background: c.line, width: w }} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Miniature window (settings.html themePreview): system = light left half + dark right half. */
export function ThemePreview({ kind }: { kind: 'system' | 'light' | 'dark' }) {
  return (
    <div className="preview" aria-hidden="true">
      {kind === 'system' ? (
        <>
          <Pane c={LIGHT} clip="inset(0 50% 0 0)" />
          <Pane c={DARK} clip="inset(0 0 0 50%)" />
        </>
      ) : (
        <Pane c={kind === 'dark' ? DARK : LIGHT} />
      )}
      <span className="lights">
        <b />
        <b />
        <b />
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`R/settings/SettingsApp.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Folder, FolderSearch } from 'lucide-react';
import { Radio, RadioGroup } from 'react-aria-components';
import type { Prefs } from '@learn/domain';
import { api, unwrap } from '../api';
import { useAppInfo, usePrefs, usePrefsSync } from '../data/queries';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';
import { Segmented } from '../ui/Segmented';
import { ThemePreview } from './ThemePreview';

const THEMES = ['system', 'light', 'dark'] as const;
const DENSITIES = ['compact', 'regular', 'comfortable'] as const;
const SIZES = [12, 13, 14, 15] as const;

/** ⌘, settings window (settings.html, design-spec 10.6): every change applies at once; no 완료 or ×. */
export function SettingsApp() {
  usePrefsSync();
  const prefs = usePrefs().data;
  const info = useAppInfo().data;
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = t.settings.title; // the window title follows the page title in Electron
  }, []);
  if (!prefs || !info) return null;
  const set = (patch: Partial<Omit<Prefs, 'libraryRoot'>>) => void api().prefs.set(patch);
  const choose = async () => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await unwrap(api().app.chooseLibraryFolder());
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="settings" data-settings-root="" data-testid="settings-root">
      <section aria-labelledby="g-appearance">
        <div className="group-label" id="g-appearance">{t.settings.appearance}</div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <div className="form-row stacked">
            <span className="fr-label" id="l-theme">{t.settings.theme}</span>
            <RadioGroup aria-labelledby="l-theme" orientation="horizontal" value={prefs.theme} onChange={(v) => set({ theme: v as Prefs['theme'] })} className="theme-tiles">
              {THEMES.map((v) => (
                <Radio key={v} value={v} className="theme-tile" data-testid={`theme-tile-${v}`}>
                  <ThemePreview kind={v} />
                  <span>{t.settings.themes[v]}</span>
                </Radio>
              ))}
            </RadioGroup>
          </div>
          <div className="form-row">
            <span className="fr-label" id="l-density">
              {t.settings.density}
              <span className="hint">{t.settings.densityHint}</span>
            </span>
            <Segmented aria-labelledby="l-density" value={prefs.density} onChange={(density) => set({ density })} options={DENSITIES.map((v) => ({ value: v, label: t.settings.densities[v], testId: `density-${v}` }))} />
          </div>
          <div className="form-row">
            <span className="fr-label" id="l-font">
              {t.settings.fontSize}
              <span className="hint ui-prose">{t.settings.fontSizeHint}</span>
            </span>
            <Segmented
              aria-labelledby="l-font"
              value={String(prefs.fontSize)}
              onChange={(v) => set({ fontSize: Number(v) as Prefs['fontSize'] })}
              options={SIZES.map((v) => ({ value: String(v), label: t.settings.fontSizes[v], testId: `font-size-${v}` }))}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="g-library">
        <div className="group-label" id="g-library">{t.settings.library}</div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <div className="form-row stacked">
            <span className="fr-label">
              {t.settings.location}
              <span className="hint ui-prose">{t.settings.locationHint}</span>
            </span>
            <div className="path-box" title={info.libraryRoot} data-testid="library-path">
              <Icon icon={Folder} size="sm" />
              <span className="ellipsis">{info.libraryRootDisplay}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="row-actions">
              <Button icon={FolderSearch} testId="library-reveal" onPress={() => void api().app.showLibraryFolder()}>
                {currentPlatform() === 'darwin' ? t.settings.reveal.darwin : t.settings.reveal.other}
              </Button>
              <Button testId="library-choose" onPress={() => void choose()}>
                {t.settings.choose}
              </Button>
            </div>
            {error && (
              <p className="field-error" role="alert" data-testid="library-error">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`R/main.tsx`의 `VIEWS`에 `settings: SettingsApp`. `R/strings/index.ts`에 `settings`.
`R/styles/screens/settings.css` (+ `index.ts`):
```css
@layer screens {
  :root[data-window='settings'] body {
    background: var(--bg-window);
  }
  .settings {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-20);
    padding: var(--space-8) var(--space-20) var(--space-20);
  }
  .form-group {
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    background: var(--bg-control);
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--separator);
  }
  .form-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-16);
    min-height: 44px; /* local: settings row (design-spec 6 설정 창) */
    padding: var(--space-8) var(--space-12) var(--space-8) var(--space-16);
  }
  .form-row + .form-row {
    border-top: 1px solid var(--separator);
  }
  .form-row .fr-label {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .form-row .fr-label .hint {
    display: block;
    margin-top: 2px;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
  .form-row.stacked {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: var(--space-12);
    padding-top: var(--space-12);
    padding-bottom: var(--space-16);
  }
  .theme-tiles {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: var(--space-16);
  }
  .theme-tile {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
    outline: none;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .theme-tile[data-selected] {
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .theme-tile .preview {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    height: 86px; /* local: settings.html preview */
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  .theme-tile .preview::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    border-radius: inherit;
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--border);
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  .theme-tile[data-selected] .preview {
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--bg-control), 0 0 0 4px var(--accent);
  }
  .theme-tile[data-focus-visible] .preview {
    outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus);
    outline-offset: 5px;
  }
  .theme-tile .pv {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 30% 1fr;
  }
  .theme-tile .pv-side {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 4px; /* local: miniature */
    padding: 18px 6px 6px; /* local: miniature */
  }
  .theme-tile .pv-main {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 4px; /* local: miniature */
    padding: 18px 8px 6px; /* local: miniature */
  }
  .theme-tile .preview i {
    display: block;
    height: 4px; /* local: miniature */
    border-radius: 2px;
  }
  .theme-tile .preview .pv-accent {
    width: 80%;
    background: var(--accent);
  }
  .theme-tile .preview .lights {
    position: absolute;
    top: 6px;
    left: 6px;
    display: flex;
    gap: 3px; /* local: miniature traffic lights */
  }
  .theme-tile .preview .lights b {
    width: 5px;
    height: 5px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: #ff5f57; /* local: macOS close */
  }
  .theme-tile .preview .lights b:nth-child(2) {
    background: #febc2e; /* local: macOS minimise */
  }
  .theme-tile .preview .lights b:nth-child(3) {
    background: #28c840; /* local: macOS zoom */
  }
  .path-box {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-8);
    min-width: 0;
    font: var(--type-mono);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
  .path-box .icon {
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }
  .row-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: var(--space-8);
  }
  .field-error {
    color: var(--danger-text);
    font: var(--type-footnote);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: E2E — 설정**

`tests/e2e/settings.spec.ts`:
```ts
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test, type ElectronApplication } from '@playwright/test';
import { FIXTURES, expectWindow, importPaths, launch, menuItemEnabled, runMenuCommand } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');
const dirs = (i: { outputPath(...p: string[]): string }) => [i.outputPath('library'), i.outputPath('userdata')] as const;
const prefsFile = (ud: string) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ud, 'preferences.json'), 'utf8')) as Record<string, unknown>;
const windowCount = (app: ElectronApplication) => app.evaluate(({ BrowserWindow }) => BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length);

test('⌘, opens one 600 px settings window; theme, density and text size apply at once to the main window', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const [lib, ud] = dirs(testInfo);
  const { app, page } = await launch(lib, ud);
  try {
    await importPaths(page, [PDF]);
    const settings = await expectWindow(app, 'settings', () => runMenuCommand(app, 'app.settings'));
    expect(await settings.evaluate(() => innerWidth)).toBe(600);
    expect(await settings.title()).toBe('설정');
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.settings'); // focuses the same window
    expect(await windowCount(app)).toBe(2);

    await settings.getByTestId('theme-tile-dark').click();
    await expect.poll(() => app.evaluate(({ nativeTheme }) => nativeTheme.themeSource)).toBe('dark');
    await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches)).toBe(true);

    await settings.getByTestId('density-compact').click();
    await expect.poll(async () => (await page.getByTestId('item-row').boundingBox())?.height).toBe(24);
    await settings.getByTestId('font-size-15').click();
    await expect.poll(() => page.getByTestId('open-item').first().evaluate((el) => getComputedStyle(el).fontSize)).toBe('15px');
    expect(prefsFile(ud)).toMatchObject({ theme: 'dark', density: 'compact', fontSize: 15 });
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('⌘+ / ⌘− / ⌘0 step the text size within 12–15 (library only)', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  const size = () => page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.dataset['fontSize']);
  try {
    expect(await size()).toBe('13');
    expect(await menuItemEnabled(app, 'view.textReset')).toBe(false);
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.textBigger');
    await expect.poll(size).toBe('14');
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.textBigger');
    await expect.poll(size).toBe('15');
    await expect.poll(() => menuItemEnabled(app, 'view.textBigger')).toBe(false);
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.textReset');
    await expect.poll(size).toBe('13');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('R4: the renderer cannot set the library path; main picks, checks, confirms and relaunches', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const [lib, ud] = dirs(testInfo);
  const target = testInfo.outputPath('other-library');
  fs.mkdirSync(target, { recursive: true });
  const { app, page } = await launch(lib, ud);
  try {
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ libraryRoot: '/tmp/x' } as never))).toMatchObject({ ok: false, code: 'VALIDATION_FAILED' });
    const settings = await expectWindow(app, 'settings', () => runMenuCommand(app, 'app.settings'));
    await expect(settings.getByTestId('library-path')).toHaveAttribute('title', lib);
    await app.evaluate(({ dialog, shell }, dir) => {
      const g = globalThis as { __revealed?: string; __asked?: number };
      dialog.showOpenDialog = (async () => ({ canceled: false, filePaths: [dir] })) as typeof dialog.showOpenDialog;
      dialog.showMessageBox = (async () => {
        g.__asked = (g.__asked ?? 0) + 1;
        return { response: 0, checkboxChecked: false };
      }) as typeof dialog.showMessageBox;
      shell.showItemInFolder = (p: string) => void (g.__revealed = p);
    }, target);
    await settings.getByTestId('library-reveal').click();
    await expect.poll(() => app.evaluate(() => (globalThis as { __revealed?: string }).__revealed)).toBe(lib);
    await settings.getByTestId('library-choose').click();
    await expect.poll(() => app.evaluate(() => (globalThis as { __learnRelaunchRequested?: boolean }).__learnRelaunchRequested)).toBe(true);
    expect(await app.evaluate(() => (globalThis as { __asked?: number }).__asked)).toBe(1);
    expect(prefsFile(ud)['libraryRoot']).toBe(target);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('R4: a folder that is not writable is refused in the settings window', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  test.skip(process.getuid?.() === 0, 'root can write anywhere');
  const ro = testInfo.outputPath('read-only');
  fs.mkdirSync(ro, { recursive: true, mode: 0o555 });
  const { app } = await launch(...dirs(testInfo));
  try {
    const settings = await expectWindow(app, 'settings', () => runMenuCommand(app, 'app.settings'));
    await app.evaluate(({ dialog }, dir) => {
      dialog.showOpenDialog = (async () => ({ canceled: false, filePaths: [dir] })) as typeof dialog.showOpenDialog;
    }, ro);
    await settings.getByTestId('library-choose').click();
    await expect(settings.getByTestId('library-error')).toContainText('쓸 수 없습니다');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('settings opens from the fatal screen when the library cannot be opened', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const lib = testInfo.outputPath('broken');
  fs.mkdirSync(lib, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(lib, 'library.sqlite'), 'not a database');
  const { app, page } = await launch(lib, testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    const settings = await expectWindow(app, 'settings', () => page.getByTestId('fatal-settings').click());
    await expect(settings.getByTestId('library-choose')).toBeVisible();
    expect(await menuItemEnabled(app, 'app.settings')).toBe(true); // main-owned
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```
다른 테스트 갱신: `platform.spec.ts` — 앱 메뉴(macOS)에 `[null, '설정…']`가 `Learn 정보` 다음 묶음으로, Linux 파일 메뉴는 `[[null, '파일 가져오기…'], [null, '외부 파일 연결…'], ['-', ''], [null, '설정…'], ['-', ''], ['close', '창 닫기']]`, 보기 메뉴 끝 레지스트리 묶음 `['-', ''], [null, '글자 크게'], [null, '글자 크게'], [null, '글자 작게'], [null, '기본 글자 크기']`(두 번째 "글자 크게"는 숨은 ⌘= 짝 — `menuSnapshot`은 `visible`을 싣지 않는다); 레지스트리 반복문의 `isCommandAvailable` 상황에 `textSize: 13`. `palette.spec.ts` 첫 테스트의 목록 기대값을 `[/파일 가져오기…/, /외부 파일 연결…/, /사이드바 보기\/숨기기/, /단축키 도움말/, /설정…/, /글자 크게/, /글자 작게/]`로. `readers.spec.ts` 치명 화면 테스트는 그대로(main 소유 명령은 이미 제외).

- [ ] **Step 7: 시각 투어와 macOS 증거**

`tour.spec.ts`:
```ts
test('settings window, compact density, 15 pt text', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launchTour(testInfo.outputPath());
  try {
    await importPaths(s.page, [path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf'), path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample-h264-aac.mp4')]);
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      const settings = await expectWindow(s.app, 'settings', () => runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.settings'));
      await shoot(settings, `settings-${theme}`, { mask: [settings.getByTestId('library-path')] });
      await settings.close();
    }
    await setTheme(s.page, 'light');
    await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ density: 'compact' }));
    await shoot(s.page, 'density-compact-light', { mask: [s.page.locator('[data-col="added"]')] });
    await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ density: 'regular', fontSize: 15 }));
    await shoot(s.page, 'font-15-light', { mask: [s.page.locator('[data-col="added"]')] });
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```
(`settings.close()`는 Playwright `Page.close` — BrowserWindow를 닫는다.)
`shell.evidence.ts`에 macOS 설정 창 캡처(메인 창 위의 별도 창이므로 전체 화면 샷):
```ts
test('M3 chrome: ⌘, settings window', TAG, async () => {
  const settings = await expectWindow(s.app, 'settings', async () => {
    if (!IS_MAC) return runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.settings');
    try {
      await nativeKeystroke(s.app, ',', ['command']);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof NativeInputNotPermitted)) throw e;
      await runMenuCommand(s.app, 'app.settings');
    }
  });
  await s.page.waitForTimeout(600);
  await fullScreenShot('m3-11-settings', 'settings window over the inactive main window (grey lights, neutral selection)');
  await settings.close();
});
```

`R/shell/drop.ts`:
```ts
import { t } from '../strings';

export interface DropSummary {
  files: number;
  pdf: number;
  video: number;
  other: number;
}

/** DataTransferItem kinds/types during dragenter (Finder gives the MIME type from the extension). */
export function summarizeDrop(items: ReadonlyArray<{ kind: string; type: string }>): DropSummary {
  const s: DropSummary = { files: 0, pdf: 0, video: 0, other: 0 };
  for (const i of items) {
    if (i.kind !== 'file') continue;
    s.files++;
    if (i.type === 'application/pdf') s.pdf++;
    else if (i.type.startsWith('video/')) s.video++;
    else s.other++;
  }
  return s;
}

export function dropSummaryText(s: DropSummary): string {
  const known = s.pdf + s.video > 0;
  const parts = [s.pdf ? `${t.drop.pdf} ${s.pdf}` : '', s.video ? `${t.drop.video} ${s.video}` : '', known && s.other ? `${t.drop.other} ${s.other}` : ''].filter(Boolean);
  return parts.length ? `${t.drop.files(s.files)}(${parts.join(' · ')})` : t.drop.files(s.files);
}
```
`R/shell/DropOverlay.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Copy, Download, Link } from 'lucide-react';
import type { StorageMode } from '@learn/domain';
import { api } from '../api';
import { t } from '../strings';
import { Icon } from '../ui/Icon';
import { Kbd } from '../ui/Kbd';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';
import { dropSummaryText, summarizeDrop, type DropSummary } from './drop';

const hasFiles = (e: DragEvent) => e.dataTransfer?.types.includes('Files') ?? false;

/** Finder → window (design-spec 7.2): copy by default, link while ⌥ is held; unsupported files are reported after the drop. */
export function DropOverlay({ enabled, onDrop }: { enabled: boolean; onDrop(paths: string[], mode: StorageMode): void }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ link: boolean; summary: DropSummary } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) {
      setState(null);
      return;
    }
    let depth = 0; // dragenter/dragleave pairs per element: the overlay disappears only when the drag leaves the window
    const enter = (e: DragEvent) => {
      if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      depth++;
      const items = Array.from(e.dataTransfer?.items ?? []).map((i) => ({ kind: i.kind, type: i.type }));
      setState((s) => s ?? { link: e.altKey, summary: summarizeDrop(items) });
    };
    const over = (e: DragEvent) => {
      if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.dataTransfer!.dropEffect = e.altKey ? 'link' : 'copy';
      setState((s) => (s && s.link !== e.altKey ? { ...s, link: e.altKey } : s));
    };
    const leave = (e: DragEvent) => {
      if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
      depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
      if (depth === 0) setState(null);
    };
    const drop = (e: DragEvent) => {
      if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      depth = 0;
      setState(null);
      const paths = Array.from(e.dataTransfer!.files).map((f) => api().pathForFile(f)).filter((p) => p.length > 0);
      onDrop(paths, e.altKey ? 'linked' : 'managed');
    };
    addEventListener('dragenter', enter);
    addEventListener('dragover', over);
    addEventListener('dragleave', leave);
    addEventListener('drop', drop);
    return () => {
      removeEventListener('dragenter', enter);
      removeEventListener('dragover', over);
      removeEventListener('dragleave', leave);
      removeEventListener('drop', drop);
    };
  }, [enabled, onDrop]);

  if (!state) return null;
  const option = currentPlatform() === 'darwin' ? '⌥' : 'Alt';
  return (
    <div className="drop-overlay" role="dialog" aria-label={t.drop.label} data-testid="drop-overlay" data-mode={state.link ? 'link' : 'copy'}>
      <div className="frame" />
      <div className="drop-panel">
        <div className="glyph">
          <Icon icon={Download} size="xl" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="ui-balance">{t.drop.title}</h2>
        <div className="files" data-testid="drop-summary">
          {dropSummaryText(state.summary)}
        </div>
        <div className="drop-modes">
          <div className="drop-mode" data-on={state.link ? undefined : ''} data-testid="drop-copy">
            <Icon icon={Copy} />
            <span className="dm-title">{t.drop.copyTitle}</span>
            <span className="dm-text ui-prose">{t.drop.copyBody}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="drop-mode" data-on={state.link ? '' : undefined} data-testid="drop-link">
            <Icon icon={Link} />
            <span className="dm-title">
              {t.drop.linkTitle}
              <Kbd keys={[option]} />
            </span>
            <span className="dm-text ui-prose">{t.drop.linkBody}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
@layer screens {

  }

    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 20px minmax(0, 1fr);
    column-gap: var(--space-8);
    row-gap: 2px;
    padding: var(--space-12) var(--space-16);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    background: var(--bg-raised);
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--separator), var(--shadow-e1);
    text-align: left;
  }
  .drop-mode[data-on] {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--accent), var(--shadow-e1);
  }
  .drop-mode .icon {
    margin-top: 1px;

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: E2E** `tests/e2e/drop.spec.ts`

```ts
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { FIXTURES, importPaths, launch, openItem } from './helpers';

type Drag = 'dragenter' | 'dragover' | 'dragleave' | 'drop';
/** A synthetic Finder drag (types come from the files, like Chromium does for real drags). */
const drag = (page: Page, type: Drag, files: Array<[string, string]>, altKey = false) =>
  page.evaluate(({ type, files, altKey }) => {
    const dt = new DataTransfer();
    for (const [name, mime] of files) dt.items.add(new File(['x'], name, { type: mime }));
    document.body.dispatchEvent(new DragEvent(type, { dataTransfer: dt, altKey, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
  }, { type, files, altKey });

const THREE: Array<[string, string]> = [['a.pdf', 'application/pdf'], ['b.pdf', 'application/pdf'], ['c.mp4', 'video/mp4']];

test('dragging files in shows the window-wide overlay with counts; ⌥ switches to 외부 파일로 연결; leaving hides it', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await drag(page, 'dragenter', THREE);
    const overlay = page.getByTestId('drop-overlay');
    await expect(overlay).toHaveAttribute('data-mode', 'copy');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('drop-summary')).toHaveText('파일 3개(PDF 2 · 동영상 1)');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('drop-copy')).toHaveAttribute('data-on', '');
    expect(await overlay.boundingBox()).toMatchObject({ x: 0, y: 52 });
    // The sidebar top (traffic lights, toggle) stays above the overlay.
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.elementFromPoint(200, 26)?.closest('[data-testid="sidebar-top"]') !== null)).toBe(true);
    await drag(page, 'dragover', THREE, true);
    await expect(overlay).toHaveAttribute('data-mode', 'link');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('drop-link')).toHaveAttribute('data-on', '');
    await drag(page, 'dragover', THREE, false);
    await expect(overlay).toHaveAttribute('data-mode', 'copy');
    await drag(page, 'dragleave', THREE);
    await expect(overlay).toHaveCount(0);
    await drag(page, 'dragenter', [['notes.txt', 'text/plain']]);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('drop-summary')).toHaveText('파일 1개');
    await drag(page, 'drop', [['notes.txt', 'text/plain']]);
    await expect(overlay).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('drop-failed')).toBeVisible(); // a synthetic file has no Finder path
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('no overlay while reading', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  try {
    await importPaths(page, [path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf')]);
    await openItem(page, 'sample');
    await drag(page, 'dragenter', THREE);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('drop-overlay')).toHaveCount(0);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```

```ts
test('drop overlay: copy and ⌥ link', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const s = await launchTour(testInfo.outputPath());
  const files: Array<[string, string]> = [['a.pdf', 'application/pdf'], ['b.pdf', 'application/pdf'], ['c.mp4', 'video/mp4']];
  const fire = (type: 'dragenter' | 'dragover' | 'dragleave', altKey: boolean) =>
    s.page.evaluate(({ type, files, altKey }) => {
      const dt = new DataTransfer();
      for (const [n, m] of files) dt.items.add(new File(['x'], n, { type: m }));
      document.body.dispatchEvent(new DragEvent(type, { dataTransfer: dt, altKey, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    }, { type, files, altKey });
  try {
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      await setTheme(s.page, theme);
      await fire('dragenter', false);
      await shoot(s.page, `drop-${theme}`);
      if (theme === 'light') {
        await fire('dragover', true);
        await shoot(s.page, 'drop-link-light');
      }
      await fire('dragleave', false);
    }
  } finally {
    await s.app.close();
  }
});
```

### Task 12: 키보드 전용 여정·F6·단축키 키맵(R2)·axe·명령 전수 실행

품질 명세 "접근성(AC18)": RAC 시맨틱, `@axe-core/playwright`(개발 전용) 심각 이상 0건, 키보드만 쓰는 여정 E2E. 명세 8.3 "F6으로 패널 사이 이동". ADR-0005 §3(R2): 한 글자·기능 키 단축키는 렌더러 키맵이 `event.code`로 처리하고 IME 조합 중·편집 중·⌘/⌃/⌥ 조합은 무시한다 — **M3가 실제로 쓰는 키는 F6/⇧F6뿐**이므로 키맵은 그 둘로 배달하고, J/K/L(M8)·1–8(M5)은 그 키를 쓰는 마일스톤이 레지스트리에 `key`만 더하면 같은 경로를 탄다(키맵 테스트가 글자 키의 무시 조건을 미리 고정한다). 품질 명세 "가짜 컨트롤 금지"의 `commands.coverage` E2E로 가용한 명령을 모두 실행한다.

**axe 판정 규칙(이 작업이 확정):** `color-contrast` 규칙은 끈다 — 명세 3.1·8.3이 3차 글자(자리표시·개수·키캡)를 3:1로 승인했고 대비는 `tokens.test.ts`가 두 테마·고대비 모드에서 지키는데, axe는 모든 글자에 4.5:1을 요구하고 비브런시 면을 계산하지 못한다. `<video>`(`video-caption`)는 자막이 M8이므로 그때까지 제외한다. 나머지 규칙은 모두 켠 채 serious·critical 0건.

**Files:**
- Modify: `package.json`(루트 devDependencies), `pnpm-lock.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/domain/src/commands.ts`, `test/commands.test.ts`
- Create: `R/commands/keymap.ts`, `R/commands/keymap.test.ts`, `R/shell/panes.ts`; Modify: `R/commands/registry.ts`, `R/commands/registry.test.ts`, `R/commands/icons.ts`, `R/app/MainWindow.tsx`
- Create: `tests/e2e/a11y.ts`, `tests/e2e/keyboard.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/commands-coverage.spec.ts`; Modify: `tests/e2e/helpers.ts`, `tests/e2e/visual/tour.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (domain): 명령 `view.nextPane`(`'다음 패널로 이동'`, `key: { code: 'F6', inText: true }`, `['app']`, `palette: false`), `view.previousPane`(`'이전 패널로 이동'`, `key: { code: 'F6', shift: true, inText: true }`).
- Produces (renderer): `keyCommand(e: KeyLike, editing: boolean, commands?): CommandId | null`, `isEditing(target): boolean`, `useKeymap(env, active: boolean)`, `focusPane(direction: 1 | -1)`, `CommandEnv.focusPane(direction)`.
- Produces (tests): `expectNoSeriousA11y(page, label)`(`tests/e2e/a11y.ts`), `enabledMenuCommands(app): Promise<CommandId[]>`(`helpers.ts`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { CommandSpec } from '@learn/domain';
import { keyCommand, type KeyLike } from './keymap';

const C: CommandSpec[] = [
  { id: 'view.nextPane', label: '다음 패널로 이동', key: { code: 'F6', inText: true }, contexts: ['app'] },
  { id: 'view.previousPane', label: '이전 패널로 이동', key: { code: 'F6', shift: true, inText: true }, contexts: ['app'] },
  // A letter shortcut as M8 will add (J), to pin the guards now.
  { id: 'nav.back', label: '10초 뒤로', key: { code: 'KeyJ' }, contexts: ['viewer'] },
];
const k = (over: Partial<KeyLike>): KeyLike => ({ code: 'F6', key: 'F6', shiftKey: false, altKey: false, ctrlKey: false, metaKey: false, isComposing: false, keyCode: 117, ...over });

describe('renderer keymap (ADR-0005 §3)', () => {
  it('matches by physical key and shift', () => {
    expect(keyCommand(k({}), false, C)).toBe('view.nextPane');
    expect(keyCommand(k({ shiftKey: true }), false, C)).toBe('view.previousPane');
    expect(keyCommand(k({ code: 'KeyJ', key: 'ㅓ', keyCode: 74 }), false, C)).toBe('nav.back'); // Korean input source: key is ㅓ, code is KeyJ
  });
  it('ignores IME composition and modifier chords (those belong to the menu)', () => {
    expect(keyCommand(k({ isComposing: true }), false, C)).toBeNull();
    expect(keyCommand(k({ keyCode: 229 }), false, C)).toBeNull();
    expect(keyCommand(k({ metaKey: true }), false, C)).toBeNull();
    expect(keyCommand(k({ ctrlKey: true }), false, C)).toBeNull();
    expect(keyCommand(k({ altKey: true }), false, C)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('letters never fire while typing; keys marked inText (F6) do', () => {
    expect(keyCommand(k({ code: 'KeyJ', key: 'j', keyCode: 74 }), true, C)).toBeNull();
    expect(keyCommand(k({}), true, C)).toBe('view.nextPane');
  });
});
```
