`R/commands/keymap.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { COMMANDS, type CommandId, type CommandSpec } from '@learn/domain';
import { runCommand, type CommandEnv } from './registry';

export type KeyLike = Pick<KeyboardEvent, 'code' | 'key' | 'shiftKey' | 'altKey' | 'ctrlKey' | 'metaKey' | 'isComposing' | 'keyCode'>;

/**
 * Single-key and function-key shortcuts from the registry's `key` (ADR-0005 §3): compared by `event.code` so the
 * Korean input source does not matter; never during an IME composition, never with ⌘/⌃/⌥ (the menu owns those),
 * and letters never while a text field has focus (only keys marked `inText`, such as F6).
 */
export function keyCommand(e: KeyLike, editing: boolean, commands: readonly CommandSpec[] = COMMANDS): CommandId | null {
  if (e.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229) return null;
  if (e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return null;
  const hit = commands.find((c) => c.key !== undefined && c.key.code === e.code && Boolean(c.key.shift) === e.shiftKey);
  if (!hit) return null;
  if (editing && !hit.key!.inText) return null;
  return hit.id;
}

export function isEditing(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  if (target.isContentEditable) return true;
  if (target instanceof HTMLInputElement || target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement || target instanceof HTMLSelectElement) return true;
  const role = target.getAttribute('role');
  return role === 'textbox' || role === 'combobox' || role === 'searchbox';
}

/** Window-level keydown → registry. Off while a modal surface (palette) owns the keyboard. */
export function useKeymap(env: CommandEnv, active: boolean): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.defaultPrevented) return;
      const id = keyCommand(e, isEditing(e.target));
      if (id && runCommand(id, env)) e.preventDefault();
    };
    addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [env, active]);
}
```
`R/shell/panes.ts`:
```ts
const PANES = ['sidebar', 'content'] as const; // M4 adds the item panel after content (design-spec 8.3)
const FOCUSABLE = 'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), [tabindex="0"], [role="option"]';

/** F6 / ⇧F6: next / previous pane (sidebar → content); folded (inert) panes are skipped. */
export function focusPane(direction: 1 | -1): void {
  const panes = PANES.map((name) => document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-pane="${name}"]`)).filter(
    (el): el is HTMLElement => el !== null && el.closest('[inert]') === null && el.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0,
  );
  if (panes.length === 0) return;
  const at = panes.findIndex((p) => p.contains(document.activeElement));
  const next = panes[at === -1 ? (direction === 1 ? 0 : panes.length - 1) : (at + direction + panes.length) % panes.length]!;
  const target = next.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE);
  if (target) target.focus();
  else {
    next.tabIndex = -1;
    next.focus();
  }
}
```
`R/commands/registry.ts`: `CommandEnv`에 `/** F6 / ⇧F6 (Task 12). */ focusPane(direction: 1 | -1): void;`, 핸들러 `'view.nextPane': (env) => env.focusPane(1), 'view.previousPane': (env) => env.focusPane(-1),`. `registry.test.ts`의 `env()`에 `focusPane: vi.fn(),`. `icons.ts`에 `'view.nextPane': ArrowRightToLine, 'view.previousPane': ArrowLeftToLine`(lucide-react 1.60.0에 있음).
`R/app/MainWindow.tsx`: `env`에 `focusPane,`(import), 그리고 `useKeymap(env, !paletteOpen);`(`const paletteOpen = useShell((s) => s.paletteOpen);`).

Run: `pnpm vitest run apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/commands packages/domain` → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 4: E2E 도우미** — `tests/e2e/a11y.ts`:
```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

/**
 * axe (dev-only) on the current window: no serious or critical violations.
 * Legacy mode: Electron pages cannot open the blank page axe's cross-frame runner needs.
 * color-contrast is off (tokens.test.ts owns contrast; tertiary text is 3:1 by design-spec 3.1/8.3);
 * <video> is excluded until captions arrive in M8.
 */
export async function expectNoSeriousA11y(page: Page, label: string): Promise<void> {
  const result = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).setLegacyMode(true).disableRules(['color-contrast']).exclude('[data-testid="video"]').analyze();
  const serious = result.violations
    .filter((v) => v.impact === 'serious' || v.impact === 'critical')
    .map((v) => `${v.id} (${v.impact}): ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(' | ')}`);
  expect(serious, `axe @ ${label}`).toEqual([]);
}
```
`tests/e2e/helpers.ts`에:
```ts
/** Registry commands enabled in the application menu right now (hidden ⌘= twins excluded). */
export async function enabledMenuCommands(app: ElectronApplication): Promise<CommandId[]> {
  const flat = (nodes: MenuNode[]): MenuNode[] => nodes.flatMap((n) => [n, ...flat(n.submenu ?? [])]);
  const ids = new Set<string>(COMMANDS.map((c) => c.id));
  return flat(await menuSnapshot(app)).filter((n) => n.enabled && n.id !== null && ids.has(n.id)).map((n) => n.id as CommandId);
}
```
(`import { COMMANDS } from '../../packages/domain/src/commands';`)

- [ ] **Step 5: 키보드 전용 여정** `tests/e2e/keyboard.spec.ts`

```ts
// Keyboard only (AC18, design-spec 8.3). In-page keys are real key presses; ⌘ shortcuts go through the menu items
// (runMenuCommand) because CDP keys never reach native accelerators on Linux — the macOS lane presses them for real.
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { expectNoSeriousA11y } from './a11y';
import { FIXTURES, expectWindow, launch, runMenuCommand, stubOpenDialog } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');
const MP4 = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample-h264-aac.mp4');

test('import → open → page → back → palette → settings → shortcuts, with axe at every stop', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  const focusedTestId = () => page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement?.closest('[data-testid]')?.getAttribute('data-testid') ?? null);
  try {
    await expectNoSeriousA11y(page, 'empty library');
    await stubOpenDialog(app, [PDF, MP4]);
    await page.keyboard.press('F6'); // sidebar
    expect(await focusedTestId()).toBe('nav-library');
    await page.keyboard.press('F6'); // content: first control of the first-run screen
    expect(await focusedTestId()).toBe('import-managed');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(2);
    await expectNoSeriousA11y(page, 'library list');

    await page.keyboard.press('F6');
    await page.keyboard.press('F6');
    expect(await focusedTestId()).toBe('open-item');
    await expect(page.locator('.table-row:has(.row-open:focus-visible)')).toHaveCount(1); // keyboard cursor ring
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('pdf-canvas')).toHaveAttribute('data-rendered', /^[1-9]\d*$/);
    await expectNoSeriousA11y(page, 'PDF reader');

    await page.keyboard.press('F6'); // sidebar
    await page.keyboard.press('F6'); // content: 다음 쪽 (이전 쪽 is disabled on page 1)
    expect(await focusedTestId()).toBe('pdf-next');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('pdf-page-indicator')).toHaveText('2 / 3쪽');
    await page.keyboard.press('Shift+Tab'); // 이전 쪽
    await page.keyboard.press('Shift+Tab'); // toolbar: 라이브러리로 돌아가기
    expect(await focusedTestId()).toBe('viewer-back');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('library-table')).toBeVisible();

    await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette'); // ⌘K
    await page.keyboard.type('ㅅㅇㄷㅂ');
    await expectNoSeriousA11y(page, 'palette');
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('shell')).toHaveAttribute('data-sidebar', 'hidden');
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.toggleSidebar'); // ⌃⌘S

    const settings = await expectWindow(app, 'settings', async () => {
      await runMenuCommand(app, 'app.palette');
      await page.keyboard.type('설정');
      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    });
    await expectNoSeriousA11y(settings, 'settings window');
    await settings.keyboard.press('Tab'); // the theme radio group
    await settings.keyboard.press('ArrowRight'); // 시스템 설정 → 라이트
    await expect.poll(() => app.evaluate(({ nativeTheme }) => nativeTheme.themeSource)).toBe('light');
    await settings.close();

    const sheet = await expectWindow(app, 'sheet', () => runMenuCommand(app, 'app.shortcuts')); // ⌘/
    await expectNoSeriousA11y(sheet, 'shortcut sheet');
    await sheet.keyboard.press('Enter');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});

test('F6 / ⇧F6 cycle sidebar ↔ content, also out of a text field; a folded sidebar is skipped', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  const inPane = () => page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement?.closest('[data-pane]')?.getAttribute('data-pane') ?? null);
  try {
    await page.keyboard.press('F6');
    expect(await inPane()).toBe('sidebar');
    await page.keyboard.press('F6');
    expect(await inPane()).toBe('content');
    await page.keyboard.press('Shift+F6');
    expect(await inPane()).toBe('sidebar');
    await runMenuCommand(app, 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await page.keyboard.press('F6');
    expect(await inPane()).toBe('content');
    await page.keyboard.press('F6');
    expect(await inPane()).toBe('content');
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 명령 전수 실행** `tests/e2e/commands-coverage.spec.ts`

```ts
// No fake controls (quality spec): every registry command that is enabled in the library and in the reader runs
// through its real menu item without a page error; the item context-menu commands run through the native menu path.
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test, type ElectronApplication, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { FIXTURES, enabledMenuCommands, importPaths, launch, openItem, runMenuCommand, stubContextMenu, stubOpenDialog } from './helpers';

const PDF = path.join(FIXTURES, 'sample.pdf');

async function closeTransient(app: ElectronApplication, main: Page): Promise<void> {
  await main.waitForTimeout(250);
  for (const w of app.windows()) {
    if (w === main) continue;
    if (new URL(w.url()).searchParams.get('window') === 'sheet') await w.keyboard.press('Escape').catch(() => {});
    else await w.close().catch(() => {});
  }
  if (await main.getByTestId('palette').count()) await main.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await main.waitForTimeout(150);
}

test('every enabled registry command runs without a page error (library, reader, item menu)', async ({}, testInfo) => {
  const { app, page } = await launch(testInfo.outputPath('library'), testInfo.outputPath('userdata'));
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const watch = (p: Page) => {
    p.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(`${p.url()}: ${e.message}`));
    p.on('console', (m) => {
      if (m.type() === 'error') errors.push(`${p.url()}: ${m.text()}`);
    });
  };
  watch(page);
  app.on('window', watch);
  try {
    await importPaths(page, [PDF]);
    await stubOpenDialog(app, []); // import dialogs come back empty
    await app.evaluate(({ dialog, shell }) => {
      dialog.showMessageBox = (async () => ({ response: 1, checkboxChecked: false })) as typeof dialog.showMessageBox;
      shell.showItemInFolder = () => {};
      shell.openPath = async () => '';
    });
    for (const view of ['library', 'reader'] as const) {
      if (view === 'reader') await openItem(page, 'sample');
      const ids = (await enabledMenuCommands(app)).sort((a, b) => Number(a === 'nav.back') - Number(b === 'nav.back')); // leave the reader last
      expect(ids.length).toBeGreaterThan(3);
      for (const id of ids) {
        await runMenuCommand(app, id);
        await closeTransient(app, page);
      }
    }
    for (const label of ['열기', process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'Finder에서 보기' : '폴더에서 보기', '기본 앱으로 열기']) {
      await stubContextMenu(app, label);
      await page.getByTestId('item-row').first().click({ button: 'right' });
      await closeTransient(app, page);
      if (label === '열기') await runMenuCommand(app, 'nav.back');
    }
    expect(errors).toEqual([]);
  } finally {
    await app.close();
  }
});
```

### Task 13: 데모 오버레이·macOS 데모 영상·냉시작 측정

M0가 만든 증거 레인의 `demo` suite(워크플로 입력·`evidence.sh`·녹화 artifact `demo-<label>-<run>`·`startRecording`)에 실제 데모 테스트를 처음 넣는다. 합성 입력(Playwright 마우스·CDP 키)은 화면 녹화에 커서·키가 보이지 않으므로 개발 빌드 전용 오버레이(`LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY=1`, M0 설계 4항)가 포인터 고리와 단축키 알약을 그린다. ⌘ 단축키는 macOS에서 `nativeKeystroke`(실제 OS 키)로 누른다. 냉시작(프로세스 시작 → `app-ready`)과 렌더러 번들 크기(ADR-0004 "M3에서 기록")를 `@perf`로 잰다. Task 14의 `all` 실행이 늘어난 증거를 다 돌 수 있게 시간 제한을 늘린다.

**Files:**
- Create: `M/perf.ts`; Modify: `M/index.ts`, `M/window.ts`
- Create: `R/shell/DemoOverlay.tsx`, `R/styles/screens/demo.css`; Modify: `R/app/MainWindow.tsx`, `R/styles/index.ts`
- Create: `tests/evidence/demo.evidence.ts`(`@demo`), `tests/evidence/perf.evidence.ts`(`@perf`)
- Modify: `playwright.evidence.config.ts`, `.github/workflows/macos-evidence.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (main): `globalThis.__learnPerf = { processStart: number; ready: number; windowShown: number }`(epoch ms, `M/perf.ts`의 `markPerf('ready' | 'windowShown')`); 메인 창 URL `demo=1`(`LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY=1`이고 비패키지일 때만).
- Produces (renderer): `DemoOverlay()`(`readWindowParams(location.search).demo`일 때만 그림); `app-ready`의 `data-at`(Task 4)을 측정에 쓴다.
- Produces (evidence): `evidence-results/<label>/m3-demo.mov` + `m3-demo.chapters.json` + `m3-demo.chapters.txt`(장 목록), `evidence-results/perf.json`.

- [ ] **Step 1: 측정 표시(main)**

`M/perf.ts`:
```ts
/** Cold-start marks read by tests/evidence/perf.evidence.ts through app.evaluate (epoch ms). */
export const perf = { processStart: Math.round(performance.timeOrigin), ready: 0, windowShown: 0 };
(globalThis as { __learnPerf?: typeof perf }).__learnPerf = perf;

export function markPerf(name: 'ready' | 'windowShown'): void {
  if (perf[name] === 0) perf[name] = Date.now();
}
```
`M/index.ts` 맨 위 import 바로 다음에 `import './perf';`(가장 먼저 평가), `whenReady` 콜백 첫 줄에 `markPerf('ready');`. `M/window.ts`의 `win.once('ready-to-show', () => win.show());`를 `win.once('ready-to-show', () => { markPerf('windowShown'); win.show(); });`로. 데모 플래그: `urlOptions`의 `demo: !app.isPackaged && process.env['LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY'] === '1'`.

- [ ] **Step 2: 데모 오버레이(렌더러)**

`R/shell/DemoOverlay.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { acceleratorKeys, commandById } from '@learn/domain';
import { useLearnEvent } from '../events';
import { Kbd } from '../ui/Kbd';
import { currentPlatform } from '../ui/platform';

const KEY_NAMES: Readonly<Record<string, string[]>> = { Tab: ['⇥'], Enter: ['↩'], Escape: ['esc'], ArrowUp: ['↑'], ArrowDown: ['↓'], ArrowLeft: ['←'], ArrowRight: ['→'], F6: ['F6'] };

/** Demo recordings only (LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY=1, unpackaged): pointer ring and a pill for each shortcut. */
export function DemoOverlay() {
  const [pill, setPill] = useState<{ keys: string[]; label: string; at: number } | null>(null);
  const [pointer, setPointer] = useState<{ x: number; y: number; down: boolean } | null>(null);
  useLearnEvent('menuCommand', ({ commandId }) => {
    const c = commandById(commandId);
    if (c.accelerator) setPill({ keys: acceleratorKeys(c.accelerator, currentPlatform()), label: c.label, at: Date.now() });
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    const key = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const k = KEY_NAMES[e.key];
      if (k) setPill({ keys: e.shiftKey ? ['⇧', ...k] : k, label: '', at: Date.now() });
    };
    const move = (e: PointerEvent) => setPointer({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, down: e.buttons > 0 });
    addEventListener('keydown', key, true);
    for (const type of ['pointermove', 'pointerdown', 'pointerup'] as const) addEventListener(type, move, true);
    return () => {
      removeEventListener('keydown', key, true);
      for (const type of ['pointermove', 'pointerdown', 'pointerup'] as const) removeEventListener(type, move, true);
    };
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pill) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setPill(null), 1400);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [pill]);
  return (
    <div className="demo-overlay" aria-hidden="true">
      {pointer && <span className="demo-pointer" data-down={pointer.down ? '' : undefined} style={{ left: pointer.x, top: pointer.y }} />}
      {pill && (
        <div className="demo-pill" key={pill.at}>
          <Kbd keys={pill.keys} />
          {pill.label && <span>{pill.label}</span>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```
`R/app/MainWindow.tsx`의 `overlays`에 `{readWindowParams(location.search).demo && <DemoOverlay />}`.
`R/styles/screens/demo.css`(+ `index.ts`):
```css
@layer screens {
  .demo-overlay {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 100;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  .demo-pointer {
    position: absolute;
    width: 22px; /* local: demo only */
    height: 22px;
    margin: -11px 0 0 -11px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--focus), 0 0 0 4px color-mix(in oklch, var(--focus) 30%, transparent);
  }
  .demo-pointer[data-down] {
    background: color-mix(in oklch, var(--focus) 35%, transparent);
  }
  .demo-pill {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    bottom: var(--space-40);
    translate: -50% 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-12);
    padding: var(--space-12) var(--space-20);
    border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
    background: var(--bg-raised);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-e2);
    font: var(--type-title3);
    animation: rise-in var(--duration-base) var(--ease-standard);
  }
  .demo-pill .kbd {
    min-width: 28px;
    height: 28px;
    font: var(--type-title3);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 냉시작 측정** `tests/evidence/perf.evidence.ts`

```ts
// @perf — cold start (process start → app-ready) and renderer bundle size. Budget 1.5 s on the reference Mac
// (quality spec); runner values are reported only. Linux CI fails only beyond 2× the budget.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { ROOT, launch } from '../e2e/helpers';
import { writeJson } from './capture';

const median = (xs: number[]) => [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b)[Math.floor(xs.length / 2)]!;

test('cold start to app-ready, 5 launches, and the renderer bundle', { tag: '@perf' }, async () => {
  test.setTimeout(180_000);
  const runs: Array<Record<string, number>> = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'learn-perf-'));
    const t0 = Date.now();
    const s = await launch(path.join(tmp, 'library'), path.join(tmp, 'userdata'));
    const wall = Date.now() - t0;
    const appReadyAt = Number(await s.page.getByTestId('app-ready').getAttribute('data-at'));
    const main = await s.app.evaluate(() => (globalThis as { __learnPerf?: { processStart: number; ready: number; windowShown: number } }).__learnPerf!);
    runs.push({ wallIncludingPlaywright: wall, mainReady: main.ready - main.processStart, windowShown: main.windowShown - main.processStart, appReady: appReadyAt - main.processStart });
    await s.app.close();
    fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
  const assets = path.join(ROOT, 'apps/desktop/out/renderer/assets');
  const files = fs.readdirSync(assets).map((f) => ({ f, bytes: fs.statSync(path.join(assets, f)).size }));
  const sum = (re: RegExp) => files.filter((x) => re.test(x.f)).reduce((n, x) => n + x.bytes, 0);
  const result = {
    runs,
    medianMs: { appReady: median(runs.map((r) => r.appReady!)), windowShown: median(runs.map((r) => r.windowShown!)), mainReady: median(runs.map((r) => r.mainReady!)) },
    budgetMs: { coldStartToAppReady: 1500 },
    bundleBytes: { js: sum(/\.js$/), css: sum(/\.css$/), fonts: sum(/\.(woff2?|ttf|otf)$/) },
    platform: `${process.platform}-${process.arch}`,
  };
  writeJson('perf.json', result, 'M3 cold start (process start → app-ready) and renderer bundle size');
  console.log('[measure] perf', JSON.stringify(result.medianMs), JSON.stringify(result.bundleBytes));
  if (process.platform === 'linux' && process.env['CI']) expect(result.medianMs.appReady).toBeLessThan(3000);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 데모 영상** `tests/evidence/demo.evidence.ts`

```ts
// @demo — a 2–4 minute macOS screen recording of the M3 shell with a chapter list. Real ⌘ keys through System
// Events, the demo overlay for pointer and keys. Elsewhere (Linux CI) the same script runs at 1/10 pace, unrecorded.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { CommandId } from '../../packages/domain/src/commands';
import { FIXTURES, NativeInputNotPermitted, expectWindow, launch, nativeKeystroke, openItem, runMenuCommand, stubOpenDialog, type KeyModifier, type Session } from '../e2e/helpers';
import { IS_MAC, outFile, record, startRecording } from './capture';

const PACE = IS_MAC ? 1 : 0.1;
const RECORD_SECONDS = 200;
const pause = (ms: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms * PACE));
const FILES = ['sample.pdf', '한글 경로 (테스트) #1.pdf', 'sample-h264-aac.mp4', 'sample-mpeg4part2.mp4'].map((f) => path.join(FIXTURES, f));

test('M3 demo recording with chapters', { tag: '@demo' }, async () => {
  test.setTimeout(420_000);
  const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'learn-demo-'));
  const s: Session = await launch(path.join(tmp, 'library'), path.join(tmp, 'userdata'), { env: { LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY: '1' } });
  const started = Date.now();
  const chapters: Array<{ at: number; title: string }> = [];
  const chapter = (title: string) => chapters.push({ at: Math.round((Date.now() - started) / 1000), title });
  const press = async (key: string, modifiers: KeyModifier[], fallback: CommandId) => {
    if (IS_MAC) {
      try {
        await nativeKeystroke(s.app, key, modifiers);
        return;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof NativeInputNotPermitted)) throw e;
      }
    }
    await runMenuCommand(s.app, fallback);
  };
  const drag = (type: 'dragenter' | 'dragover' | 'dragleave', altKey: boolean) =>
    s.page.evaluate(({ type, altKey }) => {
      const dt = new DataTransfer();
      for (const [n, m] of [['ch06.pdf', 'application/pdf'], ['paper.pdf', 'application/pdf'], ['lecture.mp4', 'video/mp4']]) dt.items.add(new File(['x'], n!, { type: m }));
      document.body.dispatchEvent(new DragEvent(type, { dataTransfer: dt, altKey, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    }, { type, altKey });
  const rec = startRecording(RECORD_SECONDS, 'm3-demo', 'M3 shell demo (2–4 min)');
  try {
    await s.page.mouse.move(700, 450);
    chapter('Learn M3 — 레이아웃 B 셸과 첫 실행 화면');
    await pause(9000);

    chapter('창 전체 드롭: 사본으로 가져오기 / ⌥ 외부 파일로 연결');
    await drag('dragenter', false);
    await pause(5000);
    await drag('dragover', true);
    await pause(5000);
    await drag('dragover', false);
    await pause(2000);
    await drag('dragleave', false);
    await pause(2000);

    chapter('파일 가져오기 ⌘O와 결과 알림');
    await stubOpenDialog(s.app, FILES);
    await press('o', ['command'], 'file.importManaged');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('item-row')).toHaveCount(4);
    await pause(8000);

    chapter('명령 팔레트 ⌘K — 초성 검색');
    await press('k', ['command'], 'app.palette');
    await pause(1500);
    for (const ch of ['ㅅ', 'ㅇ', 'ㄷ', 'ㅂ']) {
      await s.page.keyboard.insertText(ch);
      await pause(900);
    }
    await pause(3000);
    await s.page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await pause(1500);

    chapter('PDF 읽기와 라이브러리로 돌아가기 ⌘1');
    await openItem(s.page, 'sample');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('pdf-canvas')).toHaveAttribute('data-rendered', /^[1-9]\d*$/);
    await pause(4000);
    await s.page.getByTestId('pdf-next').click();
    await pause(4000);
    await press('1', ['command'], 'nav.back');
    await pause(3000);

    chapter('재생할 수 없는 동영상 안내 — Finder에서 보기 · 기본 앱으로 열기');
    await openItem(s.page, 'sample-mpeg4part2');
    await expect(s.page.getByTestId('video-error')).toBeVisible();
    await pause(7000);
    await press('1', ['command'], 'nav.back');
    await pause(2000);

    chapter('사이드바 접기 ⌃⌘S와 폭 조절');
    await press('s', ['control', 'command'], 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await pause(4000);
    await press('s', ['control', 'command'], 'view.toggleSidebar');
    await pause(2000);
    await s.page.mouse.move(240, 420, { steps: 10 });
    await s.page.mouse.down();
    await s.page.mouse.move(320, 420, { steps: 30 });
    await s.page.mouse.up();
    await pause(2500);
    await s.page.mouse.move(320, 420);
    await s.page.mouse.down();
    await s.page.mouse.move(240, 420, { steps: 30 });
    await s.page.mouse.up();
    await pause(2000);

    chapter('설정 ⌘, — 다크 테마, 밀도, 글자 크기');
    const settings = await expectWindow(s.app, 'settings', () => press(',', ['command'], 'app.settings'));
    await pause(2500);
    for (const id of ['theme-tile-dark', 'density-compact', 'font-size-14', 'font-size-13', 'density-regular', 'theme-tile-system']) {
      await settings.getByTestId(id).click();
      await pause(3000);
    }
    await settings.close();
    await pause(2000);

    chapter('단축키 도움말 ⌘/');
    const sheet = await expectWindow(s.app, 'sheet', () => press('/', ['command'], 'app.shortcuts'));
    await pause(8000);
    await sheet.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await pause(2000);

    chapter('키보드만으로: F6 패널 이동, ↩ 열기');
    await s.page.keyboard.press('F6');
    await pause(2000);
    await s.page.keyboard.press('F6');
    await pause(2000);
    await s.page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await pause(4000);
    await press('1', ['command'], 'nav.back');
    await pause(2000);

    chapter('다크 모드');
    await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ theme: 'dark' }));
    await pause(7000);
    await s.page.evaluate(() => window.learn.prefs.set({ theme: 'system' }));
    const elapsed = (Date.now() - started) / 1000;
    if (IS_MAC && elapsed < RECORD_SECONDS) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, (RECORD_SECONDS - elapsed) * 1000 + 1000)); // let screencapture finish
  } finally {
    const entry = await rec.done;
    const txt = outFile('m3-demo.chapters.txt');
    const json = outFile('m3-demo.chapters.json');
    const mmss = (sec: number) => `${Math.floor(sec / 60)}:${String(sec % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    fs.writeFileSync(txt.abs, chapters.map((c) => `${mmss(c.at)} ${c.title}`).join('\n') + '\n');
    fs.writeFileSync(json.abs, JSON.stringify({ recording: entry.file, seconds: RECORD_SECONDS, chapters }, null, 2));
    record({ file: txt.rel, kind: 'data', description: 'M3 demo chapter list' });
    await s.app.close();
    fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});
```
(macOS에서 장 합계가 약 3분 10초가 되도록 `pause`를 맞췄다. `startRecording`의 `.mov`는 M0의 별도 artifact `demo-<label>-<run>`으로 올라간다(`.github/workflows/macos-evidence.yml`의 "Upload recordings").)

- [ ] **Step 5: 증거 시간 제한** — 늘어난 `all`(baseline + spikes + mockups + shell + perf + demo ≈ 11–12분, 기능 E2E ≈ 4분)을 감당하도록:
  - `playwright.evidence.config.ts`: `globalTimeout: 900_000`(주석 "Whole-run cap below the workflow step limit (17 min)").
  - `.github/workflows/macos-evidence.yml`: mac 잡 `timeout-minutes: 35`, "Evidence suite" 단계 `timeout-minutes: 17`(주석의 "step limit 12"·"10 min"도 17·15로).
  - 이 변경은 `m3-shell` 브랜치의 워크플로 파일로 실행된다(`gh workflow run --ref m3-shell`은 그 ref의 파일을 쓴다).

Task 8에서 보정한 신호등·비브런시 결과가 이번 PNG에서 맞는지 판정한다.

- [ ] **Step 5: 데모 영상 확인** — `evidence/m3/<run>/demo/**/m3-demo.mov`(2–4분)와 `m3-demo.chapters.txt`. `ffprobe -v error -show_entries format=duration -of csv=p=0 <mov>`로 길이를 확인하고, 장마다 프레임 하나를 뽑아(`ffmpeg -ss <초> -i <mov> -frames:v 1 evidence/m3/<run>/demo/ch-<n>.png`) 장 제목과 화면이 맞는지 본다.

- [ ] **Step 6: 문서**
  - `docs/mac-verification.md`: "M3 셸" 절 — 실행 표(run id·suite·분·결과), `m3-shell.json` 값(신호등 중심, 비브런시 차이, 실제 가속기, 창 상태 복원, SF 글꼴), `perf.json`(macOS·Linux 냉시작 중앙값, 번들 크기 — 냉시작 예산 1.5초는 기준 장비에서 판정), 데모 영상 위치와 장 목록, macOS 분 누계.
  - `docs/acceptance.md`: AC02(중복 확인 = 네이티브 시트, `sheets.spec.ts`), AC18(키보드 전용 여정·F6·axe 0건·200% 확대 자동 접힘 — `keyboard.spec.ts`, `shell.spec.ts`; VoiceOver는 실기), Mac 증거 열에 M3 run.
  - `docs/decisions/0004-ui-stack.md`: 상태 "**채택**(2026-10-21, M3)", 표의 버전을 정확한 고정값(1.22.1 / 1.60.0 / 4.14.8 / 5.104.5 / 5.0.15 / @axe-core/playwright 4.14.0)으로, "결과와 위험"에 측정한 렌더러 번들 크기(`perf.json`)와 RAC Toast가 `UNSTABLE_` API라 `ui/toasts.tsx` 한 곳에서만 쓴다는 사실, axe의 `color-contrast` 끈 이유.
  - `docs/next-session.md`: M3에서 해결한 것(키맵·명령 상태·설정 창·시트)과 M4 입력(항목 패널 자동 접힘 순서 `CONTENT_MIN` 520, 표가 같은 `.table-*` 치수 재사용, 팔레트 자료 결과·최근 실행 가중치, ⇧⌘R은 선택이 생기면 `item.showInFolder`에 붙인다, 컨텍스트 메뉴 종류 `collection|tag|column` 준비됨).
  - `README.md`: 새 환경 변수(`LEARN_DETERMINISTIC`, `LEARN_VIBRANCY`, `LEARN_DEMO_OVERLAY`, `LEARN_TOUR_2X`)와 투어 기준 PNG 갱신 명령.

**자리표시 검사:** "TBD/TODO/나중에" 없음. 코드가 필요한 단계에는 코드가 있다. M2(b5cefdd)에 기대는 곳은 "시작 전"의 grep으로 확인한다. 뷰어는 M2의 위치 기억 훅을 그대로 두고 JSX만 바꾼다(Task 7). macOS 실행 결과에 따라 갈리는 곳(창 상태 기억, 비브런시, 신호등 보정)은 판정 기준과 대체 코드를 함께 적었다(Task 8 Step 9–10).

**이름 일관성:** `isCommandAvailable`/`availableMenuCommands`/`CommandContextState`(2) ↔ `contextOf`/`useReportContext`/`runPicked`(2, 4) ↔ `ui:reportContext`(2, 4 FatalScreen, 10 `textSize`); `useShell`의 `view`·`sidebar`·`importing`(4)·`paletteOpen`(9); `Toolbar({ title, count, titleTestId, leading, actions, trailing })`(4, 7, 9); `expectWindow(app, 'sheet' | 'settings', trigger)`(6, 7, 10, 12, 13); `contentHeight`/`markSized`(6, 10); testid `viewer-title`·`viewer-back`(M2 → 7), `toolbar-title`(4), `launcher`(9).
